[1] CORAL-KV: Compact Cross-Layer Prediction and Residual Coding for Autoregressive Video KV Caches cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Albert Ge, Akash Haridas, Utkarsh Saxena, Ruijing Yang, Ian Colbert

TL;DR: 提出 CORAL-KV，以跨层预测和残差量化压缩自回归视频扩散增长的 KV 缓存，并提供跨视频共享或当前视频自适应拟合两种保真—内存设置。

Details

Motivation: 长视频生成中 KV 缓存持续增长，是显存瓶颈。聚类量化虽能压缩相似向量，但重建误差会在后续帧注意历史时累积；视频模型缓存层之间还存在未利用的依赖。

Result: CORAL-KV 用每层缓存状态预测后续层的 key/value，再量化预测残差。块对角加低秩（block-DPLR）预测器由头内通道关系建模的块对角部分和跨头依赖的全局低秩项组成。可复用预测器跨视频共享，也可对当前视频重拟合轻量块对角预测器以提升保真。HY-WorldPlay、MAGI-1 上，可复用配置较 QVG-4 的 PSNR 分别高 0.32、4.95 dB，缓存分别少 48%、37%；相较 QVG-Pro，视频自适应配置在 MAGI-1 上 PSNR 高 3.77 dB，缓存少 33.7%。长程生成保真更高、时间断裂减少，生成延迟相近。

Insight: 跨层相关性可用于预测缓存并只编码残差，减少逐层独立量化误差；共享与视频自适应配置对应不同存储和保真权衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models maintain a growing attention key-value (KV) cache, making memory a key bottleneck for long video generation. Recently, clustering-based quantization has emerged as an effective method to compress video KV caches by grouping similar key and value vectors then quantizing residual errors. However, when later frames attend to the compressed history, small reconstruction errors can compound as the video grows. Beyond the within-layer similarity exploited by clustering, we find strong dependencies across cache layers in video models. This motivates Coral-KV - Calibrated Offline, Residual-coded, and Adapted Locally - a KV-cache compression framework that uses cache states in each layer to predict keys and values in subsequent layers, then quantizes the remaining prediction residuals. We construct compact block-diagonal-plus-low-rank (block-DPLR) predictors: block-diagonal components model channel relationships within each attention head, while a global low-rank term models dependencies between heads. Coral-KV exposes a flexible fidelity-memory trade-off: it can reuse its predictors across videos or refit lightweight block-diagonal predictors to the current video for higher fidelity. Across extensive evaluations on both HY-WorldPlay and MAGI-1, our reusable configuration improves PSNR over QVG-4 by 0.32 dB and 4.95 dB, respectively, while using 48% and 37% less cache memory. Against QVG-Pro, our video-adapted configuration improves MAGI-1 PSNR by 3.77 dB while using 33.7% less cache memory. Coral-KV also maintains higher reconstruction fidelity over extended generation and reduces temporal discontinuities while retaining comparable generation latency.

[2] MIRAGE: Metric Inference and Reliability-Aware Gaussian Estimation from Feed-Forward Vision cs.CVPDF

Xinzhe Wang, Changjian Jiang, Kaiwen Song, Xudong Li, Kerui Ren

TL;DR: 提出 MIRAGE，将前馈视觉 3D 几何锚定到 LiDAR-IMU 度量运动，并按对应支持度与跨模态一致性调节在线位姿校正，兼顾准确尺度和高保真 Gaussian 重建。

Details

Motivation: 前馈 3D 先验可从图像流高质量重建，但度量尺度不可靠、长程轨迹漂移；LiDAR-惯性运动有准确物理尺度，却缺少高保真重建所需的图像空间一致性。

Result: MIRAGE 融合前馈视觉几何和 LiDAR-IMU 度量运动，并依据匹配支持度及模态间一致性调节在线位姿修正，以联合估计度量位姿和高质量高斯重建。在 Oxford Spires、M2DGR、INS 上实现准确度量轨迹估计，超过常用于视觉基准但计算昂贵的 COLMAP。作者还构建多模态采集平台，为长时程大规模室内重建建立视觉专用新基准的高精度 6DoF 参考轨迹，并评测近期单目方法；摘要未列具体误差数值。

Insight: 几何重建与度量运动各有优势，按视觉对应和跨模态一致性加权融合可补足彼此短板；工作也贡献长时室内重建评测数据。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D priors enable high-quality reconstruction from image streams but often lack reliable metric scale and suffer from long-term trajectory drift. In contrast, LiDAR–inertial estimation provides metrically accurate motion but lacks the image-space consistency needed for high-fidelity reconstruction. We propose $MIRAGE$, which anchors feed-forward visual geometry to LiDAR–IMU metric motion and regulates online pose corrections according to correspondence support and cross-modal agreement, jointly enabling accurate metric pose estimation and high-fidelity Gaussian reconstruction. Experiments on Oxford Spires, M2DGR and INS show that MIRAGE achieves accurate metric trajectory estimation, outperforming COLMAP, a widely used but computationally expensive reference for vision-only benchmarks. Motivated by the scarcity of long-horizon monocular benchmarks for large-scale indoor reconstruction, we further build a multimodal data-collection platform and use MIRAGE to establish accurate 6-DoF reference trajectories for a new vision-only benchmark. We benchmark recent monocular large-scale reconstruction methods on this dataset, providing a systematic evaluation of their performance in long-horizon indoor scenes.

[3] TerraAgent: Demonstration-Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Long-Context Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering cs.CVPDF

Jingwen Huang, Yiming Fang, Hanxiao Wu, Boyuan He, Keyi Luo

TL;DR: 提出 AVA，一个免训练遥感 VQA 框架，以 GSD 分桶示范检索、证据类型子问题图和反事实视觉依赖审计降低先验作答。

Details

Motivation: 遥感问答正确答案未必来自图像证据；跨地面采样距离、传感器和城市纹理检索到的示范可能不匹配，长视觉上下文会稀释证据、误差可在多步推理传播，先验知识作答又被标准准确率掩盖。

Result: GDR 按 GSD 分桶、依据场景图检索示范，并剔除答案缺乏图像支持的示范；MACE 将查询拆为证据类型子问题 DAG，路由至感知工具，并在共享账本记录验证来源、限制重复感知；CVA 通过对引用证据做消融/交换反事实测试最终答案，计算视觉依赖分数并用于置信度校准和强制证据重查。在 RSVQA-LR/HR、VRSBench、LHRS-Bench 上，较同设置最强基线平均准确率最高提高 2.40 分，视觉依赖从 0.28 升至 0.49，先验作答率从 46.2% 降至 21.7%，幻觉率从 17.1% 降至 9.8%；成本为单次骨干前向的 2.6 倍，不需微调。

Insight: 通过反事实改变引用证据检验答案是否真由图像驱动，可量化视觉依赖并区分“答对”与“看图答对”。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual question answering (RS-VQA) requires answers that truly depend on image evidence, yet current remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS MLLMs) are still evaluated mainly by accuracy, which cannot reveal whether a correct answer was produced without using the image. This setting introduces three challenges: demonstrations retrieved across different ground sample distances (GSD), sensors, and city textures may be poorly matched; real queries often involve long visual contexts from multi-scale tiles, multi-temporal frames, and retrieved knowledge, where evidence can be diluted and errors can propagate through multi-step reasoning; and prior-only answering remains invisible to standard metrics. We present AVA, a training-free framework that wraps a frozen RS MLLM with three modules. GDR performs GSD-bucketed, scene-graph-based demonstration retrieval and filters demonstrations whose answers are unsupported by their images. MACE decomposes each query into an evidence-typed sub-question DAG, routes sub-questions to suitable perception tools, and records verified provenance in a shared evidence ledger with bounded re-perception. CVA audits the final answer through counterfactual perturbations of cited evidence, including ablation and swap, and computes a visual-dependency score for confidence calibration and evidence-forcing re-query. On RSVQA-LR/HR, VRSBench, and LHRS-Bench, AVA improves average accuracy by up to 2.40 points over the strongest same-setting baselines, raises the visual-dependency score from 0.28 to 0.49, reduces the prior-only answer rate from 46.2 percent to 21.7 percent, and lowers the hallucination rate from 17.1 percent to 9.8 percent, with 2.6x the cost of a single backbone forward pass and no fine-tuning.

[4] TraceICL: Thread-Resolved Demonstration Curation with Consistency Calibration for Robust Multimodal In-Context Learning cs.CVPDF

Yigit Yilmaz, Elena Petrova, Mehmet Kaya, Lucia Rossi, Amir Rahman

TL;DR: 提出训练免参数更新的多模态 ICL 框架 AVA，通过线程分解、影响力演示筛选和弱模型一致性校准提升交错图文示例的绑定与抗噪性。

Details

Motivation: 冻结 LVLM 仅靠少量图文答案示范学习新任务，但交错上下文容易把示例绑定到错误图像、冗余示范挤掉有效信息、噪声答案被直接复制。

Result: TRD 将交错上下文切分成逐样本 thread 并用显式边界重组；IDS 多一次前向估计候选示范边际影响，去冗余并按价值排序；WSC 用弱同类模型的重建置信度评分各示范，在解码时软重加权。在五个视觉语言基准的交错 4-shot 设置中，较同设定最强基线平均提高 1.7 分，VizWiz 最高提高 2.4；缩小 ScienceQA-IMG 对视觉信息的依赖差距；30% 示例含噪时保留大部分收益。延迟增加 26%，可用离线缓存摊销；全程冻结 LVLM，不计算梯度。

Insight: 多模态 ICL 的主要问题包括示例结构、冗余和不可靠答案，可在推理前后分别处理；抗噪收益伴随一次额外筛选前向成本。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal in-context learning (ICL) lets frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) acquire new tasks from a handful of image-text-answer demonstrations, yet it remains fragile: demonstrations bind to the wrong images, redundant exemplars crowd out informative ones, and noisy answers are copied verbatim into predictions. We present AVA, a training-free framework that organizes the demonstration pipeline of multimodal ICL into three composable stages. A Thread-Resolved Decomposer (TRD) segments the interleaved context into per-sample threads and re-stitches them with explicit boundaries, restoring image-text binding. An Influence-scored Demonstration Selector (IDS) estimates each candidate’s marginal influence with one extra forward pass, removes redundancy, and orders the survivors by descending value. A Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration (WSC) module scores each demonstration by a weak sibling model’s reconstruction confidence and softly reweights its contribution during decoding. On five vision-language benchmarks with interleaved 4-shot prompts, AVA improves the strongest same-setting baseline by +1.7 points on average, with gains of up to +2.4 points on VizWiz, shrinks the require-visual performance gap on ScienceQA-IMG, and retains most of its gain under 30% noisy demonstrations, at a 26% latency overhead that an offline cache amortizes. No gradients are ever computed; the LVLM stays frozen.

[5] ForcingBooster: Adaptive Patch-wise Denoising for Fast Autoregressive Video Diffusion cs.CVPDF

Zekai Gu, Peng Wang, Jiahao Lu, Yongsen Mao, Cheng Lin

TL;DR: 提出 ForcingBooster，为自回归视频扩散的每个 patch 动态分配去噪步数，易区域提前停止、难区域继续细化。

Details

Motivation: 现有模型生成新视频块时通常在所有空间 patch 上均匀分配计算，忽视静态、简单或重复区域更易去噪，而复杂区域需更多步骤和上下文；统一预算既浪费易区域算力，也可能使全局少步设置无法细化难区域。

Result: ForcingBooster 是可适配多种少步自回归扩散模型的通用框架，仅增加轻量路由模块和少量训练。它根据 patch 难度动态分配去噪：简单区域提前结束，困难区域获得更多细化步骤。实验中视频 patch 总去噪步数约减少 50%，生成画质与四步基线相当。

Insight: 空间异质性需要逐 patch 预算，而非全帧统一去噪步数；该动态分配在减算力同时保持四步基线视觉质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have become a leading approach to real-time interactive video generation and video world modeling. However, existing methods typically allocate computation uniformly across space when generating the next video chunk, updating all patches with the same denoising schedule and number of function evaluations. This ignores the spatial heterogeneity of video content: static, simple, or recurring regions are often easier to denoise, while other regions require further refinement or additional context. A uniform budget thus leads to redundant computation on easy patches, whereas reducing the fixed step count can leave difficult patches insufficiently refined. We introduce \methodname{}, a general framework that dynamically allocates denoising steps to individual patches: easy patches stop early to improve efficiency, while difficult patches receive more refinement steps. This design accelerates generation with negligible quality degradation. Our method is compatible with most existing few-step autoregressive diffusion models, requiring only a lightweight routing module with modest training. Experiments demonstrate that \methodname{} reduces the total number of denoising steps applied across video patches by about 50% while maintaining visual quality comparable to the four-step baseline.

[6] Mind the Temporal Gap: Evaluating Video Models for Temporal Understanding in Driving cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Shiva Sreeram, Erfan Aasi, Tsun-Hsuan Wang, Alaa Maalouf, Daniela Rus

TL;DR: 系统评估视频语言模型在驾驶场景中的时间动作理解，发现提示方式不稳定且有类别偏差，而中间视觉特征的轻量探针/LoRA 可恢复较强运动辨别能力。

Details

Motivation: 视频语言模型常被当作通用视频推理器，但在驾驶等安全关键领域是否能稳健理解时间动态尚不明确。

Result: 作者用需识别驾驶视频自车运动动作的受控任务开展研究。标准、视觉、少样本及 CoT 提示均不可靠，且高度依赖任务/模型并持续存在类别偏差，尽管新模型在特定少样本任务上有明显提升。相比之下，对中间视觉特征训练轻量 LoRA 和简单探针能获得较强运动判别性能，说明运动线索往往已存在于模型中，但最终视频到语言路径未能可靠利用。

Insight: 当前 VLM 在驾驶时间理解上常更像受偏差驱动的帧级识别器，而非可靠时序世界模型；中间表示可能保留可用运动信息。

Abstract: Modern video-language models are increasingly treated as general-purpose video reasoners, yet it remains unclear whether they robustly capture temporal dynamics in safety-critical settings such as driving. We study this question through controlled ego-motion tasks that require identifying actions from driving videos. Across video-language models, we find that standard prompting, visual prompting, few-shot prompting, and chain-of-thought prompting remain unreliable and highly task- and model-dependent, with persistent class biases, although newer models show substantial task-specific few-shot gains. In contrast, lightweight LoRA finetuning and simple probes trained on intermediate visual features recover strong motion-discriminative performance, indicating that motion-relevant cues are often present inside the models but are not reliably used by the final video-to-language pathway. These results suggest that, in driving, current video-language models remain unreliable temporal reasoners and often behave more like frame-level, bias-driven recognizers than dependable temporal world models.

[7] Feature Competition and Its Representational Origins in Vision Language Models: Evidence from the Stroop Paradigm cs.CVPDF

Nurbüke Teker, Rui Xiao, Zeynep Akata, Shuchen Wu

TL;DR: 用 Stroop 心理学范式研究 VLM 对冲突图中文字与颜色线索的选择偏差，并从表示空间解释文本线索更具影响力的原因。

Details

Motivation: 当一幅图像中多个特征都与问题相关时，VLM 如何决定依据哪类线索回答尚不清楚；如 Stroop 图中单词含义与文字颜色冲突时应报告颜色。

Result: 作者将 Stroop 范式用于对比式和生成式 VLM，发现跨模型都存在稳定文本偏置：文字与颜色冲突时，模型更常围绕文本内容作答而非报告颜色。表示分析显示文本线索嵌入更强、可分性更高；这种不对称还预测干预难度，沿文本相关方向 steering 显著容易于沿颜色相关方向。

Insight: 同一图像的不同特征拥有不平等的表示显著性，VLM 会被文本线索主导；这一偏差在行为和表示两个层面均有证据。

Abstract: When you ask a vision-language model (VLM) a question with an image, it answers your question based on the image. But to act means to choose: saying one thing means choosing to respond not say another. This becomes an issue when the image contains multiple features that equally relate to the question at hand: how would VLMs choose to respond? This problem is precisely studied by the classical Stroop task in psychology: among a set of deliberately designed images whose text and color conflicts (e.g., \textcolor{blue}{RED}), participants are instructed to report the color. We adapt this psychological paradigm to a benchmark and prompted a range of contrastive and generative VLMs on their behavior. Across models, we observe a persistent bias toward the feature of text in Stroop images: when text and color disagree, models preferentially generate responses centered to the text rather than color. We then opened the black box and examined the source of this bias and why certain features influences the model’s behavior more than others. In the embedding space, we found that textual cues are represented more strongly and separably than color cues. This representational asymmetry predicts intervention success: steering models toward text-related directions is substantially easier than steering them toward color-related directions. Using a classical psychological paradigm, we demonstrate, at both behavioral and representational levels, that distinct features within the same image can exert unequal influence on VLM behavior. Our findings suggest that VLMs assign different degrees of saliency to different features within the same image, highlighting the need to understand how cue-specific saliency shapes their performance in widely used downstream applications.

[8] Bridging Human and Model Attention: Explainability Analysis of CNN, Mamba, and ViT Architectures with Gaze-Based Validation cs.CV | cs.HC | cs.LGPDF

Marco Stefani, Edoardo Parpaiola, Matteo Bino, Gianluca Caregnato, Francesco Roncolato

TL;DR: 用眼动注视真值系统量化比较 CNN、ViT 和 Vision Mamba 的解释热图与人类视觉注意，并用多种相似度指标分解二者对齐。

Details

Motivation: 高风险应用需精确理解模型依据。既有解释性研究多定性比较，难判断模型显著区域是否与人类视觉注意一致。

Result: 作者将后验归因图和眼动热图统一归一化为空间概率分布，在细粒度分类、医学影像和情绪识别中使用架构专属 XAI 方法，测量 Jensen–Shannon 相似度、卡方相似度和 Pearson 相关，分别反映支持重叠、质量差异和结构共变。CNN 注意较全局、显著图扩散，在一般场景与人类感知相关较好；ViT 更常集中于区分性微特征；Mamba 在医学任务解释性强，但自然图像强度分布与人类不同。指标分析显示支持重叠可近乎 1，同时线性相关接近 0，提示单一指标会掩盖分布差异。作者还研究骨干冻结和集成策略。

Insight: “关注区域有重叠”不代表注意强度分布或空间结构相关；多指标及眼动真值可揭示不同架构的解释差异。

Abstract: High-risk applications of deep learning require precise characterization of model decision-making. We conduct a systematic explainability evaluation across Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Vision Mamba (ViM). Unlike predominantly qualitative studies, we quantify the alignment between model attention and human visual perception using eye-tracking ground truth. We formalize this comparison via a standardized normalization pipeline mapping post-hoc attribution maps and gaze heatmaps onto spatial probability distributions. Alignment is evaluated across fine-grained classification, medical imaging, and emotion recognition using architecture-specific XAI methods. We measure fidelity using Jensen-Shannon similarity, Chi-square similarity, and Pearson correlation, three complementary estimators capturing support overlap, mass discrepancy, and structural co-variation. Our results reveal distinct attention dynamics: CNNs tend to exhibit a more global focus, with spatially more diffuse saliency maps that correlate well with human perception in general contexts, while ViTs demonstrate fine-grained, patch-level attention, often focused on discriminative microfeatures. The Mamba architecture shows biased behavior: it achieves strong explainability in medical tasks but exhibits divergent intensity distributions in natural images; our analytical characterization of the metrics makes this dissociation explicit, since near-unit support overlap is attained simultaneously with a vanishing linear correlation. We further analyze the effect of backbone freezing and ensemble strategies on model interpretability. The results highlight how architectural and training choices influence the alignment between model saliency and human visual attention.

[9] Zebra3D: Efficient Hybrid Foundation Models For Scalable 3D Reconstruction cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Utkarsh Saxena, Ruijing Yang, Mehdi Rezagholizadeh, Akash Haridas, Vikram Appia

TL;DR: 提出 Zebra3D，将稀疏线性混合注意力按层应用于预训练 3D 基础模型，以提高多视图重建效率且不需从头训练。

Details

Motivation: 前馈 3D 基础模型可从无位姿图像重建场景，但全局注意力随视图数呈二次成本，限制大规模视图集合扩展。不同深度层对注意力替换的敏感性不一致。

Result: 作者在 VGGT、HunyuanWorld-Mirror、π³ 上观察到相同趋势：早层较适合线性注意力，中晚层更敏感、注意更集中。Zebra3D 在可替换层用线性中心注意力，在敏感层用 tile-sparse 注意力，并通过 landmark summaries 保留未选上下文。闭式仿射拟合可免训练转换；可选后训练结合任务监督及输出/关系特征蒸馏。三个骨干上高稀疏度仍保持较强重建与相机位姿准确度，免训练版本接近稠密模型。输入视图数增加时端到端加速最高 7.1×（1,024 视图）。后训练 Zebra3D-VGGT 平均注意力稀疏度 94% 时，RealEstate10K AUC@30 为 80.5%，距同数据和日程微调的稠密 VGGT 仅 1.1 个百分点。

Insight: 按深度分配注意力结构比全层统一稀疏更有效；高视图数带来更大速度收益，同时指标比较明确控制了后训练设置。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct scenes from unposed images, but the quadratic cost of global attention limits their scalability to large view sets. We present Zebra3D, a layerwise sparse-linear hybrid attention framework that improves the efficiency of pretrained 3D foundation models without training from scratch. Across VGGT, HunyuanWorld-Mirror, and $π^3$, we observe a broadly consistent depth-wise pattern: early stages are relatively tolerant to linear replacement, while sensitive middle-to-late stages tend to exhibit more concentrated attention. Zebra3D exploits this structure by assigning linear-centered attention to tolerant stages and tile-sparse attention to sensitive stages, using landmark summaries to preserve unselected context. Closed-form affine fitting enables training-free conversion, while optional post-conversion training combines task supervision with output and relational feature distillation. Across all three backbones, Zebra3D achieves strong reconstruction and camera-pose accuracy at high attention sparsity, with training-free variants remaining close to their dense counterparts. End-to-end speedups increase with input view count, reaching up to $7.1\times$ at 1,024 views. Abstract: Referring Audio–Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) is an emergent multimodal task requiring the precise identification and segmentation of a specific object based on a natural language expression grounded in both visual and auditory cues. Unlike traditional AVS, which segments generic sound sources, or referring video object segmentation (Ref-VOS), which relies solely on visual–textual grounding, Ref-AVS necessitates fine-grained tri-modal interaction across vision, audio, and language. However, existing methods often fail to maintain consistency when subjected to target shift, a phenomenon where the model erroneously transfers its focus between objects due to transient occlusions, acoustic fading, or identity ambiguities. To address this, we propose a novel Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL) refinement framework. It introduces a dual-token strategy that decouples frame-level dynamics from a global semantic anchor to explicitly model target-shift cues. We design a shift-aware refinement module that rectifies drift by aligning tokens with domain-specific evidence. Finally, we leverage a decoder that integrates the refined embeddings with a continuous memory update to maintain spatio-temporal coherence and identity consistency throughout the video sequence. Extensive experiments conducted on two Ref-AVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method, significantly outperforming existing AVS, Ref-VOS, and Ref-AVS approaches. The code will be released after publication.

Details

Motivation: 图像融合通常需要同时优化视觉质量与语义属性，但两类目标存在冲突：提升分割语义可能损害融合图像质量。作者希望在退化输入下仍得到清晰融合结果，并强化其对下游分割的语义支持。

Abstract: Existing image fusion methods struggle to balance visual quality and semantic attributes due to the inherent conflict between their optimization objectives. To address this challenge, we propose an attack-defense semantic reinforcement framework for multi-modal image fusion, termed AD-SRF. By decoupling the optimization of these two aspects and then losslessly reintegrating them, this framework injects rich semantic attributes into the fused image without compromising its visual quality. First, we develop a transformer-based restorative visual fusion network as a strong visual baseline. Equipped with the cross-modal retrieval module and the information restoration-fusion collaborative loss, it produces highly visible fused images even under degraded conditions. Second, we introduce an attack-defense game mechanism to strengthen the semantic attributes of the fused images. The attacker learns perturbation patterns that impair downstream segmentation, whereas the defender restores missing semantics from the original inputs and reintegrates them into the fused image through an imperceptible semantic map. Through iterative adversarial interaction, this mechanism drives the defender to continually strengthen the fused image’s semantic attributes, boosting downstream segmentation performance without compromising visual quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AD-SRF achieves a win-win performance in both visual quality and semantic attributes. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/HaoZhang1018/AD-SRF.

Abstract: Existing multi-modal image fusion (MMIF) methods generally face an optimization gap between the visual and semantic domains, limiting the enhancement of perception capabilities. Abstract: Few-step text-to-image generators produce high-quality images with few network forward passes, yet struggle with complex prompts involving multiple subjects, interactions, spatial relations, counting, and attribute binding. Can these errors be reduced without changing the generator’s parameters or increasing its forward-pass count? We propose a lightweight correction framework for a frozen 6B-parameter generator. The generator predicts velocities that determine how its intermediate image representation is updated; a corrector with fewer than 20M parameters learns residual adjustments at selected sampling steps. Because earlier corrections change the states encountered at later steps, our On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision derives residual targets at the states actually reached during corrected generation. A single corrector is shared across selected steps and runs in parallel with the generator. Only the corrector is trained, requiring neither reward optimization nor re-distillation. To our knowledge, this is the first framework for lightweight velocity-residual adaptation of fully frozen few-step text-to-image generators that preserves their original sampling schedule and forward-pass count. On our target complex-scene distribution, the framework achieves a +17.60-percentage-point NetWin over frozen Z-Image-Turbo in pairwise instruction-following evaluation, with only 26.9 ms (0.84%) additional end-to-end latency per image and no increase in backbone evaluations.

Across VideoMAEv2, CLIP, DINOv2 and the egocentric VC-1, the leading principal directions of the embedding are dominated by where the camera looks. Measured head orientation explains them on Ego-Exo4D for egocentric (R^2 0.49-0.70) but not exocentric cameras, and the scene layout explains them on WEAR. The part that the limbs’ inertial signals predict separates activities across people. In a set-level probe, the rest separates exercise sets within a participant but not activities across people. This is consistent with separate roles: the video finds the clips of the same set, and the inertial classifier labels them. Motivation: 联邦视觉语言模型需要在分散客户端数据上适配，同时兼顾每个客户端的个性需求和跨客户端泛化。只共享模型或只做本地适配都难以兼顾这两个目标。

Result: BLEND 选择性微调并聚合 VLM 参数：全局视觉与文本适配器在客户端共享，每个客户端另有个性化视觉投影适配器。损失函数混合全局与个性化适配器输出以促进个性化，并利用零样本 VLM 的投影头构造锚点以促进泛化。论文报告在多种系统设置下持续优于现有基线；摘要未给出数据集、指标数值或具体系统设置。

Insight: 该设计将跨客户端共享能力与本地视觉适配拆分，并用零样本投影头约束个性化结果，减少个性化对通用表示的破坏。现有摘要只能支持定性比较，不能据此补写提升幅度或数据集表现。

Abstract: Federated vision-language models (FedVLMs) represent one of the emerging frontiers of machine learning, aiming to integrate federated learning within the fine-tuning pipelines of vision-language models (VLMs). In this work, we introduce BLEND, a new framework designed to jointly enhance the personalization and generalization capabilities of FedVLMs. BLEND proposes a selective VLM parameter fine-tuning and aggregation strategy that consists of (i) global vision and text adapters shared across clients, and (ii) a personalized vision projection adapter tailored to each client. BLEND further introduces a novel loss function that blends the outputs of the global and personalized adapters to achieve personalization, while simultaneously promoting generalization through a principled anchor design implemented via the projection heads of the zero-shot VLM. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BLEND consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting its ability to effectively balance personalization and generalization across diverse system settings. (Code is available at: https://github.com/payamsiabd/BLEND.git)

[20] UniDeblur: Unified Motion Deblurring via Fourier Convolution Learning cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: UniDeblur 用频域全局滤波学习模糊形成的卷积过程，面向多相机、多场景统一处理运动模糊，并报告在域内与分布外基准上均有竞争力。

Details

Motivation: 现有单幅去模糊模型往往针对特定场景，跨相机和图像内容时性能明显下降。像素级映射容易拟合图像内容，而忽略模糊本身的卷积结构，因此难以适应多来源模糊。

Result: 作者构建多来源去模糊数据集并设计数据剪枝与质量控制流程，提出基于 Fourier Convolution Learning 的统一框架。在频域进行模糊自适应的全局滤波器学习，避免空间域核估计受局部支持范围限制。论文报告该模型可处理不同来源和场景的运动模糊，在域内及 OOD 基准上取得有竞争力的结果；摘要未给出具体数值或数据集名称。

Abstract: Image deblurring is a fundamental task in computer vision, yet existing methods mainly specialize in isolated scenarios. Consequently, the deblurring performance of these models drops significantly across scenarios with different cameras and image contents. This motivates the development of a unified motion deblurring framework that can handle diverse blur patterns. To this end, we construct a multi-source dataset and develop a data pruning and quality control pipeline. Then, to restore blur images with various motion blur kernels across different scenarios, we propose a unified motion deblurring framework via Fourier Convolution Learning. Existing deblurring networks usually rely on pixel-level mappings and overlook the convolutional nature of blur. Thus, the network mostly fits the image content itself rather than learning the convolution process, making it difficult to handle multi-source blur images. Instead of relying on spatial mapping, we perform blur-adaptive global filter learning in Fourier space, which avoids the limited spatial support of kernel estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model effectively handles motion blurs across diverse sources and scenes, achieving competitive performance across both in-domain and OOD benchmarks. Code will be released at github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur.

[21] In-Context Learning Can Help Vision-Language Models Overcome Training Priors cs.CVPDF

Kun Wang, Xindi Wu, Sanghyuk Chun, Olga Russakovsky, Esin Tureci

TL;DR: 受控视觉上下文示例能让 VLM 在与训练先验冲突的反事实图像上更依赖视觉证据，五类任务中最高报告提升 27.2%；当视觉能力本身薄弱时，示例帮助有限。

Details

Motivation: VLM 可能因训练中学到的统计先验而忽略图像中与先验冲突的证据。单看错误难以判断原因是缺少视觉能力，还是模型已有能力却被先验带偏，因此需要受控评测区分两者。

Result: 作者在五类视觉任务上比较先验冲突与先验一致的反事实样例。加入示例后，Qwen3-VL 235B 和 32B 在先验冲突样例上的准确率分别提升 27.2 和 15.1 个百分点，而先验一致样例准确率基本不变；五个 VLM 家族的提升范围为 9.7–30.3 个百分点。表示分析显示示例会将注意力转向视觉依据并降低对先验线索的依赖。若底层视觉能力弱或缺失，上下文学习的纠错作用有限。

Insight: 部分所谓视觉推理失败可能源自模型未调用已有的视觉 grounding 能力，而非完全缺少该能力；控制先验冲突的测试有助于诊断这一差别。提升仅适用于具备相关视觉能力的模型与所测反事实任务，不能认为上下文示例可以普遍补足能力缺口。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) learn strong statistical regularities during training, which can make them fail to properly respond to visual evidence when input images violate those regularities. It is challenging to disambiguate these prior-induced errors from errors caused by the model missing the underlying visual capabilities. In this work, we use controlled visual in-context learning to encourage the model to focus on the visual evidence, and demonstrate that this alone is frequently sufficient to correct the errors – suggesting that the models in fact do possess the relevant visual capabilities. Specifically, we show that across five vision-centric tasks, demonstrations yield large gains on prior-conflicting counterfactual examples ($+27.2%$ for Qwen3-VL 235B and $+15.1%$ for Qwen3-VL 32B) while leaving accuracy on prior-aligned examples nearly unchanged. These findings generalize across five VLM families with gains from $+9.7%$ to $+30.3%$. We analyze visual representations and find that demonstrations indeed redirect attention toward grounding cues while reducing reliance on cues that support the learned prior. Finally, we find that in-context learning is limited when the underlying visual capability is weak or absent. Together, these results show that current VLMs possess visual grounding capabilities that standard evaluations may overlook, underscoring the need for controlled evaluation design in diagnosing and improving model capability.

Details

Motivation: 真实图结构证据可能分散在不同来源、时间快照或局部观察中，但既有图推理基准通常只给单个完整图，无法测量模型能否跨视图对齐并组合证据。

Result: MVGraph 包含 8,000 个实例，覆盖八类图论任务，来自局部不完整视图和时间视图两种多图场景；每题均经程序构造与验证，保证不能仅凭单个视图求解。基准还将能力拆为感知、对齐、组合、推理四阶段，提供 29 项诊断任务、64,000 个实例及用于评估中间过程的标准中间表示。对 15 个 LVLM 的评测显示，多图推理整体困难，跨视图边对齐与关系级组合是主要瓶颈；时间约束和视图数量增加也暴露更多能力边界。

Insight: 通过强制证据跨视图分布并验证单视图不可解，MVGraph 将跨视图证据组合与单图理解区分开；阶段诊断和标准中间表示便于定位错误发生在何处。摘要没有列出具体模型分数，所述能力瓶颈来自对 15 个模型的定性诊断。

Abstract: We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities—Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning—and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at https://github.com/AAAndy-Zhu/MVGraph.

Xihua Sheng, Ruijie Liu, Chang Wen Chen

Motivation: 视频大语言模型处理长视觉 token 序列的推理开销高。现有免训练压缩虽然减少送入语言模型的 token 数，但在帧内 token 保护和跨帧合并阶段仍需大量视觉特征比较，压缩本身也会带来成本。

Result: 方法将编码参考帧依赖、运动矢量和残差信息映射到视觉 token 网格：残差生成 token 保护的紧凑候选池，运动信息沿参考帧链传播，为稀疏采样帧间的时间 token 合并提供局部候选。随后仅在候选范围内比较视觉特征并作出压缩决策，不修改基础 VLLM。三种代表性 VLLM、四个视频理解基准的实验中，性能与当前先进免训练基线 FlashVID 相当，部分设置超过它，同时显著减少 token 保护和时间匹配所需的特征比较。

Insight: 压缩视频编码信息可作为低成本搜索先验，让相似性计算集中在更可能相关的 token 上，从而改善准确率与计算量的折衷。论文没有给出摘要级具体压缩率、延迟或各基准分数，且评测仅覆盖所列模型与基准。

Abstract: Video large language models (VLLMs) rely on long visual token sequences to understand complex temporal content, which makes visual token compression important for efficient inference. Existing training-free compression methods mainly reduce the number of tokens sent to the language model, but the compression process itself can still require many visual feature comparisons, especially for intra-frame token protection and inter-frame temporal token merging. This paper proposes a video codec-guided training-free token compression method for VLLMs that uses coding information already available in compressed video as a low-cost search prior. The method aligns reference-frame dependencies, motion vectors, and residuals with the visual token grid. Residual information forms compact candidate pools for token protection, while motion information is propagated along reference-frame chains to construct localized candidates for temporal token merging between sparsely sampled frames. Local visual feature similarity comparison is then used within these candidates to make the final compression decisions, without modifying the base VLLM. Experiments on three representative VLLMs and four video-understanding benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art training-free baseline FlashVID, and in some settings surpasses it, while substantially reducing the feature comparisons required by token protection and temporal matching. These results demonstrate that codec information can effectively improve the accuracy-complexity tradeoff of video token compression.

This paper focuses on professional motion editing, which involves fine-grained motion technique and temporal structure. Given professional motion names but lacking detailed descriptions, existing models often fail to generate professional motions, such as incorrectly lifting the front wheel under a Stoppie instruction. To address this challenge, we introduce MotionCoach, a trained multimodal agent that uses multi-turn tools and performs self-verification, producing a grounded prompt and a reference clip as reliable and sufficient multimodal guidance to inject external professional motion knowledge into video editing models. [25] The Evidence Anchoring Boundary: Where Visual Interventions Matter and When They Help in Multimodal LLMs cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Qiji Zhou, Junyi Hu, Peiyu Wang, Yue Zhang

TL;DR: 研究提出视觉干预的 Evidence Anchoring Boundary，并据此选择性路由干预：校准后属性题准确率提升 1.56–1.81 个百分点，而对所有题统一做反事实相减可能无效或有害。

Details

Motivation: 免训练视觉干预可以减轻多模态大模型幻觉，但不同解码层的干预影响力可能不同；即使它改变答案，也可能破坏原本正确的判断。需要弄清干预在哪一层仍能改变证据利用，以及哪些题型适合采用。

Result: 作者在三个模型家族的六个检查点上，逐层施加 RMS 强度匹配的视觉残差编辑，发现干预效果随深度持续减弱，并定义该编辑的操作起点为 Evidence Anchoring Boundary（EAB）。用校准查询固定边界后，在独立测试查询中，EAB 前的干预效果是边界后的 13.78–26.10 倍；中间层读出还显示，干预影响力可能在答案偏好稳定前就已消失。对存在性、属性和关系问题的反事实相减可能纠正误导偏好、保持不变，也可能删掉支持正确答案的偏好。因此作者设计校准式选择路由，在校准支持该题型干预时才修改预测；三个检查点的全量属性准确率提高 1.56–1.81 个百分点，普遍相减中性或有害。

Insight: 模型内部对证据的锚定与答案偏好稳定不是同一时刻；干预效果取决于层位置和问题类型。EAB 是针对该视觉残差编辑定义的操作边界，不能直接视为所有干预方法通用的模型层常数。

Abstract: Visual interventions provide a training-free way to reduce hallucinations in multimodal large language models. However, an intervention may have little leverage at some layers and may damage a correct answer even when it changes the output. We investigate where visual interventions retain leverage and when they improve predictions. First, we apply the same visual-residual edit at successive decoder layers, matching its RMS strength across depth. Across six checkpoints from three model families, we observe a persistent drop in intervention effect and define its operational onset for this edit as the \textbf{Evidence Anchoring Boundary (EAB)}. With boundaries fixed on calibration queries, held-out effects on disjoint evaluation queries before EAB are $13.78\times$–$26.10\times$ larger than those after it. Comparison with intermediate readouts shows that the edit can lose leverage before answer preferences stabilize. Second, we compare counterfactual subtraction across presence, attribute, and relation questions. Subtraction can correct a misleading preference, leave a decision unchanged, or remove a preference supporting the correct answer. Based on these observations, we develop calibrated selective routing, which preserves the original prediction unless calibration supports an intervention for the question type. It improves full-set attribute accuracy by $1.56$–$1.81$ percentage points across three checkpoints, while universal subtraction is neutral or harmful.

[26] Unified Generative Video Compression with Autoregressive and Bidirectional Video Diffusion cs.CVPDF

Tianyu Zhang, Shuai Huo, Dengchao Jin, Hewei Liu, Meng Lei

TL;DR: UniGVC 用单一码流支持双向高质量回放与自回归低延迟流式解码；1080p 下 BI 模式在 0.0005 bpp 仍有时间稳定性，AR 模式达 36.85 FPS，速度比 BI 快 250 倍以上。

Details

Motivation: 双向视频扩散能利用完整序列上下文，压缩质量较好，但需要等待整段序列且延迟大；自回归模型支持低延迟流式解码，却因时间上下文受限而压缩效率较低。实时传输与后续高质量回放常同时需要这两种能力。

Result: UniGVC 是一个统一生成式视频编解码器，从同一码流支持两种解码模式：UniGVC-BI 使用密集注意力双向 DiT 做随机访问、高质量解码；UniGVC-AR 使用窗口化自回归 DiT 做低延迟流式解码。两路共享分层潜变量编解码器并递归生成超低码率条件，BI 路径还作为条件教师对 AR 路径进行单步蒸馏；轻量条件视频解码器进一步降低流式延迟。标准 1080p 基准上，BI 模式在评测方法中取得总体率失真—感知折衷最佳，在最低 0.0005 bpp 仍生成时间稳定视频；AR 模式以 36.85 FPS 解码 1080p，速度超过 BI 的 250 倍。

Insight: 系统在同一码流上兼顾实时预览与后续高质量回放，核心是共享潜变量条件并让双向模型监督单步 AR 解码。码率、质量和速度结论属于所测标准 1080p 基准；摘要未列出具体失真或感知指标。

Abstract: Video diffusion models can reconstruct visually compelling content from severely compressed representations at ultra-low bitrates, but they impose a rigid deployment trade-off. Bidirectional (BI) models achieve high quality through dense full-sequence attention, yet require the entire sequence before reconstruction and incur substantial latency. Autoregressive (AR) models enable low-delay streaming, but their restricted temporal context reduces compression efficiency. This tension is particularly relevant when a video stream must be decoded in real time yet is also stored for later, quality-oriented playback, making AR and BI models natural choices for the live and replay stages, respectively. To support both stages, we present UniGVC, a Unified Generative Video Codec that supports two decoding modes from a single bitstream. UniGVC-BI employs a BI-DiT with dense attention for quality-oriented random-access decoding, whereas UniGVC-AR uses windowed AR-DiT for low-delay streaming. Both paths share a hierarchical latent codec that recurrently produces the same conditions with ultra-low bitrates, allowing UniGVC-BI to serve as a conditional teacher for single-step distillation of UniGVC-AR. A tiny conditional video decoder further reduces streaming latency while preserving most of the reconstruction quality. On standard 1080p benchmarks, UniGVC-BI achieves the best overall rate-distortion-perception trade-off among the evaluated methods and produces temporally stable video at rates as low as 0.0005 bits per pixel. Meanwhile, UniGVC-AR remains competitive while decoding 1080p video at 36.85 FPS, over $250\times$ faster than UniGVC-BI. Code and models will be released later.

Details

Motivation: 世界动作模型继承视频生成模型的视觉先验，但其 VAE 表示主要为重建画面优化，未必适合操控。额外视觉表示可能帮助操作，不过以往工作常同时改变表示和整合机制，难以区分改进来源。

Result: 作者在受控设置下比较视觉表示、五种整合机制及特征组合，得出三点发现：通用视觉表示可带来更广泛的鲁棒性收益，且能匹配或超过为特定能力预训练的表示；通道级联合预测的平均成功率最高，分析认为收益与未来特征预测及动作专家利用新增信息有关；额外来源应按相对已有表示新增的任务相关信息选择，同一来源可能因组合不同而有益或有害。即使最佳组合带来的增益也小于在纯 VAE 基线上加入强通用表示的初始收益。所得表示方案 R²-WAM 在 RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop 达 84.7%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 达 92.8%，较 VAE-only 基线分别提高 5.9 和 2.2 个百分点。

Insight: 对 WAM 来说，先加入强通用视觉表示通常比继续堆叠多源特征更关键；表示组合应以新增任务相关信息为准，整合机制也会影响是否能用好信息。结论基于所测两项机器人基准与比较方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) inherit powerful priors from pretrained video generation models, but their variational autoencoder (VAE) representations are optimized for visual reconstruction rather than manipulation. External visual representations can improve manipulation, but existing methods couple representation choices with different integration mechanisms, obscuring their individual effects. We systematically compare visual representations, five integration mechanisms, and feature combinations under controlled settings. Our study yields three main findings. First, strong general-purpose visual representations provide broader robustness gains and can match or outperform representations pretrained for specific visual capabilities. Second, channel-wise joint prediction achieves the highest average success rate; further analyses suggest that future-feature prediction and the action expert’s effective use of added visual information contribute to its gains. Third, additional sources should be selected for the task-relevant information they contribute beyond existing representations, as the same source can help one combination but harm another. However, even the best evaluated composition provides a smaller additional gain than the initial improvement from adding a strong general-purpose representation to the VAE-only baseline. We instantiate the resulting Representation Recipe as R$^2$-WAM. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop with an 84.7% success rate and reaches 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0, improving over the VAE-only baselines by 5.9 and 2.2 percentage points, respectively. Taken together, these findings suggest that future work should prioritize stronger general-purpose visual representations and more effective mechanisms for integrating them into WAMs, with feature composition serving as a supplementary route to improvement.

Abstract: Natural-image-pretrained foundation models are increasingly adapted to medical imaging, yet transfer remains unreliable under domain shift, modality heterogeneity, and label scarcity. We trace part of this fragility to an overlooked design choice. Standard vision transformers place all global modeling on self-attention over spatial tokens, an object-layout prior well suited to natural scenes but not to medical images, where the discriminative signal lies in feature correlations and texture that recur across scanners while layout does not. We ask whether the axis of global attention, spatial versus feature, is itself a lever for transferable medical representations. Existing channel-aware hybrids confound this question, adding channel attention to a spatially-mixed backbone and leaving the axes coupled and the feature axis unmeasurable in isolation. We design FAD-ViT, a backbone that strictly decouples the two axes, making feature-axis attention the primary global operator and the attention axis a controlled variable. Evaluating comprehensively across 23 classification, segmentation, and low-data tasks under matched tokenization, capacity, and protocol, we find that shifting global attention from the spatial to the feature axis leaves from-scratch performance essentially unchanged, yet markedly strengthens transfer. Identical ImageNet pretraining yields up to roughly three times the transfer gain, with consistent improvements on every held-out segmentation task at up to a quarter the parameter count and far lower compute than standard and channel-attention baselines. The advantage is largest where texture rather than geometry is discriminative, tracking greater reuse of pretrained features. The axis of global attention is thus a design lever for medical transfer, and making it channel-primary is a simple, cheap, and principled choice.

[29] Redirecting Teacher Supervision: Temporal-Response-Guided Latent Distillation for Video-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Xinkai Lu, Junli Zha, Haolan Wei, Jiahui Wang, Chao Zhou

TL;DR: TRG 根据视频学生表示对局部时间扰动的响应调整教师隐藏态监督方向，在四个视频基准上使 4B 学生相对输出级 OPD 基线的宏平均准确率提高 2.86 个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏会基于学生自己生成的序列传递教师知识，但直接对齐隐藏状态并未利用学生对时间变化的响应来决定监督方向，因而对视频任务中的时间学习支持不足。

Result: Temporal-Response Guidance（TRG）先测量表示对局部时间扰动的响应，再将教师修正量在这些响应张成空间中的投影加回原始修正量，并恢复原修正量范数。投影不单独归一化，响应空间之外的修正分量也予以保留。采用正交切空间投影和分离梯度的目标构造时，TRG 改变监督方向，同时保持目标构造时的余弦损失值和对齐表示的梯度范数。在 TempCompass、Video-MME、MVBench 和 Video-MMMU 上，4B 学生相对报告的仅输出 OPD 基线宏平均准确率提升 2.86 个百分点。

Insight: 监督方向由学生对局部时间扰动的敏感方向引导，能将隐藏状态蒸馏用于时间表征学习，而不只是直接拉近教师与学生表示。摘要报告的是四基准宏平均增益，并给出保持损失值和梯度范数的特定构造条件。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers capabilities through teacher predictions on student-generated sequences, as studied by GKD and MiniLLM for language models and Video-OPD for video tasks. We ask how internal supervision can further support temporal learning. Direct hidden-state alignment does not explicitly use the student’s temporal responses to choose a supervision direction. We propose Temporal-Response Guidance (TRG), which measures representation responses to local temporal perturbations, adds the teacher correction’s projection onto their span back to the original correction, and restores its original norm. The projection is not normalized separately, and correction components outside the response space are retained. With an orthogonal tangent projector and a detached target, TRG changes the supervision direction while preserving the cosine-loss value and aligned-representation gradient norm at target construction. On TempCompass, Video-MME, MVBench, and Video-MMMU, TRG improves the 4B student’s macro accuracy by 2.86 percentage points over the reported output-only OPD baseline.

[30] GeoGR: Spatial-Semantic Token Compression and Group Routing for Efficient 3D VLMs cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yongxin Zheng, Hao Wu, Fang Li, Hu Fu, Peiran Yin

TL;DR: GeoGR 联合压缩三维场景 token 与语言模型中的视觉计算：跨视角聚合为带语义锚点的体素 token，并按相关性分组跳层；在两种 7B 3D VLM 上保留 95.2%/96.8% 性能，prefill 加速 6.8×/6.1×。

Details

Motivation: 多视角表示让图像/视频 VLM 能理解三维场景，但重复观察同一区域会造成 token 冗余，而且并非每层 LLM 计算都需要所有视觉 token，推理成本因此较高。

Result: GeoGR 分两阶段处理。投影层模块 GeoSemZip 将跨视角观察合并为 3D 体素 token，结合语义显著性和空间覆盖形成紧凑场景表示，并把未选体素聚合成上下文锚点以保留信息。LLM 层的 GroupRoute 利用 token 相关性在较短层区间局部稳定、长区间变化的特点，结合晚进入、早退出和可恢复分组跳过：低相关 token 暂时跳过完整层并保存隐藏状态，在预算选择的锚点层重新加入。压缩感知后训练集含 67K 样本，为原训练池的 30%。相对稠密后训练模型，LLaVA-OneVision-7B 和 Video-3D LLM-7B 分别保留 95.2% 和 96.8% 性能，prefill 分别加速 6.8× 与 6.1×。

Insight: 方法同时消除视角冗余与逐层视觉计算冗余，且通过上下文锚点和可恢复跳层降低信息损失。结果覆盖含/不含显式 3D 空间编码的两种模型；性能保留率相对于各自稠密后训练版本。

Abstract: Multi-view representations enable pretrained image and video vision-language models to understand 3D scenes, but their long visual token sequences make inference costly. Redundancy arises both from repeated observations of the same scene regions across views and from processing all visual tokens at every LLM layer, regardless of their relevance to the current computation. We propose GeoGR, a two-stage framework that jointly compresses scene representations and visual computation. At the projector level, GeoSemZip consolidates cross-view observations into 3D voxel tokens and combines semantic saliency with spatial coverage to construct a compact scene representation. Unselected voxel features are aggregated into contextual anchors to retain scene information that would otherwise be discarded. At the LLM level, we identify an effective visual computation window and observe that query-conditioned token relevance remains locally stable but changes over longer layer intervals. Guided by these findings, GroupRoute combines late entry and early exit with recoverable group-wise skipping. Low-relevance tokens bypass complete LLM layers while retaining their hidden states and can rejoin the active group at subsequent anchor layers selected under a computation budget. For compression-aware post-training, we construct a compact dataset of 67K samples by retaining 30% of the original training pool. With this protocol, GeoGR retains 95.2% and 96.8% of the dense post-trained models’ performance on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Video-3D LLM-7B, respectively, while accelerating LLM prefill by $6.8\times$ and $6.1\times$. These results demonstrate the consistent effectiveness of GeoGR across 3D VLMs with and without explicit 3D spatial encoding, highlighting a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/GeoGR.

Abstract: In Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks involving multi-step instructions as guidance, an agent must associate its observations with the relevant instruction content at each decision point. Most graph-based methods commonly match candidate trajectories with the entire instruction, allowing already executed content to interfere with subsequent decisions. Although instruction-execution progress provides a direct cue for separating executed and pending content, existing progress estimation approaches often rely on explicit progress supervision, sophisticated progress-learning stages, or costly online reasoning with LLMs. In this paper, we propose a robust VLN framework with Awareness of Instruction-Execution Progress (VLN-AIEP), which couples progress estimation with navigation decision-making. VLN-AIEP associates the visited trajectory with the instruction to suppress embeddings corresponding to executed content, and then matches candidate locations with the pending content. This coupling makes correct navigation decisions depend on accurate progress estimation, allowing the progress estimator to learn directly from navigation decision labels without explicit progress annotations. To support progress estimation, we construct a landmark-anchored trajectory representation that encodes the semantic labels and viewpoint-relative geometry of instruction-referred landmarks. These landmarks serve as spatial references for associating the visited trajectory with executed instruction content. Extensive experiments on R2R, RxR, CVDN, and R2R-CE demonstrate that the proposed method achieves favorable performance across multiple metrics.

[34] What Do Visual-Head Discovery Scores Actually Find? Attention Source, Necessity, and Random-Control Pitfalls in Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Md Abdul Kadir, Daniel Sonntag

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析视觉头发现分数、注意力来源与验证方式，发现查询跨度注意力更能找到必要头，而视觉头引导效果并不优于同层随机头。

Details

Motivation: VLM 研究常按注意力模式分数选出关注问题相关图像区域的头，再通过引导或屏蔽验证它们。但选分数的注意力来源和查询类型如何影响选头，以及引导能力能否证明这些头确有特殊作用，尚不清楚。

Result: 研究在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上对查询类型、发现语料和注意力读取 token 位置进行 2×3×2 全因子比较，并用 CERS 测 steerability、NCS 测 necessity，均与层匹配随机头组比较。影响大小排序显示注意力来源与查询类型远大于语料：更换来源时 top-100 头集合中位数仅保留 49 个，更换查询保留 55 个，更换语料保留 84 个；同一数据两半则共享 96%–99%。必要性随注意力来源变化：查询跨度注意力发现的头比末 token 注意力发现头更必要，差值 0.083、p=6×10⁻¹⁰；指代表达+查询跨度头组高于 250 个层匹配随机组 17–19 个标准差，Holm 校正 p=0.048，并在若干新近 VLM 上复现。相反，已发现头组的引导效果不高于随机组（rank p≥0.14）。

Insight: 头组是否必要与它是否能被强力引导是不同问题；此处必要性可区分发现设置，而所用强度下的 steerability 并不特异。注意力读取位置和查询形式会显著改变被发现头集合，论文据此提出视觉头发现结果的报告协议。结论主实验来自 Qwen3-VL-8B，必要性现象另在多个模型复现。

Abstract: Attention heads that attend to image regions relevant to a question are increasingly used to steer, ground and interpret vision-language models (VLMs) and to reduce their hallucinations. They are mostly found by ranking heads with an attention- pattern score and validated by steering or masking them. We ask how the choices inside such a discovery score shape the heads it returns, and which of the two validations identifies them. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct we vary the query type, the discovery corpus and the token positions whose attention is read (the attention source) in a full 2 x 3 x 2 design, and test all 12 head-sets for steerability (CERS: Contrastive Evidence-Region Steering score) and necessity (NCS: Necessity Con- trast Score) against layer-matched random head-sets. (1) The attention source and the query type reshape the selected heads far more than the corpus: changing the source keeps a median of 49 of the top-100 heads, the query 55 and the cor- pus 84, while two halves of the same data share 96-99. (2) Necessity follows the attention source: head-sets discovered from query-span attention are more nec- essary than last-token ones (difference 0.083, p=6 x 10^-10), and the two re- ferring + query-span sets lie 17-19 standard deviations above 250 layer-matched random sets (Holm-adjusted p=0.048). The referring + query-span effect re- plicates on several recent VLMs. (3) Steerability is not specific to the discovered heads: no discovered head-set steers better than its layer-matched random sets (rank p >= 0.14), and random sets drawn from the same or from any layer span the discovered sets’ scores. Necessity therefore separates the discovery settings, while steering at this strength does not. We turn these findings into a protocol for reporting visual-head discoveries.

[35] Replaying the Movie in Your Mind Decoding Dynamic Visual Perception and Recall from EEG cs.CVPDF

Insight: 相同视频内容的语义信息在感知和内在回忆中可能有相近可解码程度，但产生这些表征的状态仍可被区分。解码准确率不等同于纯视觉神经表征证据，因为当前验证协议下眼动等任务相关因素仍可能参与。

Abstract: Decoding dynamic visual content from EEG has advanced rapidly for visual perception, yet it remains unclear which aspects of neural representations are preserved when people internally replay dynamic scenes. We introduce PAIR, an EEG benchmark collected from 20 participants using an interleaved, clip-wise paradigm that pairs naturalistic video viewing with immediate visual recall. This design enables a comparison between externally driven perception and internally generated recall for the same stimulus content within a fixed trial sequence. We benchmark multiple decoding models on hierarchical semantic classification at both fine-grained and coarse-grained levels, meta-feature decoding tasks covering motion, color, object number, face, and human presence, and a state-decoding task that discriminates perception from recall. Within-subject validation results show numerically similar performance for several model–task combinations, alongside higher perception accuracy in two coarse-grained comparisons after multiple-comparison correction. In contrast, perception and recall are highly separable at the state level under the present validation protocol. State accuracy remains high after alpha-band ablation, although this does not exclude ocular or other task-related contributions.

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction provides a scalable representation for building inspection and maintenance. However, photorealistic reconstruction alone does not fully provide the geometric measurability, semantic structure, and interactive information access required for engineering applications. This study proposes a measurable semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework with region and depth supervision for interactive building inspection from multi-view imagery. Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to track progress through an extended instruction, identify landmarks relevant to its current objective, and understand the surrounding spatial structure to act. End-to-end navigation policies typically rely on action supervision to implicitly learn these capabilities. Without explicit guidance on the evidence underlying each decision, however, policies can overfit to correlations predictive of training actions rather than learn the task-relevant grounding and geometry needed for generalization. Our insight is to guide the representations that connect instructions to actions: identifying what remains to be done determines which visual evidence matters, while understanding the geometry around that evidence informs how to move. We introduce GuidedNav, a unified framework that explicitly shapes these representations through training-time guidance. Subtask and landmark guidance steer native pre-action attention toward the active instruction segment and relevant visual regions, grounding action prediction in the current objective. Spatial guidance aligns the policy’s internal representations with geometric features that capture long-horizon context and local spatial structure. Together, these objectives strengthen navigation capabilities while avoiding the inference overhead of intermediate textual reasoning and online geometric encoding. We also introduce Guided-R2R and Guided-RxR, two annotation-enriched navigation datasets that support more interpretable VLN research. GuidedNav establishes state-of-the-art performance among the compared monocular RGB policies, improving success rates by 8.1% and 13.1% relative to the strongest prior results on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, respectively, while achieving faster inference. Extensive experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness and real-world transferability of our approach.

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) generally fall short of expectations in Fine-Grained Visual Recognition (FGVR), although they exhibit promising FGVR foundations, i.e., visual perception and broad category knowledge. Most existing studies seek to remedy this deficiency by adopting domain-specific post-training, at the risk of compromising generalization to unseen domains. Different from these efforts, in this paper, we revisit the FGVR mechanism underlying MLLMs from both the empirical and theoretical perspectives and uncover an inference-time phenomenon termed FGVR Laziness. In brief, FGVR Laziness refers to the insufficient association between visual cues and category concepts under certain candidate category lists and recognition guidance, leaving the FGVR foundations of MLLMs largely under-utilized. To mitigate FGVR Laziness, we propose Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition (LIVE), a training-free framework which jointly refines candidate lists and recognition guidance to elicit the latent FGVR capability of MLLMs. Specifically, LIVE first performs query-adaptive candidate inference to derive a compact yet reliable candidate list. Meanwhile, LIVE constructs category-specific FGVR skills by mining the intra-category characteristics and inter-category differences. Finally, LIVE selectively invokes detailed skill guidance according to recognition uncertainty and category confusion, shifting MLLM-based FGVR from a cursory glance to a focus on associating discriminative visual cues with category concepts. Extensive experiments on 13 benchmarks against 24 state-of-the-art methods spanning fine-grained category recognition, action recognition, and general category recognition, verify the effectiveness and generalizability of LIVE across different MLLM backbones. Details

Motivation: 迭代式文生视频需要根据反馈纠正构图与时间一致性错误，但现有方式每次修改往往重新完整生成，重复计算且可能破坏已正确内容。

Result: PreCoG 是生成阶段的视频迭代纠错框架。PreQA 从低成本预览中诊断语义失败并构造修正条件；PreEdit 在缓存的生成轨迹上按条件编辑，并衰减与修正方向正交的变化，以减少对正确内容的干扰。系统仅对选定候选完成生成，复用其缓存轨迹，减少重复计算并维持预览与最终视频的语义一致性。实验中 PreCoG 的 VBench 与 T2V-CompBench 得分最高；在 0.56× VSR 推理时间下，其 VS2 分数接近 VSR 的领先值。此外，在推理时间为 GENMAC 的 0.86× 时，PreCoG 在三项质量与对齐指标上均胜过 GENMAC。

Abstract: Iterative text-to-video generation relies on feedback to correct compositional and temporal failures. However, existing methods typically require another full generation run for each correction, repeating computation and potentially disrupting already correct content. We introduce PreCoG, an iterative framework for correcting semantic failures during video generation. PreQA diagnoses failures from low-cost previews and constructs corrective conditions, while PreEdit edits cached generation trajectories under these conditions, attenuating orthogonal changes to limit disruption of already correct content. PreCoG completes generation only for the selected candidate, reusing its cached trajectory to reduce redundant computation and help preserve semantic consistency between the preview and the final video. Experiments show that PreCoG achieves the highest VBench and T2V-CompBench scores and approaches VSR’s leading VS2 score at $0.56\times$ its inference time. Abstract: Generative video super-resolution (VSR) can recover visually compelling high-frequency detail by leveraging diffusion priors, but iterative sampling and heavy spatio-temporal coupling make practical deployments difficult. We study a different operating point: practical HD generative VSR on a single high-end consumer GPU without end-to-end retraining. To this end, we present a zero-shot framework built entirely from pretrained components. The proposed system decouples spatial enhancement from temporal stabilization. A time-distilled one-step diffusion backbone handles spatial upscaling, while temporal coherence is promoted by a lightweight pretrained flow-based interpolator that synthesizes intermediate frames between super-resolved keyframes. To further improve scalability, we introduce a resolution-aware hybrid attention scheme that, in a zero-shot manner, replaces selected high-resolution global-attention layers with neighborhood attention while preserving global attention in semantically critical low-resolution layers, while also improving temporal consistency. At 4K, the proposed attention substitution can yield up to a 44% speedup over the original attention implementation. Across standard VSR benchmarks, the method consistently shifts the speed-quality trade-off decisively toward runtime efficiency, trading reconstruction fidelity for strong no-reference perceptual quality, while remaining reasonably competitive against generative approaches in temporal consistency. Abstract: Generative inbetweening aims to synthesize temporally coherent intermediate frames between a start frame and a target frame. Although pretrained image-to-video diffusion models provide strong priors for large-motion inbetweening, they are not explicitly designed to reach a specified target frame. They may therefore generate realistic individual frames while drifting from the target or producing unstable motion under large displacements and non-rigid dynamics. We propose DSG-GI, a framework that adapts pretrained latent video diffusion models through \textbf{D}estination-Conditioned Adaptation and \textbf{S}emantic-\textbf{G}eometric Alignment. Destination-Conditioned Adaptation treats the target frame as the destination of the sequence and provides temporally progressive guidance along the generated trajectory. Semantic-Geometric Alignment aligns the token-wise spatio-temporal relational geometry of backbone features with that of a frozen video foundation encoder and applies a flow-matching-aware latent flow constraint to samples near the data endpoint of the probability path. Together, these components strengthen destination guidance and preserve coherent semantic-geometric motion structure. Extensive experiments on standard and large-motion inbetweening benchmarks demonstrate improvements in temporal stability, target-frame faithfulness, and perceptual quality. Our code will be made publicly available.

Motivation: 当前可控视频生成常由文生图模型先生成锚帧，再交给图像到视频模型控制身份、构图和风格。但图像的美学和质量分数未必能预测下游视频质量，优化单帧图像指标甚至可能损害视频运动。

Result: 分析发现，锚帧结构/纹理评分与 I2V 视频质量在视觉和运动维度上呈负相关。对 T2I 模型按图像奖励微调会产生结构和角色细节更密集、图像指标更高的锚帧，却降低所测所有 I2V 模型的视频奖励，并不可预测地改变运动。Golden Anchor Learning（GAL）冻结 I2V 模型，用候选锚帧生成的视频反馈为图像级偏好打分，再通过 DPO 微调 T2I 生成器。在四种 I2V 模型上，VBench 平均分较未调优生成器提高 2.97%–8.46%；视频在 VideoReward-Overall、抽取帧在 HPSv3 和 UniPercept 上也更高；VBench 动态程度提高 5.62%–38.24%。GAL 仅用 HunyuanVideo-1.5 生成结果训练，增益也迁移至另外三个模型。

Abstract: Video generation supports digital content creation, with applications in virtual avatars, AI-driven filmmaking, interactive game engines, and world models. State-of-the-art systems achieve controllability primarily through image-to-video (I2V) generation, in which an injected anchor image determines the identity, composition, and style of the resulting video. In modern pipelines, this anchor is synthesized by a text-to-image (T2I) generator. This raises a largely overlooked question: Are images obtained directly from the T2I model truly suitable for current video models? Our analysis shows that anchor aesthetic and quality scores are weakly correlated with downstream video quality. The structure-and-texture score is negatively correlated with I2V video quality in both the visual and motion dimensions. Fine-tuning the T2I model on an image reward accordingly yields anchors with visibly denser structural and character detail: they score higher on image metrics, yet lower the video reward on every I2V model we test and change downstream motion unpredictably. We therefore introduce Golden Anchor Learning (GAL), to our knowledge the first framework for learning golden anchors by optimizing a T2I generator with downstream video feedback. GAL evaluates candidate anchors through the videos they induce under a frozen I2V model and converts video rewards into image-level preferences for DPO fine-tuning of the T2I generator. On all four I2V models, GAL raises the VBench average by $2.97%$–$8.46%$ over the untuned generator. The rendered clips also score higher under VideoReward-Overall, and their extracted frames under HPSv3 and UniPercept. GAL yields consistent motion gains across all four I2V models, as measured by a $5.62%$–$38.24%$ increase in VBench dynamic degree. Although GAL is trained only on HunyuanVideo-1.5 renders, these gains extend to the other three I2V models.

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on real-world driving logs, which record only the trajectory actually executed in each scene. This provides limited supervision for learning alternative valid behaviors. Multi-target supervision can mitigate this limitation, but broader trajectory coverage makes safe candidate selection more challenging. We propose FPD-Drive, an end-to-end planner that distills privileged future knowledge into a visual driving policy to improve both multi-trajectory supervision and safety selection. Specifically, we introduce Future-Privileged Multi-Target Distillation to transfer diverse, high-quality trajectory targets from a teacher with access to recorded future observations to the student. These targets extend supervision beyond the single recorded human trajectory. For candidate selection, we further introduce Safety-Aware Score Correction to correct ranking errors between nearby safe and unsafe candidates and raise underestimated safety scores of safe candidates. At deployment, the student requires neither future observations nor teacher inference. Our base model achieves 94.9 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest and 55.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navhard. The scaled FPD-Drive-Pro improves these scores to 95.5 PDMS and 58.1 EPDMS, respectively, achieving SOTA performance among the compared methods on both benchmarks. Abstract: The unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based maritime surveillance is becoming an important part of maritime Internet of Things (IoT) and intelligent transportation systems. It provides a flexible and cost-effective way to large-scale maritime traffic monitoring. However, achieving robust situational awareness remains challenging in practical maritime environments. The main challenges arise from heterogeneous sensing modalities, adverse imaging conditions, and spatial misalignment caused by UAV motion. To address these problems, this paper proposes an airborne multi-modal graph learning-driven framework, termed AMGT, for vessel identification and tracking in UAV-assisted maritime IoT. Specifically, visible and infrared images are first aligned and fused through a multi-parameter perspective transformation model. Abstract: Visual grounding combines image–language understanding with geometric prediction, yet autoregressive multimodal models often express a box as a coordinate sequence. Sequential decoding adds computation to the multimodal processing needed for localization. Adapting these representations to box geometry offers a route to direct prediction. We present Jag, which adapts a compact pretrained multimodal model for direct continuous box prediction. A lightweight regression head reads the last valid input-token state, conditioned on the image and referring expression, and predicts all four box coordinates together. Geometric supervision jointly adapts the language backbone, visual merger, and regression head, allowing localization losses to shape both the multimodal representations and the coordinate mapping. Inference retains image–language processing and returns the box in one forward pass, without coordinate generation or text parsing. Across benchmarks, Jag consistently improves grounding accuracy over its base model across the observed target sizes and expression groups. Component ablation show that jointly adapting the representations and head substantially improves accuracy over training the head alone. Against existing specialized grounding models, it achieves competitive accuracy with lower single-request latency and GPU memory use. These results show how supervision tailored to the geometric answer can specialize a compact pretrained multimodal model for accurate grounding and efficient direct prediction. Abstract: Can a vision model learn what words mean from pixels alone? We show that the two largest DINOv3 vision transformers organize words rendered in images by word meaning, despite being pretrained using self-supervision on images only, without paired captions, class labels, or other language supervision. We derive word representations from embedding differences between word images and matched controls, then test whether semantically related words are neighbors within each model’s embedding space, while controlling for spelling similarity. We find that brand names in the same industry group together, as do nouns from the same semantic domain. Country-capital pairs exhibit consistent vector offsets, similarly to word embeddings trained on text. These word representations also align with the representations of the visual object categories they name. DINOv3-H and DINOv3-7B are, to our knowledge, the first self-supervised vision models to show substantial semantic structure for rendered words. Comparisons with text and speech encoders reveal shared structure among representations of the same words. Contrastive alignment with either encoder further strengthened DINOv3-H’s word-level semantic structure, while leaving the other finetuned vision models largely unchanged. Image-only training can therefore teach vision models not just what words look like, but also how their meanings relate.

Abstract: Person re-identification is typically addressed by learning identity-discriminative embeddings from individual person crops. Existing crop-based approaches largely ignore this contextual information, which can be critical for resolving visually similar identities. Here, we propose ContextReID, a contextualized embedding framework that represents a player by jointly considering other players in the same frame and observations from different frames of the broadcast sequence. By incorporating both co-occurring players and cross-frame observations, the model learns identity features relative to the surrounding visual context rather than relying only on isolated appearance cues. We implement this framework with a vision-language model that takes sets of player crops as input and generates context-aware embeddings for individual players. Experiments across three multi-object tracking benchmarks show that ContextReID improves player retrieval over conventional single-crop embedding approaches in both Rank-1 and mAP. These results highlight the utility of spatial and temporal context for reliable player identification in broadcast sports videos.

[49] SheafPrune: Sheaf-Guided Graph Propagation for Visual Token Pruning cs.CVPDF

Xiaoyang Ma, Chi Wang, Hong Sun

TL;DR: SheafPrune 用模态特定映射和稀疏文本—视觉图传播相关性，再在视觉图上做度数校正和 top-k 保留；LLaVA-NeXT 保留 10% token 时 prefill 净加速 5.15×。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型处理完整视觉 token 序列会增加 prefill 开销，许多 token 与指令无关。单 token 显著性难以表达 token 之间的依赖；图选择的效果则取决于关系如何传播重要性及节点度数如何影响排序。

Result: SheafPrune 采用受 sheaf 几何启发的支持约束传播：先用模态特定映射和稀疏文本—视觉图传递相关性，再在独立视觉图上继续传播并按节点度数校正。剪枝器在冻结语言模型前一次性保留硬 top-k 子集，约 4.5M 参数，通过冻结 LLaVA-NeXT-8B 主干上的语言建模目标端到端训练。显式条件化保留率使单个检查点可覆盖不同预算。四个主干、十个基准和七种剪枝率的统一基线重实现评测中，SheafPrune 在 28 个“主干—预算”组合里的 22 个保持效果最佳；中度到激进剪枝时优势随压缩加大。在 LLaVA-NeXT 上保留 10% 视觉 token，prefill 净加速 5.15×，端到端 FLOPs 减少 84.0%。

Insight: 图关系传播和度数校正共同避免重要性仅由局部显著性或高连接度决定，并可用单一模型适配不同 token 预算。22/28 的领先统计与特定统一评测协议有关；10% token 结果专指 LLaVA-NeXT。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models process the complete visual token sequence, increasing prefill cost even when many tokens are irrelevant to the instruction. Token-wise saliency captures limited inter-token dependence; graph-based selection instead depends on how relations govern importance propagation and ranking. We study support-constrained propagation with node-degree correction. Inspired by sheaf geometry, SheafPrune uses modality-specific maps and a sparse text–visual graph to transfer relevance, then propagates scores on a separate visual graph and corrects them for degree. It retains a hard top-$k$ subset once before the frozen language model. The selector has about $4.5$M parameters and is trained end-to-end through the frozen LLaVA-NeXT-8B backbone using only the language-modeling objective. Explicit retention-ratio conditioning lets one checkpoint serve every budget. Across four backbones, ten benchmarks and seven pruning ratios, with all baselines re-implemented under one protocol, SheafPrune achieves the best retention at $22$ of $28$ backbone–budget settings, with monotonically growing margins in the moderate-to-aggressive regime. On LLaVA-NeXT, retaining $10%$ of visual tokens yields a $5.15\times$ net prefill speedup and an $84.0%$ end-to-end FLOPs reduction. Together with component ablations, these results support graph-constrained importance propagation and degree correction as a design for visual token selection across the evaluated backbones and tokenization schemes, improving the trade-off between accuracy retention and inference cost. code:https://github.com/Bob0817912/SheafPrune_ICLR_2027

[50] CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation cs.CV | cs.LG | cs.MMPDF

Haowei Wang, Hao Lin, Qi Zhu, Jiayi Ji, Deyi Ji

TL;DR: CV-OPD 通过正负图像裁剪下的教师分布对比，补足 on-policy 蒸馏缺少视觉约束的问题，减少学生只凭答案提示走捷径；多个细粒度视觉基准上视觉推理能力得到改善。

Details

Motivation: On-policy distillation 能从教师输出为 VLM 学生提供密集监督，但作者发现学生即使相关图像区域被遮挡或加噪仍能答对，说明它可能没有真正利用视觉证据。理论分析将此归因于蒸馏中视觉约束不足，使学生能够依赖答案提示等捷径。

Result: Contrastive Vision OPD（CV-OPD）在相同学生前缀下比较正向和负向图像裁剪诱导的教师分布，并添加间隔式对比约束。方法引入与答案无关的负教师及 Contrastive Divergence Loss，以正负教师对比约束学生特征分布；再用 Discrepancy Aware Gate 根据 token 级和样本级差异选择与视觉相关的 token 参与优化，从而校正学生对视觉区域的利用。多个细粒度视觉基准实验显示，CV-OPD 能显著缓解捷径行为，并持续提升现有 VLM 的视觉推理能力；摘要未给出具体分数。

Insight: 只匹配答案输出分布不保证学生建立视觉 grounding；用正负裁剪产生的教师差异提供显式视觉约束，可让训练信号指向证据相关 token。摘要报告多基准定性收益，但未说明增益幅度或模型/数据集列表。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise. Building on this observation, our quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that OPD, which uses the answer as a hint, has failed to teach students to genuinely seek out and rely on visual evidence. Our theoretical analysis suggests that this failure arises from a shortcut enabled by the lack of visual constraints during distillation. To address the issue, we introduce Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD), which compares teacher distributions induced by positive and negative crops while conditioning on the same student prefix and augments optimization with a margin-based contrastive constraint, which is modulated with discrepancy gating across token and sample level. Specifically, CV-OPD first introduces a negative teacher unrelated to the answer and designs a Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL) that constrains the feature distribution of student by contrasting the positive and negative teachers, correcting how the student exploits visual regions. The CV-OPD introduces a Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG) that uses token-level and sample-level discrepancies to select visually relevant tokens for optimization, directing the student’s attention toward visual content. Extensive experiments across a broad range of fine-grained visual benchmarks demonstrate that CV-OPD substantially alleviates shortcut and consistently improves the visual reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs.

[51] OWLET: On-Policy Weighted Learning via Evidence Transfer for Video Large Language Model Post-Training cs.CVPDF

Abstract: Post-training has become a key stage for eliciting reasoning capabilities in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing post-training methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, they do not fully exploit visual evidence for effective training. RL supervises the policy with a single trajectory-level reward, which is too coarse-grained for video reasoning: the same response-level advantage is applied to every generated token, disregarding that the tokens of a video response are grounded in the visual stream to very different degrees. OPD provides token-level supervision through output-distribution matching, but lacks direct supervision of the spatiotemporal representations that produce those outputs. We observe across extensive experiments that the same model reasons more accurately when its video view combines global temporal context with question-relevant details. Comparing the same student-generated response under this view and uniform sampling reveals changes in token likelihoods and internal representations, providing a training signal for evidence use. Building on this observation, we propose OWLET, an evidence-privileged RL method that derives training signals from a richer teacher view. This view couples uniform global context with question-conditioned local windows, providing visual evidence without requiring a larger model. To better exploit this privileged visual evidence, OWLET introduces (i) \textbf{evidence-aware credit redistribution}, which compares token likelihoods across the two views and redistributes positive response-level advantages according to their relative evidence support; and (ii) \textbf{spatiotemporal state alignment}, which aligns the two views’ hidden states on verifier-accepted responses.

Insight: 全局 register 特征为已有空间 patch 表示补充场景外观信息，可同时改善重建与从固定采样 patch 进行的生成。量化结果限定于 ImageNet-1K、ViT-B 和指定 DINO 编码器/基线。

Abstract: Pretrained vision transformers learn structured image representations spanning spatial patch tokens and non-spatial tokens. Recent analyses suggest that non-spatial register tokens encode global scene properties such as illumination, blur, and style. Yet patch-based image decoders discard these outputs, leaving the decoder to recover image appearance from patches alone. We investigate whether retaining register features improves decoding when the complete spatial patch representation is already available. We study this question using representation autoencoders (RAEs) with frozen register-equipped encoders. Our decoder processes four register tokens together with all patch tokens through joint self-attention. Appearance probes, paired reconstruction-error analysis, and fixed-decoder register substitutions examine which global image properties are accessible from registers and how their values affect reconstructed pixels. For generation, we train a multi-head attention pooling (MHAP) predictor to recover register features from sampled patches, keeping the pretrained diffusion model and its samples unchanged. On ImageNet-1K, our ViT-B decoders achieve mean reconstruction FIDs of 0.472 with DINOv2 and 0.442 with DINOv3, reductions of 9.5% and 5.2% relative to their respective patch-only baselines.
