Abstract: Modeling human reactions to spatial audio requires understanding both what is heard and where it comes from. Prior work generates motion offline from complete acoustic and spatial input sequences. Humans, however, react to sounds without knowing what they will hear next. We present Sonic-AMG, to our knowledge the first framework for streaming human motion generation from spatial audio. The model encodes acoustic content and body-relative source geometry as separate tokens. Conditioned on these tokens, a causal transformer with a flow head generates continuous motion latents, from which a causal decoder reconstructs motion segments. Standard teacher-forced training derives motion histories and body-relative source locations from ground-truth motion, whereas streaming inference relies on generated motion for both, resulting in a training-inference mismatch. To reduce this history discrepancy, we introduce rehearsal training, which combines full-sequence teacher-forced supervision with supervised prediction along short autoregressive self-rollouts. Abstract: Image-to-video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single reference image into high-quality, subject-consistent videos, posing threats of misuse as anyone can turn personal photos into fabricated videos without consent. However, due to an insufficient understanding of what governs how subsequent frames read the reference image in modern I2V models, existing protections struggle to offer strong protection under common perturbation budgets. In this paper, we approach the problem by exploring this underlying reading mechanism. Through a convex geometric analysis, we theoretically identify the spread of the reference value vectors as a key factor governing this reading process, and empirically find that even a mild contraction of this spread can severely degrade the generated video, making it an efficient lever for protection. Guided by this insight, we propose LETHE, a novel protection method that directly disrupts this reading process. Abstract: Drag-based image editing provides an intuitive interface for spatially controlled image manipulation by allowing users to specify handle and target points. [58] VideoAWR: Reinforcing Video Flow Models with Decoupled-Timestep Advantage-Weighted Rectification cs.CVPDF

Zheming Fu, Wenbin Ning, Ruizhe He, Wei Shang, Xiaoxiao Ma

TL;DR: VideoAWR 用 EMA 策略生成受 CFG 引导的高保真 rollout，同时以无 CFG 条件分布作为优化锚点，避免视频流模型的冷启动退化和引导爆炸；Wan2.1 1.3B/14B 实验显示视觉与运动质量改善。

Details

Motivation: 视频流模型的回归式 RL 通常要求无 CFG 采样，使回归目标匹配参考边际速度，但无引导 rollout 可能落入低保真区域，组内奖励差距不再代表感知质量。若改用 CFG rollout 又把目标绑定在引导分布上，EMA 更新可能导致引导几何放大。

Result: VideoAWR 将采样分布与优化锚点解耦：EMA 参考策略使用 CFG 产生高保真 rollout，训练策略则对无 CFG 条件锚点做优势加权修正。论文证明该目标数学上精确：中心化优势在期望中抵消锚点，过滤掉与奖励无关的引导偏移，仅保留奖励相关协方差增量。因此训练后单分支无 CFG 推理仍可保持测试时 CFG 原有语义。另提出 Decoupled Timestep Rectification，每条 rollout 独立采样监督时间步以提升样本效率且不引入偏差。Wan2.1-1.3B 和 14B 实验报告优化稳定，并在启用和不启用 CFG 两种设置下提升视觉及运动质量。

Insight: 引导用于获得可区分奖励的高保真训练样本，而优化目标仍锚定无引导条件分布，从而化解采样质量与目标分布之间的冲突。摘要未提供 FID、运动指标或效率数字；理论保证针对文中解耦目标。

Abstract: Regression-based RL for flow models mandates sampling \emph{without} classifier-free guidance (CFG) to align the rectification target with the reference marginal velocity. Extending Advantage-Weighted Rectification (AWR), which dynamically scales this regression target based on reward, to video generation encounters a cold-start pathology: unguided rollouts collapse into low-fidelity regions where intra-group reward gaps remain wide but no longer track perceptual quality, rendering the optimization target arbitrary. Conversely, utilizing guided rollouts to restore fidelity, while keeping the regression target tied to the guided sampling distribution, provably triggers a geometric guidance blow-up under exponential-moving-average (EMA) updates. We propose \textbf{VideoAWR}, which resolves this tension by untying sampling from the optimization anchor. An EMA reference policy generates \emph{guided} rollouts to restore reward separability, while the trainable policy is rectified against the \emph{unguided} conditional anchor. We prove this decoupled objective is mathematically exact: the centered advantage cancels the anchor in expectation, filtering out the reward-neutral guidance offset and retaining only the reward-correlated covariance increment. Consequently, inference runs single-branch and CFG-free, yet inference-time CFG preserves its original semantics. Furthermore, we introduce \emph{Decoupled Timestep Rectification}, supervising each rollout at an independently sampled timestep without bias to improve sample efficiency. Experiments on Wan2.1-1.3B and 14B demonstrate stable optimization and superior visual and motion quality in \emph{both} CFG-free and CFG-enabled regimes. Code is available at \pdfstartlink attr{/Border [0 0 0]} user{/Subtype /Link /A << /S /URI /URI (https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR) >>}% \textcolor{pearDark}{VideoAWR}\pdfendlink.

[59] CoVA: Composing VAE Representations for Generative Learning cs.CVPDF

Zhaowei Chen, Yikai Ge, Linze Li, Yao Tang, Xiang Li

TL;DR: CoVA 在线组合 VAE 特征形成语义和空间结构引导，并校正软标签嵌入平均造成的条件强度混淆；ImageNet 256² 上 SiT-XL/2 的 FID 从 17.2 降至 7.4。

Motivation: 预训练视觉编码器表示能改善扩散 Transformer 训练，但依赖额外编码器并增加训练成本。直接使用生成模型 VAE 特征更轻量，却因面向重建而缺少生成学习所需的语义区分性和空间结构。

Insight: 除了引入辅助表征，软标签条件的范数变化本身也会影响 FID，因此必须分离语义方向与条件强度。CoVA 报告在 ImageNet 256² 的显著质量/训练效率收益，8.5M 参数只在训练时使用。

Abstract: Representation alignment with pretrained visual encoders improves diffusion transformer training but requires auxiliary encoders and extra training overhead. Recent approaches instead exploit representations from the generative model’s own variational autoencoder (VAE). However, reconstruction-oriented VAE features are not optimized for the semantic discrimination and spatial structure needed for generative learning. We introduce \textbf{CoVA} (\textbf{Co}mposed \textbf{VA}E Representations), a lightweight framework that composes VAE features online into stage-wise guidance for flow-matching training. A small latent composer is jointly trained with class supervision, masked latent reconstruction, and horizontal-flip consistency, yielding semantically discriminative, spatially structured dense features and sample-dependent class posteriors. CoVA aligns intermediate SiT features with the composed representations early in training, then removes the increasingly detrimental alignment and reuses detached composer posteriors for sample-dependent soft label conditioning. We further uncover a hidden confound in soft label conditioning: averaging class embeddings attenuates their magnitude, coupling semantic interpolation with conditioning strength. This attenuation alone can substantially improve FID while degrading Recall, revealing a non-semantic source of the apparent gain. CoVA corrects this confound by restoring the hard-label magnitude, isolating posterior-dependent directional semantics. CoVA adds only 8.5M training-only auxiliary parameters (1.3% of SiT-XL/2) and introduces no inference-time overhead. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, CoVA reduces SiT-XL/2 FID from 17.2 to 7.4 (\textbf{57%}) in 400K steps. With classifier-free guidance, it achieves an FID of \textbf{2.03} in only 400 epochs, versus 2.06 after 1,400 epochs for vanilla SiT-XL/2.

[60] Why DiT Models Underperform as Representation Learners without Long Skip Connections cs.CVPDF

Benyuan Meng, Yiliang Zhang, Jin-Wen Wu, Qianqian Xu, Qingming Huang

TL;DR: 受控从头训练表明 DiT 的长跳连可改善扩散特征质量：它把扩散噪声信息直接传到输出端，减轻主干图像表示被噪声污染，从而提升生成与理解统一所需的特征。

Details

Motivation: 扩散模型中间特征可用于统一图像生成与判别理解，但已有研究发现 DiT 虽生成能力强，表征质量却偏弱。作者考察长跳连是否影响该问题，以及其作用机制是否与扩散训练目标相关。

Result: 通过受控从头训练，作者发现带长跳连（LSC）的 DiT 能产生更好的视觉特征。机制分析指出，扩散主干输入含潜变量图像和噪声；噪声变量实际编码扩散轨迹信息，而预测 v 或 ε 需要同时使用这两类信号。若两者都通过主干传播，噪声会污染图像信号；LSC 可将扩散噪声直接传向需要它的输出位置，降低主干中噪声信号强度，改善特征质量。作者据此认为 LSC 并非纯粹技巧，而与扩散目标结构相关。

Insight: 提升生成目标优化与获得高质量理解表征之间存在架构层面的张力，长跳连通过隔离扩散轨迹信号帮助保留图像语义特征。摘要没有给具体表征基准、模型规模或提升数值，结论主要来自机制分析与受控训练。

Abstract: Diffusion feature, i.e., intermediate activations extracted from diffusion model backbones, has emerged as a promising approach for unifying generation and vision understanding (discrimination). Despite the advancement of diffusion backbones into Diffusion Transformers (DiT), it has been found in previous literature that DiT models tend to yield weaker features despite their stronger generation capability. This poses a threat to good generation-understanding unification. In this work, we show via controlled from-scratch training that long skip connections (LSCs) affect feature quality: a DiT with LSCs can produce better features. Furthermore, through detailed diagnosis, we reveal the mechanism causing this effect. Specifically, the input to a diffusion backbone contains both the latent image and the diffusion noise, where the diffusion noise, though appearing in the form of noises, actually reflects the information of the diffusion trajectory. To make the final prediction of a noisy variable ($v$ or $ε$), the diffusion backbone needs both signals. However, carrying both signals through the main backbone effectively “pollutes” the image signal with noises, leading to poor feature quality. In comparison, LSCs can shortcut diffusion noises directly to where they are needed, leaving much weaker noise signals in the main backbone, thus improving feature quality. To name the broader impact, this reveals that LSCs are not a mere trick but closely related to the diffusion objective. The code is at https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-lsc.

Insight: 基准关注文字目标与 OCR 检测区域之间的时空对应关系，不把 OCR 单次识别率等同于视频中的文字生成和保持能力。摘要未明确最强 Ref2V 模型，且多文字区域导致退化的趋势应结合具体分组结果解读。

Abstract: Modern video generators can synthesize convincing scenes while rendering a few characters incorrectly, omitting requested text, or changing it across frames. Measuring these failures is harder than applying OCR independently to each frame: when a scene contains multiple text targets and multiple noisy OCR regions, the evaluator must first determine which observation belongs to which intended text. We introduce TextVBench, a benchmark and evaluation framework that formulates visual-text evaluation as a multi-target, multi-instance spatiotemporal correspondence problem. TextVBench contains two complementary tracks: TextVBench-T2V measures the creation and maintenance of prompt-specified text, while TextVBench-Ref2V measures preservation of text from reference images. Both tracks use target-conditioned candidate selection rather than unconstrained VLM transcription, support one target mapping to multiple OCR regions, and separate frame-level spatial fidelity from cross-frame temporal fidelity. A length-weighted harmonic score summarizes these factors, while extra-text diagnostics expose failure modes hidden by a single OCR score. The benchmark is organized around controlled text, layout, carrier, and motion attributes so that aggregate scores can be traced back to specific capability limits. Across the evaluated model runs, MiniMax-H3 reaches a T2V TextVScore of 68.32, while the strongest Ref2V score is 56.51; attribute slices reveal a pronounced drop as the number of simultaneous reference text regions increases.

[62] When MLLMs miss what humans read: diagnosing and learning to read squint text cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Chen Peng, Shan Huang, Jianbo Huang, Linrui Qin, Qibo Sun

Motivation: 多模态大模型可能无法读出人类眯眼仍能辨认的、融入图像粗结构中的文字，甚至回答图中没有文字。该问题来自实际内容审核案例；作者希望区分视觉证据本身不足与模型空间选择/文字识别不足。

Result: 作者将此类文字命名为 Squint Text，构建含 1,076 张生产审核图片的 SquintBench 和用于机制诊断的合成控制集 SquintCtrl。冻结权重分析发现，隐藏文字保留粗粒度信号，但字形身份表征较弱；提供正确空间区域后，字符身份显著更易读，说明空间选取是主要瓶颈。固定视觉 token 预算时抑制高频内容也有助于阅读，token 数量可能同样影响。Squint OPSD 让冻结教师获得缩小图像视图及真实转录作为特权上下文，以 token 级分布监督学生的 on-policy 轨迹；推理时学生只看原始图像。方法在 SquintBench 达到 88.61 ANLS，未适配主干为 26.50，原视图最佳通用模型为 24.44，现成模型在缩小视图上最高为 68.60。

Insight: 视觉证据在图中存在不代表模型能够正确选择对应区域并读出文字；特权视图和转录可用于训练，但不需在推理时增加辅助图像。提升针对 SquintBench 的 ANLS，摘要未说明独立外部数据集表现。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs can fail to read text embedded in an image’s coarse structure (text a human recovers by squinting) and report that no text is present. We name this class Squint Text and study it as a recognition deficiency, motivated by cases from a production moderation pipeline. We introduce SquintBench, comprising 1076 production-derived moderation images, and SquintCtrl, a controlled synthetic suite for mechanism diagnosis. Frozen-weight analysis shows that hidden text leaves a coarse signal while glyph identity is weakly represented; identity becomes substantially more accessible when the correct spatial regions are supplied, implicating spatial selection as a major bottleneck. Suppressing high-frequency content also improves reading at a fixed visual-token budget, while visual token count may also contribute. These findings motivate Squint OPSD: a frozen teacher receives a downscaled view and the ground-truth transcription as privileged context, and its token-level distributions supervise a student along on-policy trajectories. At inference, the student reads the original image alone. Squint OPSD reaches 88.61 ANLS on SquintBench, versus 26.50 for the unadapted backbone, 24.44 for the best general-purpose model on the original view, and 68.60 at best for an off-the-shelf model under a downscaled view. These results substantially narrow the gap between available visual evidence and its generated read-out without auxiliary views at inference.

[63] GraftOOD: Grafting Out-of-Distribution Exemplars into Advantage-Weighted Rectification for Video Reward Optimization cs.CVPDF

Zheming Fu, Ruizhe He, Wenbin Ning, Xiaoxiao Ma, Xuan Yang

TL;DR: GraftOOD 将格式匹配的分布外视频示例混入部分在线 rollout，以序列级示例监督补足奖励优化并缓解运动塌缩；Wan2.1-1.3B 的 Dynamic Degree 从 4.86 升至 44.41，但 HPSv3 从 8.10 降至 7.32。

Details

Motivation: 视频奖励优化可能提高代理奖励却降低视频运动动态，作者称为 motion collapse；即使采用视频级运动质量奖励也可能发生。单一标量奖励难以稳定兼顾动态性和视觉质量。

Result: GraftOOD 在选定 prompt 组中用分布外视频示例替换部分在线 rollout。共享速度回归目标结合组内相对奖励修正与序列级示例监督，不需要示例生成轨迹，也无需额外监督损失系数；论文还将最优场刻画为两个来源目标场按来源后验加权的组合。作者构建 GraftSet-Video，含 2,397 个提示对应的 9,588 个格式匹配示例。在 Wan2.1-1.3B 上，相对仅奖励 FlowAWR，Dynamic Degree 从 4.86 提升到 44.41，HPSv3 从 8.10 降至 7.32，VisionReward 则接近不变。主设置下，三种示例增强方法的十项指标中 GraftOOD 领先九项。示例频率和组内数量消融显示运动—奖励之间存在权衡，增加示例并非所有指标都改善。

Insight: 外部分布视频示例能补充标量奖励缺少的序列级运动监督，但动态度提高不等价于运动更连贯或视觉更可信；质量指标同时出现退化。应把结果理解为特定模型上的多目标折衷，而非所有维度统一提升。

Abstract: Video reward optimization can increase proxy rewards while reducing video dynamics, a mismatch we identify as \emph{motion collapse}, including under a video-level motion-quality reward. We propose \textbf{GraftOOD}, which replaces part of the online rollouts in selected prompt groups with out-of-distribution video exemplars. A shared velocity-regression objective combines group-relative reward rectification with sequence-level exemplar supervision, requiring neither exemplar generation trajectories nor an additional supervised-loss coefficient. We characterize its pointwise optimal field as a source-posterior-weighted combination of the two source-specific target fields. We construct \textbf{GraftSet-Video}, containing 9,588 format-matched exemplars for 2,397 prompts, to supply external supervision. On Wan2.1-1.3B, GraftOOD raises Dynamic Degree from 4.86 to 44.41 relative to reward-only FlowAWR, while HPSv3 decreases from 8.10 to 7.32 and VisionReward remains close. At the main setting, GraftOOD leads nine of ten metrics among three exemplar-augmented methods. Ablations of exemplar frequency and within-group count reveal dynamics–reward trade-offs rather than uniform gains from increasing exemplar usage. These results support external exemplar supervision as a complement to scalar reward feedback, without equating higher measured dynamics with coherent, visually plausible motion in the generated videos. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR}{GraftOOD}.

[64] S-Space: Exploring Spatial Workspace in Multimodal Language Models cs.CV | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Zhiyuan Shi, Xiang Li, Xingzhuo Guo, Shangchen Miao, Weiliang Chen

TL;DR: S-Space 发现 MLLM 对象 token 中存在可读出水平、垂直和距离坐标的线性子空间，并显示视角变化推理的主要瓶颈是空间变换能力，而非空间表征完全缺失。

Details

Motivation: 多模态语言模型在空间推理上表现有潜力，但支撑能力的内部机制不清楚；需要识别模型是否形成内部空间工作区，以及感知、变换与语言回答如何利用它。

Result: 研究提出 S-Space：对象 token 表示中的线性子空间，编码连续水平、垂直和距离坐标。研究通过空间答案得分对对象 token 激活的梯度识别坐标轴，并发现该子空间并非由特定数据或提示诱导，非空间问题下仍可读出。作者将其视作内部空间工作区：感知阶段把物体位置写入其中，读出支持空间判断，视角变化时内部推理会对其进行变换；因果干预可改变空间判断，同时大体保留非空间回答。案例还显示 S-Space 会为未见实体和观察者分配位置，并连接物理方向与抽象语言含义。诊断发现方位词读出会系统混合基数方向与图像相对参照系；感知—计算拆分显示，模型可形成有用空间表示，但跨视角可靠推理所需的空间变换仍是瓶颈。

Insight: 表征探针和因果干预共同支持模型内部确有可操作的坐标工作区；错误更多可能发生在变换阶段。参照系混淆揭示这种能力也有系统性边界，不能仅靠发现线性坐标子空间就推断空间推理普遍可靠。

Abstract: Multimodal language models (MLLMs) exhibit promising spatial reasoning capabilities, yet the internal mechanisms supporting them remain poorly understood. We uncover S-Space, a linear representation subspace in which object-token representations encode continuous coordinates along horizontal, vertical, and distance axes. Although we identify these axes by differentiating spatial-answer scores with respect to object-token activations, S-Space is intrinsic rather than induced by specific data or prompts, remaining readable under non-spatial questions. We show that S-Space functions as an internal spatial workspace of MLLMs. Perception encodes object locations into it, forming spatial representations that can be directly read out to support accurate spatial judgments. Internal reasoning transforms it for changing viewpoints. Verbal responses selectively access it, evidenced by causal interventions that alter spatial judgments while largely preserving non-spatial responses. Case studies further show that S-Space assigns locations to unseen entities and the observer, and connects physical directions with abstract linguistic meanings, suggesting both the capabilities and limitations of large-scale language training. Finally, we use S-Space as a diagnostic tool for spatial reasoning failures. Direction-word readouts reveal systematic coupling between cardinal and image-relative reference frames. More importantly, a perception-computation decomposition exposes a key bottleneck: MLLMs can form capable spatial representations, yet fall short in the spatial transformations required to reason reliably across viewpoint changes.

[65] Stride: Scale-Aware Multi-Agent Evolutionary Refinement for Infographic Generation cs.CVPDF

Yan Zhou, Yang Yang

TL;DR: Stride 将信息图要求满足度评估与错误位置定位分开，再按全局/局部尺度分派给构图和修图子智能体，并只接受验证分数提升的候选；在多项基准上开源系统匹敌或超过专有模型。

Details

Motivation: 信息图单张图片需要同时满足视觉、文字和语义约束。局部编辑难以修复整体构图问题，整图重生成又可能破坏已经正确的区域；随机生成器和不完美验证器还使单步迭代不可靠。

Result: Stride 将冻结生成器上的迭代优化建模为具历史的演化策略。它分离约束评估（是否满足要求）与视觉定位（问题在哪里、如何违反），再将全局修改路由给 composer 子智能体、局部修正路由给 retoucher。两个子智能体各自规划并生成多个候选，Stride 仅当候选提升验证器分数时才接受，以减少迭代回归。在完全开源的生成器—验证器系统上，Stride 在 BizGenEval 超过 Nano-Banana-2.0 和 Seedream-5.0-Pro；在 IGenBench 匹配 GPT-Image-2.0 并超过 Seedream-5.0-Pro；在 WISE 达到新的 SOTA，并在所有评测基准上明显超过既有智能体方法。

Insight: 尺度感知路由、多候选冗余和单调接受共同应对全局/局部编辑尺度错配与随机迭代退化。各基准上的比较对象不同，摘要未给具体分数；结论也依赖其验证器评分和开源生成器设置。

Abstract: Infographic generation challenges text-to-image models because a single image must satisfy numerous visual, textual, and semantic constraints. Iterative agentic refinement with a multimodal verifier is a natural remedy, but existing methods face a scale mismatch: local edits cannot correct global composition errors, while full regeneration may destroy already-correct regions. We introduce Stride, a constraint-grounded multi-agent framework that treats refinement over any frozen generator as a history-aware evolution strategy. Stride separates constraint evaluation - whether each requirement is satisfied - from visual grounding - where and how it is violated. It then routes global revisions to a composer subagent and local corrections to a retoucher subagent. Each subagent conducts internal planning and generates multiple candidates, while Stride accepts a candidate only when it improves the verifier score, reducing regression across iterations. Because stochastic generators and imperfect verifiers make any single step unreliable, it is this combination of scale-aware routing, per-step redundancy, and monotonic acceptance that yields stable improvement across iterations. With fully open-weight generator-verifier systems, Stride outperforms proprietary systems including Nano-Banana-2.0 and Seedream-5.0-Pro on BizGenEval, matches GPT-Image-2.0 and surpasses Seedream-5.0-Pro on IGenBench, and achieves a new state-of-the-art score on WISE, while substantially outperforming existing agentic methods across all evaluated benchmarks.

Motivation: 开放集遥感视觉定位不仅要定位目标，还须判断图像是否包含足够证据。闭集定位模型对不存在目标也常输出看似合理的框，把定位置信与真实视觉支持混淆。

Result: AnchorProbe-Distill（APD）以教师—学生框架学习 Visual Support。教师 APD-T 把原始查询作为参考锚点，解释查询扰动产生的定位变化：语义保持查询应仍指向标注目标；近类别歧义查询根据是否形成可靠替代锚点或坍缩到参考锚点来判定；远类别反事实查询提供拒绝监督。随后将这种参考感知推理蒸馏到学生 APD-S，使模型只凭一张图和一个查询即可联合定位或拒识。实验显示，模型能够拒绝图像中不存在的目标，同时也提高对存在目标的定位能力；摘要未报告具体指标。

Insight: 用扰动查询下锚点是否稳定作为视觉支持证据，将拒答能力与坐标回归放入统一定位任务中。当前摘要只给定性实验结果，未说明遥感数据集、拒识阈值或定位提升数值。

Abstract: Open-set visual grounding in remote sensing requires models to determine not only where a queried object is located, but also whether the image provides sufficient evidence for the query at all. Existing closed-set grounding models, however, tend to return plausible boxes even for absent objects, blurring localization confidence with genuine Visual Support. We introduce AnchorProbe-Distill (APD), a teacher-student framework that recasts open-set grounding as Visual Support learning. APD builds on the insight that supported queries should induce coherent spatial anchors, whereas unsupported or mismatched queries tend to produce unstable or misleading localization patterns. APD-T therefore uses the Original Query as a Reference Anchor to interpret query perturbations: Semantic-Preserving Queries retain the annotated target, Near-Category Ambiguous Queries are resolved according to whether they form a reliable alternative anchor or collapse onto the Reference Anchor, and Far-Category Counterfactual Queries provide rejection supervision. This reference-aware reasoning is distilled into APD-S, enabling unified localization and rejection from a single image–query pair. Experiments show that APD enables the resulting model to reject absent targets while also improving its localization of present targets.

Details

Motivation: VLM 每次评测都会生成逐题预测、判定和元数据，但排行榜通常只保留汇总分数并丢弃这些产物。利用已存运行结果可审计基准是否真正依赖图像，而无需重新开展完整评测。

Insight: 逐题旧产物可以揭示“高基准分但不看图”的潜在问题，并用模型池、置换基线和校准分析避免把原始零增益直接归因于作弊。3.2 个百分点为匹配零模型后的超额；知识替代视觉只是与类别分布一致的解释，尚未由逐题审查确认。

Abstract: Every evaluation run of a vision-language model (VLM) already produces per-question artifacts (predictions, judged correctness, metadata) that leaderboards reduce to a single score and then discard. We reuse those artifacts to audit the benchmark itself, and add an audit layer to VLMEvalKit: six detectors that run as post-processing over saved outputs, plus one additional text-only rerun with visual inputs removed, emitting a per-question report. The layer reports per-question findings together with aggregate audit statistics; for the headline quantities, it attaches permutation-null excess where applicable, interval estimates for proportions, and sensitivity to the model pool. Auditing MMStar, a benchmark curated specifically so that the image is indispensable, with three models, we find zero aggregate accuracy gain from the image on 20.2% of its questions. An item-matched permutation null accounts for 17.0%, leaving a +3.2 pp excess (corrected permutation p = 0.001). This excess is concentrated in the mathematics and science categories and is absent from coarse perception, a localization consistent with knowledge substituting for visual evidence; confirming that mechanism would require item-level review. A second detector flags 134 questions on which every model that answered in parseable form picked the same non-gold option, and these candidates peak in the perception categories, whereas the zero-gain excess is largest in mathematics and science & technology. Replacing the LLM judge with exact string matching changes no conclusion, and every scoring discrepancy between the two traces to one model whose free-form answers a parser cannot read. On a binary yes/no benchmark the detectors measurably fall out of calibration, a scope boundary that we measure and report. The audit layer, the prediction artifacts and the generated reports accompany this submission.

To mitigate this, we present ELMA, an episodic long-term motion dataset with dense segment-level annotations that preserve cross-segment dependencies. Built via a fully automated collection and filtering pipeline, ELMA extracts high-quality, continuous 30-second clips from in-the-wild videos, capturing the episodic narratives necessary for long-range state modeling. Abstract: In grounded remote sensing visual question answering, a model produces a textual answer and identifies relevant objects in the image as grounding evidence. AESP applies task-specific rules to existence, counting, and comparison questions. Motivation: 近期物理 3D 生成能产生外观逼真且可仿真的资产，但材料行为往往仅从外观推断，难以控制；复杂物体交互和语义部件之间的耦合也使物理参数估计困难。

Result: PhysForge 基于视频扩散模型，包含两个互补模块。PhysFit 以材料条件 VDM 动态作为物理属性拟合参照，使用窗口化拟合聚焦能揭示材料的交互，并用 Hallucinated Forcing 区分 VDM 虚构的非物理运动与真实材料响应。PhysComp 将复杂交互拆为独立物体和语义组件，使物理参数可分别优化、灵活组合用于联合仿真。在 PhysAssets 和 Google Scanned Objects 上，作者报告相较现有方法具有更强的材料相关物理响应控制；常见和不常见材料的 CLIP 与物理可控性分数均为最高。定性比较、组件消融和用户研究也支持所述改进，但摘要未提供具体数值。

Abstract: Recent advances in physical 3D generation have enabled visually faithful assets with simulatable mechanical behavior. However, existing methods provide limited control over material behavior beyond appearance-based inference and struggle to estimate physical properties from complex interactions between objects and semantic parts. Therefore, we present PhysForge, a Video Diffusion Model (VDM)-based framework for addressing these challenges, comprising two complementary modules for material-controllable physical fitting and componentlevel interaction modeling. The first, PhysFit, leverages material-conditioned VDM dynamics as references for physical-property fitting, introducing windowed fitting to focus on material-revealing interactions and Hallucinated Forcing to disentangle nonphysical motion hallucinated by the VDM from material responses. The second, PhysComp decomposes complex interactions into individual objects and semantic components, enabling their physical parameters to be optimized independently and flexibly composed for joint simulation. Experiments on PhysAssets and Google Scanned Objects demonstrate that, compared with existing methods, PhysForge provides stronger control over material-dependent physical responses while supporting component-level physical modeling for complex interactions. It achieves the best CLIP and physical controllability scores on both common and uncommon materials, with qualitative comparisons, component-level ablations, and a user study further supporting these improvements. Our project page is available at https://masterwanna.github.io/PhysForge.html .

Result: GLIMPSE 提出全局—局部记忆接口。局部 patch 记忆提供视角相关外观，循环全局记忆提供场景上下文；检索时在固定局部读取预算下选择历史 patch。多精度坐标按频带表示位置，并用几何条件频谱衰减降低不准确投影地址对后续计算的影响。在相同检索与读取预算下，GLIMPSE 相较确定性位置传输提升几何对应和光度一致性；摘要未给具体数值或下游视频质量评分。

Abstract: A camera-controlled video world model must keep a scene consistent when the camera returns to it. Memories that render stored features into the target frame can turn depth and pose errors into ghosting or duplicated structure. Positional transport avoids that resampling by relocating a historical patch’s address rather than its content, but assigns full positional precision even to inaccurate projections. We present GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface with band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses. Local patch memory supplies view-specific appearance, while recurrent global memory provides scene context. Footprint-guided retrieval selects historical patches under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.

Motivation: AI 测试评估通常只有留出测试集，不知道训练数据覆盖哪些语义条件。如果时间、天气或地点等关键输入条件在测试集中不足，评估结论可能被语义覆盖缺口误导。

Abstract: In test and evaluation (T&E) of AI models, evaluators typically have access only to a held-out test dataset and no knowledge of the training data or its semantic composition. If critical input conditions—such as time-of-day, weather, or scene location—are underrepresented in this test dataset, T&E results can be misleadingly due to this semantic coverage gap. We propose a three-stage generative AI pipeline to detect and fill such semantic gaps without requiring ground-truth labels of these semantics. First, vision-language models (VLMs) perform zero-shot binary labeling of semantic factors, and an embedding-space clustering step identifies which semantics most affect downstream model behavior. Second, diffusion-based image-to-image models transform existing covered images into the underrepresented semantic domain, preserving scene content while augmenting high-level appearance. Third, statistical analysis of the augmented image embeddings verifies that the synthetic imagery improves dataset coverage before it is incorporated. Crucially, the synthetic data is used only to enrich the test set and no model retraining is performed for T&E. We validate the full pipeline on both image classification and object detection under day/night semantic gaps, showing that the augmented test datasets better distinguish models with and without exposure to the missing semantic, thereby yielding T&E conclusions that more closely match those of an ideal, fully covered dataset. Abstract: The rapid development of personalized video generation has raised serious concerns over identity privacy, as a few facial videos of a person now suffice to reproduce their identity in synthetic videos. This report presents the first-place solutions to both phases of the ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos, which aims to protect facial videos from unauthorized customization under a limited and imperceptible perturbation budget. For Phase 1, we introduce Reference Capacity Collapse, a pair of complementary losses that degrade the information carried by the reference condition while keeping subsequent video representations dependent on it, together with a Motion-Guided EOT mechanism that improves robustness across unknown inference conditions. Phase 2 further considers tuning-based customization and unseen generative models. To address both threats within a single perturbation, we design a two-layer framework that protects the facial and background regions separately, constructing structure-aligned perturbations from a synthetic target identity in the former and strengthening the white-box disruption of Phase 1 in the latter. Our method achieves nearly perfect protection under white-box reference-based customization, and remains effective under other customization paradigms, various models, and temporal attacks. Both solutions are built upon our recent work LETHE. We hope our solutions can inspire future work on protecting facial videos against unauthorized customization. [74] State-Isolated Vision Mamba via Update-Tendency Routing for Underwater Image Enhancement cs.CVPDF

Yan Luo, Laibin Chang, Zefan Feng, Yi’ang Chen, Kui Jiang

TL;DR: IsoMamba 按 token 的状态更新倾向聚类并路由到隔离的 Mamba 状态流，缓解异质图像内容竞争共享隐状态；水下增强基准上报告多项 SOTA 和更干净的状态响应。

Details

Motivation: Vision Mamba 通过递归状态演化建模长程空间依赖，但已有方法主要改进 token 排列、扫描或专家路由，较少研究异质图像区域应如何组织递归状态。不同更新行为的 token 共用状态可能产生持续跨内容干扰，作者称为 State Contention。

Result: 作者提出 State Isolation 原则：状态更新倾向相容的 token 在独立递归状态流中演化。IsoMamba 以 Δ 引导构造模型内生更新表示，通过无标签聚类发现更新兼容的 token 组，再按 cluster—route—isolate 流程路由到隔离的 Mamba 专家。针对水下图像不同波长的重建难度，还设计动态通道级重加权目标。多个水下增强基准实验显示，IsoMamba 持续达到所比较方法中的 SOTA，并产生更干净的隐状态响应；摘要未列出数据集、指标和具体分数。

Insight: 状态组织按更新兼容性而非 token 顺序划分，目标是减少共享递归状态中的内容干扰；动态通道权重补偿波长差异。当前摘要只给总体定性结论，没有量化各基准收益。

Abstract: Vision Mamba has recently emerged as an efficient alternative to convolutional and Transformer-based architectures by modeling long-range spatial dependencies through recurrent state evolution. Existing studies primarily improve token serialization, scanning strategies, or expert routing, yet largely overlook a state level question question: how should recurrent states be organized for spatially heterogeneous visual restoration? In this paper, we show that forcing tokens with heterogeneous update behaviors to recurrently modify a shared hidden state leads to persistent cross-content interference, which we term \textbf{State Contention}. We further argue that effective recurrent modeling should organize state evolution according to token compatibility rather than token ordering. Based on this observation, we introduce \textbf{State Isolation}, a new recurrent state organization principle for Vision Mamba,tokens with compatible state-update tendencies evolve within independent recurrent state streams. To instantiate this principle, we propose \textbf{IsoMamba}, a $Δ$-guided architecture that constructs model-intrinsic update representations, discovers update-compatible token groups through label-free clustering, and routes them to isolated Mamba experts following a \textbf{cluster–route–isolate} paradigm. In addition, we develop a dynamic channel-wise reweighting objective to compensate for wavelength-dependent reconstruction difficulty in underwater images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IsoMamba consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks while providing cleaner hidden state responses, validating the effectiveness of state isolated recurrent modeling.

Abstract: Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. [77] OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What’s Missing? cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Jasin Cekinmez, Addison J. Wu, Ryo Mitsuhashi, Linrong Cai, Xingyu Fu

TL;DR: OccludeBench 区分由阴影/反射直接恢复遮挡物与由场景语境推断两类任务；五种 VLM 在直接案例准确率平均 42.7%，显著低于推断案例的 62.7%。

Details

Motivation: 理解图像中被完全遮挡、因而不可见的物体需要从剩余物理线索或场景上下文推断。作者希望定位 VLM 在视觉证据恢复过程中的具体失败环节，而不只测最终答案。

Result: OccludeBench 是人工整理的全遮挡物体恢复基准，分直接线索案例（如阴影或反射）和场景语境推断案例。五种 VLM 的直接案例平均准确率为 42.7%，推断案例为 62.7%，相差 20.0 个百分点；人类分别为 63.4% 和 68.5%，差异不显著。四阶段诊断工具 OccludeTrace 显示瓶颈出现在从完整图像投影中恢复相关物体：模型在失败样例中只有 50% 能找回该物体，而正确作答样例中为 91%。长推理、工具和少样本提示效果不稳定，无法可靠消除差距。对具图像生成能力的两种模型，先补绘候选隐藏物体、再让模型依据可见物理线索检验该假设，可将两类差距降至统计上不显著。

Insight: 模型更擅长借助整体语境猜测，而不擅长从局部物理投影证据恢复被遮挡实体；分阶段诊断揭示了答案错误的视觉定位瓶颈。补绘再核验的效果目前报告于具图像生成能力的两种模型，不能泛化到所有 VLM。

Abstract: How well can vision-language models (VLMs) reason about what is missing from an image? We study this question with OccludeBench, a manually curated benchmark for recovering fully occluded objects from the visual evidence that remains. OccludeBench distinguishes direct cases, where the hidden object must be recovered from a specific physical cue such as a shadow or reflection, from inferential cases, where it can be inferred from broader scene context. Across five VLMs, accuracy averages 42.7% on direct cases versus 62.7% on inferential cases, exhibiting a 20.0-point gap. Humans attain higher accuracies of 63.4% versus 68.5% for direct and inferential images, respectively, with no significant gap. To locate this failure, we introduce OccludeTrace, a four-stage diagnostic that isolates where the visual reasoning process breaks down. The bottleneck emerges when models must recover the relevant object from the projection within the full image: they succeed on only 50% of failed examples, compared with 91% of examples they answer correctly. Extended reasoning, tool use, and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent improvements and do not reliably eliminate the gap. In contrast, an inpaint-then-confront intervention, which prompts models to first generate a candidate hidden object and then evaluate that hypothesis against the visible physical cue, reduces the gap to a statistically non-significant difference for both models with image-generation capabilities. Together, our results establish a tractable, controlled diagnostic for isolating how VLMs reason about visual content that is missing from an image.

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from fMRI is important for understanding human perception and developing brain-computer interfaces. Recent generative decoding methods can reconstruct plausible images and videos, but visual plausibility alone does not ensure that the output is faithfully grounded in brain activity. In this work, we propose Neurons++, a visual-stream fMRI reconstruction framework for both static image and dynamic video reconstruction. Compared with our conference version Neurons, Neurons++ extends the paradigm from video-only reconstruction to visual-stream reconstruction for both images and videos, and replaces parallel task conditioning with serial cortex-inspired decoding. The model first uses image-fMRI pretraining to obtain a stable spatial-semantic initialization, and then performs subject-specific fine-tuning for image and video reconstruction separately. Abstract: Driving world action models (WAMs) learn from paired trajectories and future observations. Post-training with reinforcement learning allows the policy to explore alternative trajectories and improve planning using driving rewards. However, driving logs contain future videos only for the recorded trajectories, leaving these alternatives without corresponding visual supervision. Thus, we introduce SimVTA, a simulation-grounded post-training framework that combines trajectory optimization with video supervision. First, we sample candidate trajectories for each observation and use a real-world simulator (e.g., World Engine) to render future videos for high-reward candidates in real-world scenes reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). This provides trajectory-matched supervision independent of the model’s own predictions. Under a limited rendering budget, we prioritize video supervision for higher-error futures among these rendered candidates. Finally, we optimize trajectory planning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and use a flow-matching loss on the matched videos to update shared representations. At inference time, the model predicts trajectories directly, without simulator calls. On NAVSIM v1, applying SimVTA to a WAM initialization improves PDMS from 86.2 to 91.3 (+5.1). It achieves 88.2 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and an average HD-Score of 38.8 in closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show that updating shared representations with video supervision improves planning, while prioritizing higher-error futures among high-reward trajectories further improves performance.

Abstract: Selective state-space models are an efficient approach to long-sequence video modeling. However, their recurrent computation makes it difficult to determine which video tokens drive a prediction. Existing attribution methods often require additional backward passes or repeated model evaluations, making them substantially more expensive than the forward inference they aim to explain. In this work, we introduce READ, a parameter-free attribution method for selective state-space models that unrolls the recurrence at the position read by the classifier. READ traces the contribution accumulated in the recurrent state back to the video tokens that contribute to the final prediction. By anchoring attribution at the classifier’s readout, it produces a single spatiotemporal relevance map from which frame- and region-level relevance can be obtained directly. Despite requiring only one forward pass, READ achieves competitive faithfulness and localisation compared with substantially more expensive attribution methods. Experimental results show that READ avoids the systematic relevance biases of existing methods while recovering meaningful temporal and class-specific structure across datasets and backbones. More broadly, our results show that the recurrence underlying selective state-space models is not only an efficient mechanism for inference, but also a direct source of interpretable evidence for their predictions.

Abstract: When solving complex spatial reasoning tasks, ordinary humans naturally revisit the scenes from task-relevant viewpoints and mentally reconstruct the spatial layouts from those viewpoints. [82] LapMerge: Differentiable Exact-Budget Token Merging in Linear Time cs.CVPDF

Magdalena Trędowicz, Aleksandra Nowak, Tomasz Steifer, Łukasz Struski, Szymon Płotka

TL;DR: LapMerge 用可学习质量分布把有序 token 切为精确等质量段，并用前缀/后缀扫描线性聚合，实现可微、精确预算且前后向均为 O(M+N) 的 token 合并。

Details

Motivation: 长 token 序列是视觉和语言模型推理成本的主要来源。筛选 token 会丢信息，而已有合并方法依赖两两 token 匹配或 M×N token—输出交互，难以同时保留信息、可微训练和高效率。

Result: LapMerge 根据 Laplace 核密度学习质量分布，将长度为 M 的有序序列划分为恰好 N 个等质量段，再通过前缀/后缀扫描聚合每段。前向和反向计算均为 O(M+N)，同时满足精确输出预算和端到端可微；运行时改变预算不需修改算子参数，学得的质量分布还能显示预算分配到哪些 token。视觉和视觉语言任务实验中，LapMerge 达到或超过强 token reducer，在长序列时最高快两个数量级；当 token 顺序体现空间或时间局部性时尤为有效，也可作为即插即用模块或可训练架构组件。

Insight: 利用有序结构和质量分段绕开全量两两相似度比较，兼顾聚合保留信息与计算复杂度。两数量级是长序列设置下最高加速，摘要未列具体任务、长度和绝对延迟。

Abstract: Modern vision and language models process ever longer token sequences, and reducing them is the main lever on downstream cost. Token compression, however, faces a fundamental trade-off: selecting tokens discards information, while existing merging methods preserve it only through pairwise token-to-token matching or $MN$ token-to-output interactions. We introduce LapMerge, a differentiable merging operator that resolves this trade-off for ordered sequences: a learned mass distribution partitions the sequence into exactly $N$ equal-mass segments under a Laplace-kernel density, and prefix/suffix scans aggregate each segment, so both the forward and the backward pass run in $\mathcal{O}(M+N)$ time. This combination - an exact output budget, end-to-end differentiability, and linear cost - is precisely what previous reducers trade off against one another. The budget can be changed without changing operator parameters, and the learned mass doubles as a token-level map of where the budget is allocated. Across vision and vision-language tasks, LapMerge matches or improves on strong token reducers while being up to two orders of magnitude faster at large sequence lengths; it is particularly effective when token order reflects spatial or temporal locality, and works both as a drop-in reduction module and as a trainable architectural component.

Emma Norrby, Tomas Wilkinson, Per Nyblom, David Gustafsson

TL;DR: 该研究提出多帧事件表示并用稀疏感知掩码自编码预训练 ViT，以改善长时复杂事件序列动作识别；在 THU-EACT-50 CHL 和 DailyDVS-200 上达到 SOTA。

Motivation: 事件相机能捕捉快速运动并具有更好的隐私属性，但基于帧的事件表示在短序列上有效，面对更长、更复杂事件序列时区分能力显著下降。

Abstract: Action recognition is a crucial task in domains like robotics, patient monitoring, or surveillance. Recently, event-based action recognition has attracted much attention due to the event camera’s ability to capture fast motions and its improved privacy over conventional vision cameras. While frame-based event representations work well for short event sequences, their discriminative ability declines sharply with longer and more complex event sequences. In this work, we introduce a multi-frame representation that greatly improves recognition performance over its single-frame counterpart. Abstract: Natural disasters such as earthquakes, tropical storms, and floods cause extensive damage to buildings, and rapid, reliable damage maps are essential to guide emergency response. Automating this task, referred to as building damage assessment (BDA), from satellite imagery, has become an active research topic at the intersection of remote sensing, computer vision, and machine learning, particularly since supervised deep learning methods were shown to outperform earlier handcrafted approaches. Yet, despite a decade of intense activity and the release of several large-scale datasets, the field lacks a shared foundation. This chapter revisits the task of BDA from first principles, organized around three pillars: data, evaluation, and methods. We first clarify the candidate task for- mulations and argue for a sensor-agnostic, instance-based definition that tolerates missing pre-event imagery. We then review existing datasets along the axes of sensing modality, temporal configuration, and geographic coverage. Abstract: Modern machines can solve equations, follow logic and reason through language, yet they still struggle with problems that require visual non-verbal reasoning. This is evident even in advanced systems such as DeepSeek, ChatGPT and Gemini, which remain limited on visual tasks such as completing abstract patterns or delineating ambiguous regions-of-interest. Unlike explicit intermediate steps in verbal reasoning which can be verified through logic or automated tests, visual reasoning involves implicit intermediate steps with no clear methods for verification. Non-verbal reasoning is therefore challenging to model and, as a result, remains a fundamental limitation of current machine intelligence. Here we introduce GenVerOpt, a general framework that unifies generation, verification and optimisation into a reasoning pipeline for machines. By generating candidate solutions, verifying their correctness, and iteratively refining them, GenVerOpt enables per-sample optimisation rather than relying solely on dataset-level trends captured during model training. This process mirrors human problem-solving where multiple possibilities are imagined, evaluated, and refined until a correct solution emerges. Across diverse domains, GenVerOpt demonstrates capabilities beyond existing systems: it surpasses humans on abstract IQ puzzles, matches clinicians in prostate cancer segmentation, exceeds experts in liver vessel reconstruction and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in tumour boundary alignment. These results show that machine reasoning can extend beyond language, establishing visual non-verbal reasoning as a foundation for more flexible and generalisable intelligence.

Abstract: Interpreting machine learning model predictions is a central challenge, particularly for black-box vision models. Concept-based attribution methods interpret models using human-understandable concepts. Existing concept-based methods can only interpret classification and regression models. They require access to model weights or large concept-annotated datasets, and most methods cannot provide image-level attribution. We propose Hide and Seek (HnS), a new method for concept-level attribution that does not require access to model weights or labeled concept datasets. HnS can interpret a wide range of vision models. Given a concept and image set, HnS retrieves concept-aligned images using a vision-language model, localizes the concept within each image, masks the localized region, and measures attribution from the change in the black-box model’s output between the original and masked images. This enables faithful attribution by directly measuring the model’s behavior and supporting local attributions. We validated HnS on CelebA and COCO-Stuff. Compared to existing methods, HnS showed better agreement with ground-truth attributions. [87] Images Talk Back: Guiding MMDiTs by Suppressing Image-to-Text Feedback cs.CVPDF

Yucheng Yang, Xiaoxiao Sun, Shusheng Yang, Jiakang Chen, Serena Yeung-Levy

TL;DR: SFG/iSFG 通过削弱多模态扩散 Transformer 的图像到文本反馈并加强文本到图像条件改善生成；FLUX 上 GenEval 从 0.6661 升至 0.7479，SD3.5M 的 iSFG 相对 CFG 提高 HPSv2.1 1.33 且不增加 NFE。

Details

Motivation: MMDiT 联合注意力使正在生成的图像不断更新内部文本表示，文本表示再影响后续图像。该反馈可能修正偏离提示的内容，也可能固化已经出现在图像中的错误；作者研究其作用并设计选择性干预。

Result: 实验发现图像到文本（I2T）反馈会保留当前图像中已有对象和属性，包含错误；移植反馈消息既可能挽救失败，也可能破坏成功。在匹配干预强度下，抑制 I2T 反馈可改善生成，反向控制则变差。Semantic Feedback Guidance（SFG）通过远离一个增强 I2T 反馈、削弱文本到图像条件的辅助分支来抑制反馈；原位变体 iSFG 直接减弱 I2T、增强文本到图像条件，不需额外模型评估。时间干预实验建议 SFG 只在采样前 20% 步运行，约需基线 1.2× NFE（无 CFG）。三个图像主干上对齐度和偏好分数提高：FLUX 加 CFG 后 GenEval 从 0.6661 到 0.7479；SD3.5M 上 iSFG 相较 CFG 多 1.33 HPSv2.1 且零额外 NFE。控制也迁移到文生视频和图生视频并提升 VBench。

Insight: 生成中的图像状态会经 I2T 路径反馈并固化已有内容，因此减少这种反馈可恢复文本条件的纠偏作用。不同变体成本不同：iSFG 无额外 NFE，而 SFG 约增加 1.2×；具体分数对应所列主干和指标。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs) allow the evolving image to update internal text representations through joint attention. These representations then guide subsequent image updates, raising a question: does this feedback help correct deviations from the prompt, or preserve them? We find that image-to-text (I2T) feedback can preserve the objects and attributes already represented in the evolving image, including errors. Transplanting I2T messages can rescue failures or disrupt successes. At matched intervention strengths, suppressing the influence of I2T feedback improves generation, whereas opposite controls degrade it. These findings motivate Semantic Feedback Guidance (SFG), which suppresses the influence of I2T feedback by guiding away from an auxiliary branch that strengthens this feedback and weakens text-to-image conditioning. The in-place variant, iSFG, directly suppresses I2T feedback and strengthens text-to-image conditioning, requiring no extra model evaluations. Temporal interventions motivate running the SFG branch during the first 20% of image sampling steps, requiring about $1.2\times$ the base NFE without classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments across three image-generation backbones show gains in alignment and preference-model scores. On FLUX, adding SFG to CFG raises GenEval from $0.6661$ to $0.7479$; on SD3.5M, iSFG adds $+1.33$ HPSv2.1 over CFG at zero extra NFE. The control also transfers to text-to-video and image-to-video generation, improving VBench scores. Code is available at https://github.com/yyc1215lll-creator/SFG.

[88] Contextual Correction of Parallel Drafts for Speculative Decoding cs.CLPDF

Roy Eisenstadt, Harel Lidar, Tal Gonen, Omer Luxembourg, Itamar Zimerman

TL;DR: 提出 DCOCO，在保持扩散草稿并行生成的同时，为草稿块候选 token 进行邻位上下文兼容性评分，恢复自回归生成中可用的块内依赖。

Details

Motivation: 扩散草稿器可一次并行预测多个未来 token，避免逐 token 自回归开销；代价是块内 token 预测看不到前面草稿 token 的实际身份，丢失自然的局部依赖。

Result: DCOCO 不为每个位置只选 top-1，而保留多个 token 候选，并并行评估相邻草稿位置的上下文兼容性，以较低额外成本选取更合适组合。在四种 verifier 骨干、十个基准上，所有评测设置（贪心/随机解码、扩展推理开/关）平均吞吐均最高，较原始解码最高加速 6.6×。并发服务 4–32 个请求时较最强竞品高 6%–23%；其他顺序校正器在这些负载下甚至低于未经校正 DFlash。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position’s top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.

[89] SWE-Duel: Towards a Self-Scaling Arena for Coding Agents cs.CL | cs.SEPDF

Wei Minn, Zhensu Sun, Lwin Khin Shar, David Lo

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-Duel，一个评测时生成任务和 oracle 的自扩展编码代理竞技场，通过红蓝回合让代理互相出题和修复，避免静态基准泄漏及饱和。

Details

Motivation: 静态编码基准发布后任务会泄漏到训练数据，更强代理最终会饱和；基准难度固定且内容预先存在。作者主张任务和判定应在评估时生成，并让参与代理能力决定挑战难度。

Result: 每轮 Red 代理在真实仓库中跨三个上下文隔离会话实现新功能，加入细微 bug 和密封触发测试。PR 需通过功能测试、仓库现有测试且恰好失败 bug 测试；同代理的新会话还需盲修复成功，排除无解任务。Blue 不知存在 bug，只有修订通过三类测试才获胜，随后交换角色。11 种模型—harness 配置在 15 个仓库、五种语言进行 1,500 回合比赛。GPT-5.5+Codex CLI 解题能力最强，修复所审 bug 的 98.3%；Claude Opus 4.8+mini-swe-agent 出题能力最强，Red 胜率 27.3%。同一模型家族中，最新版所写任务击败固定参考解题器的比例为 42.9%，旧版任务为 4.0%，初步支持任务难度随出题能力扩展。

Insight: 基准能够评测出题和解题两种能力，并在评估时生成任务降低预泄漏。SWE-Duel 的可解性验证依赖同一 agent 后续盲修复。

Abstract: Static coding benchmarks are fixed at release. Over time their tasks leak into training data, and stronger agents eventually saturate them. We argue that a benchmark should instead aim to be self-scaling: its tasks and oracles are created at evaluation time, so no content exists in advance to leak, and its difficulty is set by the participating agents rather than fixed at release. We introduce SWE-Duel, an arena in which two coding agents evaluate each other over mirrored Red-Blue turns. In each turn, the Red agent works on a real repository across three context-isolated sessions. It proposes and implements a new feature with feature tests, then embeds a subtle bug in that feature along with sealed bug-triggering tests. The resulting pull request is admitted only if (1) it passes the feature tests and the repository’s existing tests while failing exactly the bug tests, and (2) a fresh session of the same agent can repair the bug blind, which rules out unsolvable tasks. The Blue agent then reviews the pull request without being told a bug exists. It wins only if its revision passes all three test suites, and then the two agents swap roles. We run a tournament among 11 model-harness participants on 15 repositories in five languages for 1,500 turns. The leaders win in different ways. GPT-5.5 with Codex CLI ranks first as the strongest solver, repairing 98.3% of the defects it reviews. Claude Opus 4.8 with mini-swe-agent ranks second as the strongest task author, with a 27.3% Red win rate. Finally, within one model family, tasks written by the newest version defeat a fixed-reference solver far more often than those of older versions (42.9% vs. 4.0%), giving preliminary evidence that task difficulty scales with task authoring capability.

[90] AutoIndexer: Flexible-Order Decoding by Training Causal Models on Chains of Edits cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Shu Ishida, Aliasghar Khani, Tianyu Zhang, The Cong Luong, James Seale Smith

TL;DR: 提出 AutoIndexer，通过训练自回归语言模型执行可变长度的链式编辑，使模型能在生成后对任意上下文 token 插入、替换和删除。

Details

Motivation: 自回归模型不擅长跨引用或依赖未来内容等非因果顺序数据，生成后不能修订；扩散 LLM 虽部分缓解，也难灵活改变预生成上下文的 token 数量。

Result: AutoIndexer 训练模型预测编辑开启/关闭标记，并移动光标定位现有 token 序列，像文本编辑器一样进行后验修改。训练样本通过对真值执行逆链式编辑生成，模型学习撤销这些扰动；这种扰动训练使其适应错误上下文和代码纠错。作者展示后生成编辑能力，这是大多数自回归和扩散模型缺少的，同时标准 LLM 基准表现未受损。摘要未提供各任务具体分数。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding is less suited to non-causally ordered data, e.g., cross-references or future dependencies, since their outputs cannot be revised post hoc, unlike iterative human reasoning. Diffusion LLMs partially mitigate this limitation but still cannot flexibly insert or remove arbitrary numbers of tokens from a pre-generated context. We design AutoIndexer as a generative model with a novel training and decoding mechanism that simulates a Chain of Edits, allowing the model to flexibly modify its prior outputs in hindsight via token insertions, substitutions, and deletions. The model learns to predict marker tokens for opening and closing edits, simultaneously moving a cursor to positions in the existing token sequence, analogous to a text editor. Training sequences are obtained by perturbing ground-truth labels with inverse chained edit operations, and the model learns to edit by undoing those inverses. The perturbation training allows AutoIndexer to be robust to erroneous context and adaptable to editing tasks such as code correction. We demonstrate that our method exhibits post-generation context-editing capabilities that most autoregressive and diffusion models lack without compromising performance on standard LLM benchmarks. Abstract: Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.

TL;DR: 提出 COFFEQA，用经上下文依赖度验证的事实—反事实样本对进行少样本问答蒸馏，避免选取教师决策边界附近的不确定样本造成退化。

Details

Motivation: 少样本蒸馏可降低生成式任务成本，但效果高度依赖训练样本选择；作者发现教师决策边界附近的不确定样本可能显著损害蒸馏。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to train a smaller student model to mimic a larger teacher model for resource-constrained environments. However, the process of distillation is typically data-intensive and computationally expensive, particularly for generative tasks. Few-shot distillation offers an alternative by transferring teacher knowledge using limited training samples. However, we observe that few-shot distillation performance is heavily dependent on the choice of these training samples. In fact, we demonstrate that using more uncertain samples near the teacher’s decision boundary can significantly deteriorate distillation performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose Counterfactual-guided Few-shot Distillation in Open-book Question Answering (COFFEQA), a novel strategy that aptly uses counterfactuals to provide additional supervision around challenging factual samples. In the generative QA setting, we define a counterfactual as a minimally modified context that induces the teacher to change its response to an alternative answer. We provide geometric insights on how factual-counterfactual pairs can constrain the deviation between the teacher’s and student’s decision boundaries. We further introduce a new metric, Context Following Difficulty (CFD) that further validates counterfactuals prior to distillation based on agreement with the intended alternative answer and dependence on the modified context. COFFEQA uses CFD-validated factual-counterfactual pairs for few-shot distillation. Experiments across five in-domain and three out-of-domain QA benchmarks using T5 and Qwen2.5 families show that COFFEQA outperforms factual-only distillation baselines under the same few-shot budget. For instance, on TriviaQA, COFFEQA improves over the factual-only baseline by 8.77 average EM points and 9.98 average F1 points under the T5 setting with 32 few-shot pairs.

Abstract: Search agents extend large reasoning models with multi-round access to external knowledge, yet most existing systems largely conflate retrieving evidence with consuming it. Retrieved documents are commonly incorporated according to fixed or rank-based policies, despite the fact that evidence utility is conditioned on the agent’s current reasoning state. Consequently, a lower-ranked candidate may be critical for the current step, while a higher-ranked candidate may provide redundant, irrelevant, or misleading information. We formulate this overlooked problem as evidence admission, which asks, at each search step, which retrieved candidates should be allowed to enter the agent’s evolving reasoning context. To enable such selective control, we introduce SEEDS (Selective Evidence Entry During Search), a preview-guided evidence admission framework for search agents. SEEDS first exposes lightweight previews of retrieved candidates and then lets the agent jointly determine which candidates and how many should be fully inspected, conditioned on its current reasoning state. Unlike fixed or adaptive prefix policies, SEEDS can select arbitrary subsets with flexible cardinality, and it can be instantiated directly with an existing reasoning agent or further optimized through learning. Across nine knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks under both local corpus and live web search, SEEDS consistently improves search effectiveness while substantially reducing full-evidence consumption. Experimental analyses show that SEEDS more effectively selects useful candidates beyond the corresponding retrieval prefix. These results establish evidence admission as a complementary dimension of agentic search, separating what information is retrieved from what information is ultimately allowed to shape reasoning.

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) remains a widely used approach for post-training large language models, because fixed demonstrations can directly introduce desired reasoning strategies and behavioral patterns. Yet forcing a model to imitate externally provided trajectories may also interfere with behaviors already supported by pretraining, limiting generalization beyond the training distribution. Recent dynamic SFT methods mitigate this issue by reweighting token-level supervision according to model-side signals such as predictive probability, prior support, or distributional compatibility. However, model compatibility is not equivalent to learning value: a token that is weakly supported by the current model may either be an undesirable mismatch or precisely the task-specific decision that the demonstration is intended to teach. We propose CIG-SFT, an information-theoretic framework that estimates the task-conditioned information carried by each demonstrated token. Under teacher forcing, we compare the token likelihood from a frozen reference model with and without access to the task input while keeping the gold solution prefix fixed. The resulting conditional information gain quantifies how much the task itself reduces uncertainty about the demonstrated decision. We then convert this information into a KL-regularized projection of the current policy and derive an equivalent scalar-weighted SFT objective whose logit gradient exactly matches that of the projected target. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, CIG-SFT consistently outperforms standard SFT and recent adaptive fine-tuning baselines, with particularly strong improvements in generalization to challenging evaluation distributions, while requiring neither additional training data nor online rollouts.

[99] Evo$^2$: Evolving the Evolver through Weight-Harness Co-Evolution cs.CLPDF

Chejian Xu, Shujian Zhang, Wenxuan Zhou, John Lambert, Beck Chen

TL;DR: Evo² 联合进化智能体权重与工具使用 harness，通过同一策略的提案者—反思者双角色更新，在代码基准和跨任务迁移上超过单独优化权重或 harness 的方法。

Details

Motivation: 自进化智能体通常分别改进模型权重或提示、工具、上下文与执行反馈构成的 harness，忽略两者相互影响。作者希望让 harness 修改及执行结果同时指导策略训练，从而实现更连贯的联合进化。

Result: Evo² 使用单一策略承担 Proposer（编写 harness 修改）和 Reflector（诊断效果并指导修订）两个角色； harness 更新及执行结果也用于训练这两个角色。训练流水线 EvoTIDE 通过编辑提取和前缀续写恢复可用轨迹，并在两类角色间交替使用 SFT 与 GRPO。三个代码基准实验中，Polyglot 得分 82.1%，SWE-bench Verified 得分 48.3%，对应最强基线为 62.6% 和 41.0%；进化后的 harness 可迁移到其他任务，跨基准评测排名第一。双角色方法用于 GPT-5-mini 时，成绩由 24.4% 提升到 48.3%。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are increasingly used to improve language model systems without human intervention through either weight updates or harness improvements. Existing approaches typically pursue these directions separately: some methods update model weights using self-generated experience, while others rewrite the harness that defines how an agent uses prompts, tools, context, and execution feedback. However, these approaches overlook the relationship between model weights and the agent harness. To connect these processes and ensure effective and smooth agent self-evolving, we propose Evo$^2$, a framework for weight-harness co-evolution. For harness updates, we design a unified dual-role pipeline that a single policy serves as both a Proposer, which writes harness edits, and a Reflector, which diagnoses their effects and guides revision. For weight updates, harness modifications and their execution outcomes guide the training of both the Proposer and the Reflector. We further develop EvoTIDE, a training pipeline that recovers usable trajectories through edit extraction and prefix continuation, then combines SFT with interleaved GRPO across the two roles. Extensive experiments across three coding benchmarks demonstrate that Evo$^2$ significantly outperforms existing self-evolution methods. It achieves 82.1% on Polyglot and 48.3% on SWE-bench Verified, compared with 62.6% and 41.0% for the strongest baseline, respectively. We also show that the evolved harnesses transfer to different tasks without further evolution and rank first in cross-benchmark evaluations. Our dual-role harness improvement framework is also able to extend to self-evolution with proprietary models, improving a GPT-5-mini agent’s performance from 24.4% to 48.3%. These results demonstrate the applicability of the framework Evo$^2$ across domains and its broad transferability across models and tasks.

[100] RAMP: Recurrent Adaptive Mixed Precision for Efficient Prefill in Recurrent-Depth Language Models cs.CLPDF

Ke Wan, Chen Chen

TL;DR: RAMP 把混合精度计算安排在递归深度前段、末段恢复高精度，利用误差会被后续递归细化衰减的规律，在 H100 上实现最高 1.16 倍首轮 prefill 加速。

Details

Motivation: 递归深度语言模型反复运行共享网络进行推理，长上下文 prefill 因整段输入被多次处理而成本高。量化虽能加速，但数值误差会沿递归轨迹传播；需要找到低精度计算最适合放置的深度位置。

Result: 作者分析递归深度中的误差传播，发现较早引入的量化误差会受到更多后续递归步骤的衰减。等预算实验支持将低精度放在较早递归步、终端细化恢复高精度时保真度最好。RAMP 对前段递归使用低精度权重与激活计算，末段切回高精度。在最长 4K 上下文实验中，prefill 质量接近全精度；NVIDIA H100 上首轮 prefill 的端到端加速最高为 1.16 倍，对应延迟降低 13.9%。

Insight: 混合精度在递归模型中的收益取决于误差出现的位置：较早误差可由后续细化吸收，末段输出则需要高精度保护。报告的最高加速来自 H100、最长 4K 上下文范围内的实验；摘要未说明其他硬件或生成阶段性能。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth language models improve inference-time reasoning by repeatedly applying a shared network to refine internal representations, but the additional recurrent computation also increases inference cost. This overhead is particularly pronounced during long-context prefill, where the full input sequence is repeatedly processed across recurrent depth. Weight-and-activation quantization offers a natural way to accelerate this computation through lower-precision arithmetic, yet repeatedly applying quantized computation introduces numerical perturbations that accumulate along the recurrent trajectory. We systematically analyze how such errors propagate across recurrent depth and show that quantization errors introduced earlier are attenuated by more subsequent recurrent steps and therefore have a smaller effect on the final representation. Matched-budget experiments strongly support this prediction, showing that placing quantized computation earlier in recurrent depth consistently yields the best fidelity. Based on this insight, we introduce RAMP (Recurrent Adaptive Mixed Precision), which performs early recurrent refinement using low-precision weight-and-activation computation and restores high precision for terminal refinement. Across context lengths up to 4K, RAMP preserves near-full-precision prefill quality while achieving up to 1.16x end-to-end first-prefill speedup on an NVIDIA H100, corresponding to a 13.9% latency reduction.

[101] CoBe: A Benchmark For Conversational Counterfactual Text Editing cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.ET | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: CoBe 是包含 2,000 个自然对话式反事实文本编辑问题的基准，用于检验模型能否只修改假设变化造成的后果并保留其他事实；前沿模型平均得分为 58.05%。

Details

Motivation: 反事实编辑要求改变假设中的原因后，只改动受其因果影响的事件，同时保留原情境中不受影响的事实；关联式生成则可能输出听起来合理、但破坏原有因果结构的故事。既有数据集未能清楚区分这两种能力。

Result: CoBe 覆盖医疗、科学与工程、商业及日常情境，共 2,000 道问题，测试模型在自然对话提示下零样本编辑给定文本的能力。前沿系统平均得分为 58.05%。分析发现模型经常退化为关联式生成；表现也未随模型规模或输出 token 长度稳定增长，提示失败可能与训练目标有关，而非单纯容量不足。

Insight: 评测重点是因果变化的局部性与事实保留，而非生成一个统计上合理的新场景。平均分揭示了当前模型的普遍困难，但摘要未列出各模型分项成绩或类别级差异。

Abstract: Counterfactual text editing is the ability to revise a given text in light of a hypothetical change while preserving facts not causally affected by that change. When asked what would have happened if a doctor had not recommended surgery, the model output should retain the patient’s medical history and modify only the consequences of the decision. This requires causal understanding: a model must determine, without being explicitly instructed to, what events are affected by the hypothetical change and what should remain fixed. This skill is central to human reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like healthcare or law. By contrast, the task of associational text generation merely generates text statistically associated with the altered condition. In the medical example, an associational response might make the patient’s symptoms less severe because milder cases correlate with no surgery. Such responses produce a plausible scenario, but not the counterfactual version of the given one. Prior counterfactual-text datasets do not clearly separate these modes of reasoning. We introduce the Counterfactual Benchmark (CoBe), a testbed for models’ zero-shot ability to counterfactually edit a given textual scenario when prompted to do so in a conversationally natural way. CoBe contains 2000 questions spanning healthcare, science and engineering, business, and everyday scenarios. We test a suite of frontier and smaller models, with frontier systems achieving an average score of $58.05%$. Our findings indicate that models frequently default to associational generation rather than preserving the causal structure of the original scenario. Moreover, performance does not scale consistently with either model size or output token length, suggesting that the failure may reflect model training objectives rather than insufficient model capacity.

[107] PreSTO: Predictive Subtree Prefetching for Fast LLM Power Sampling cs.CLPDF

Nan Jiang, Panagiotis Theodoropoulos, Wei Deng

TL;DR: PreSTO 在不改变 Metropolis–Hastings 转移核的前提下，批量预取 PowerMH 的未来候选并预测遍历子树；七个数据集、五个基础模型上的中位加速最高为 2.33 倍。

Details

Motivation: Power sampling 从基础模型分布的锐化版本采样，能增强测试时推理，但 PowerMH 的接受/拒绝更新是串行的，每一步状态决定下一步候选，限制并行化。既有方案常通过替换采样器或修改模型来降低成本。

Result: PreSTO 将未来 MH 接受/拒绝结果表示成树，在一次模型调用中批量生成一个预取子树中的候选，随后仍按原始 MH 接受规则沿实际路径前进，因此转移核保持不变。考虑到实际采样通常只走子树中稀疏、近似单分支的路径，方法还加入预测遍历规则，以最大化预计完成的转移数。在七个数据集、五种基础 LLM 上，PowerMH、EntropyCut 和 MultiTryMH 的中位加速分别为 1.71×、1.91×、2.33×；前两者模型调用中位数减少 3.9×，单次调用时长为原来的 2.1×。在 LCB V6 的 Qwen3.5-9B 设置中，预测遍历相较默认广度优先遍历再快 1.25–1.37×。

Insight: 通过预取未来候选并保留原接受规则，可并行化采样计算而不近似改变目标转移过程；预测遍历进一步减少无用分支计算。加速数字针对指定算法、基准、模型和中位统计，不能直接外推到其他硬件或负载。

Abstract: Power sampling improves large language model (LLM) reasoning at test time by sampling from a sharpened version of the base model’s distribution. Its sampler, PowerMH, is slow because its Metropolis–Hastings (MH) updates are sequential: each accept/reject decision sets the state from which the next proposal is generated. Prior work reduces this cost by changing the sampler or the model (e.g., sequential Monte Carlo, approximate lookahead, distillation). We propose Predictive SubTree Prefetching (PreSTO), which accelerates PowerMH by prefetching future proposals and provably leaves its MH transition kernel unchanged. PreSTO views future MH outcomes as a tree of accept/reject decisions: in each model call, it generates the proposals of a \textit{prefetched subtree} of this tree in one batch, then follows the realized path with the original MH acceptance rule. We also observe that MH reaches only a sparse, nearly unary part of each prefetched subtree, we further design a \textit{predictive traversal rule} that chooses the subtree to maximize the predicted number of completed transitions. Across seven datasets and five base LLMs, PreSTO achieves median speedups of $1.71\times$, $1.91\times$, and $2.33\times$ on PowerMH and its follow-ups EntropyCut and MultiTryMH, respectively; on the first two, it makes a median of $3.9\times$ fewer model calls, each only $2.1\times$ longer. On LCB V6 with Qwen3.5-9B, the predictive traversal rule is a further $1.25\times$–$1.37\times$ faster than the default breadth-first traversal.

[108] Do Prompt Optimizers Break Requirements They Are Not Scored On? A Preregistered Study cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Sangdo Han, Moonsoo Kim

TL;DR: 一项预注册研究发现，提示优化器会提高目标准确率，却可能因 200 token 上限导致输出未出现答案而违反格式等未评分要求；将上限增至 1,024 后此类失败消失。

Details

Motivation: 提示优化器会改写提示以提升某个目标指标，但提示中还可能包含目标指标不计分的格式、引用或拒答要求。若优化只顾目标分数，可能出现副作用；评估时也需区分真正的内容退化与输出截断造成的契约违反。

Result: 研究在 HotpotQA、SQuAD 2.0 和 IFEval 上预注册评估 GEPA、MIPROv2、TextGrad，并用确定性检查统计每题对未评分要求的违反。按预注册标准，三种优化器均导致退化；HotpotQA 上引用有效性的净退化为 10.7–41.6 个百分点，同时 exact match 提升 21–34 个百分点。事后拆解发现，优化提示诱发先推理、后输出 JSON；在统一使用的 200 token 上限下，13.9%–47.0% 的回答在任何答案出现前就结束。只看可解析输出时，净退化在 ±3 个百分点内。将上限调到 1,024 token 后，抽取失败消失、内容退化低于 1 个百分点，exact match 进一步上升；两个简单门控均未满足预注册判据。

Insight: 观察到的副作用主要是优化后输出超出紧张 token 预算、未能遵守显式格式契约，内容质量本身并未出现相同幅度退化；宽松目标指标没有体现这一成本。预注册结论与事后拆解需分开理解，研究范围限于三种优化器、三项任务和所设长度上限。

Abstract: Prompt optimizers rewrite a prompt to raise one target metric, but prompts also state requirements the metric does not score, such as an output format, citations, or abstention. In a preregistered study, we run GEPA, MIPROv2, and TextGrad on HotpotQA, SQuAD 2.0, and IFEval and count per-item violations of unscored requirements with deterministic checks. By the preregistered criterion, all three optimizers produce regressions, substantially on HotpotQA, where net regression in citation validity reaches 10.7 to 41.6 points while exact match rises by 21 to 34 points. A post-hoc decomposition changes this reading. The optimized prompts elicit reasoning before the required JSON object, and under the 200-token output cap used throughout, 13.9% to 47.0% of responses end before any answer appears. Among parseable outputs, net regression is within 3 points of zero. With a 1,024-token cap, extraction failures disappear, content regression stays below 1 point, and exact match rises further. Two simple gates fail the preregistered criterion. The side effect is narrower than hypothesized: optimized prompts are more accurate and break an explicit output contract, and a lenient target metric does not show the cost.

[109] Test-Time Memory Construction cs.CLPDF

Zijun Wang, Yifan Ji, Haidong Xin, Zhenghao Liu, Yukun Yan

TL;DR: T²Mem 将复杂记忆构建推迟到推理时，离线只保存轻量事件/时间元数据并链接原对话，随后按当前问题逐步检索组织；在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 上优于强基线且降低构建开销。

Details

Motivation: 跨长交互历史问答需要整合散落在多个会话中的证据。传统记忆系统在用户提问前离线构建复杂记忆，开销较大，也难以针对具体问题选择要保留和组织的信息。

Result: T²Mem 离线仅提取轻量 episode 级元数据，记录关键事件及时间关联作为检索线索，并保留指向原始交互的链接，以便重建跨 episode 依赖。测试时，LLM 根据推理进展自适应检索并组织元数据和原始对话，逐步形成面向当前问题的记忆。在 LoCoMo 与 LongMemEval 上，跨不同主干模型持续超过强基线，同时显著降低记忆构建开销；进一步分析显示，推迟构建让记忆能够动态适配各查询的信息需求。

Insight: 系统把预先构造完整记忆改为按需生成查询特定记忆，以轻量元数据引导回溯原始记录。摘要报告跨主干模型的定性优势，但未列出具体分数和开销数值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to answer questions over long interaction histories, where relevant evidence may be scattered across distant sessions. Existing memory systems typically construct and maintain sophisticated memory representations offline, before user queries are observed, incurring substantial overhead and limiting their ability to adapt memory construction to query-specific information needs. In this paper, we propose T$^2$Mem, a Test-Time Memory construction framework that defers memory construction to inference and progressively builds memory along the reasoning process. Specifically, T$^2$Mem extracts lightweight episode-level metadata offline to capture key events and temporal associations as retrieval cues, while retaining links to the original interactions to facilitate the reconstruction of dependencies across episodes. At test time, the LLM adaptively retrieves and organizes metadata and raw interactions, progressively constructing query-specific memory as reasoning unfolds. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show the effectiveness of T$^2$Mem by consistently outperforming strong baselines across different backbone LLMs while substantially reducing the overhead of memory construction. Further analysis demonstrates that deferring memory construction to inference enables the memory construction process to dynamically adapt to the information needs of individual queries. All code is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/T2Mem.

[110] MoPrO-SD: Modular Prompt Optimization for Stance Detection cs.CLPDF

Meysam Sabbaghan, Nikesh Gyawali, Arman Zareian Jahromi, Sebastian Loera-Lozano, Doina Caragea

TL;DR: MoPrO-SD 将立场检测提示拆成语义模块并依据类别条件错误逐步优化，还发布人工标注的 VaccineStance；在多个数据集和开源模型设置下，多数情况下优于人工基础提示。

Motivation: 隐含观点、讽刺和语境依赖表达使大语言模型难以判断立场。通用结构化提示优化没有针对立场标签含义和类别特定歧义进行设计。

Result: MoPrO-SD 将提示分为功能语义模块，并用 LLM 反馈逐步细化；比较三种策略：只优化指令（I）、指令加推理（I+R）、指令加逐样本推理（I+R+E），并利用按立场类别条件化的错误进行修正。作者另建人工标注的疫苗立场基准 VaccineStance。在四个数据集、五种开源权重 LLM 上，方法在许多设置中超过人工撰写的 Base 提示。SemEval-2016 上，五个主干中有四个采用类别条件反馈时的平均 Macro-F1 高于汇总反馈；两种推理策略表现较强，但相对效果随模型和数据集变化，整体与近期提示优化方法有竞争力。

Abstract: Stance detection remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to implicit opinions, sarcasm, and context-dependent expressions of stance. Although automatic prompt optimization offers an alternative to fine-tuning without updating model parameters, existing structured approaches are not specifically designed around stance-label semantics and class-specific ambiguities. We introduce MoPrO-SD, a stance-specific prompt optimization framework that decomposes prompts into functional semantic modules and refines them sequentially using LLM-based feedback. We study three refinement strategies: instruction-only optimization (I), instruction with reasoning (I+R), and instruction with per-example reasoning (I+R+E), using class-conditioned errors for stance-label refinement. We also introduce VaccineStance, a new human-annotated benchmark for vaccine stance detection. Experiments across four datasets and five open-weight LLMs show that MoPrO-SD improves upon the human-written Base prompt in many settings. On SemEval-2016, class-conditioned feedback achieves higher mean Macro-F1 than pooled feedback for four of five backbones. Both reasoning-based strategies, I+R and I+R+E, achieve strong performance, with their relative effectiveness varying across models and datasets, while remaining competitive with recent prompt optimization methods. The MoPrO-SD code and VaccineStance dataset are available on GitHub.

[111] Proactive Agentic Orchestration cs.CLPDF

Xueyang Feng, Chaozhuo Li, Xiaohe Bo, Jiakai Tang, Chenxi Li

Motivation: 现有子智能体编排通常在创建时固定目标和执行环境，难以适应动态任务；编排策略训练又常只有稀疏、延迟的最终反馈，难以针对具体调度决策进行有效学习。

Result: POrchestra 让子智能体可在任意逻辑节点主动联系编排器，请求修改 harness，同时保持本地执行连续性。由此提出 On-Policy Orchestration Distillation（OPOD）：把子智能体事后提出的修订请求归因回此前的编排决策，提取定向过程监督，并与最终奖励一起训练编排策略。在深度研究、动态数字助理和软件工程三个长程智能体基准上，POrchestra 相对最强基线的任务成功率平均提升 9.1%；与 OPOD 结合后，小型编排器相对现有训练基线平均提升 10.0%，且成本—性能折衷更有利。
