Abstract: Agentic orchestration delegates tasks to sub-agents with independent contexts, providing a key path to extending the effective execution horizon of large language model agents. However, existing orchestration methods face two limitations: in execution, current methods fix sub-agents’ goals and harnesses at creation time, limiting their adaptability to dynamic real-world environments; in optimization, sparse and delayed feedback constrains effective learning of the orchestration policy. To address these limitations, we introduce POrchestra, an on-the-fly proactive agentic orchestration framework. It allows sub-agents to proactively communicate with the orchestrator at any logical point to request harness adjustments while maintaining continuity of local execution. This mechanism not only enables sub-agents to adapt seamlessly to dynamic environments but also naturally inspires On-Policy Orchestration Distillation (OPOD) based on hindsight feedback. OPOD attributes sub-agents’ proactive revision requests back to preceding orchestration decisions to derive targeted improvement signals, and leverages these signals as process supervision alongside the final reward to optimize the orchestration policy. We evaluate POrchestra on three long-horizon agent benchmarks spanning deep research, dynamic digital assistance, and software engineering. POrchestra improves task success by an average of 9.1% relative to the strongest baseline; combined with OPOD, it improves small-orchestrator success by an average of 10.0% relative to existing training baselines. POrchestra also achieves a more favorable cost-performance trade-off. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/XueyangFeng/POrchestra.

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems are developed through multiple model based activities, comprising several stages of development. [113] zip2zip++: Towards Inference-Time Token Compression with Minimal Quality Loss cs.CLPDF

Saibo Geng, Andrea Gentilini, Xinxian Ma, Nathan Ranchin, Yunzhen Yao

TL;DR: zip2zip++ 通过输入/输出动态嵌入解耦、长度感知 RoPE 和可选择 LZW 合并缓解压缩词表的质量损失；四种模型上所有测试语料困惑度和数学推理准确率均改善，并在相近压缩率下弥合约一半质量差距。

Details

Motivation: LZW 动态词表能让模型用更少 token 表示同一文本，但以往工作发现推理能力和困惑度会退化。作者试图确认这一损失是否为压缩的必然代价，并定位压缩层和 codec 可改进的原因。

Result: 分析指出四类问题：LZW 造成输入与输出嵌入的隐式冲突，共享嵌入难以处理；合并 token 跨越多个原始 token，直接用常规 RoPE 会忽略其跨度；合并数字等特定 token 会明显伤害下游准确率；动态词表还引入分段歧义，影响标准评估。对应提出解耦输入/输出的动态嵌入层、按 token 长度调整的位置编码，以及支持选择性合并和多视角分段的修改版 LZW。四种模型实验中，所有评测语料上的困惑度和数学推理准确率都有改善；在通用文本相近压缩率下，约弥合 zip2zip 与未压缩基线之间一半的质量差距。

Insight: 质量损失并非只能由底层预训练模型改变来修复，动态词表接口、位置建模和合并策略自身也会影响推理。作者称向无损自适应词表压缩迈出重要一步，但摘要的量化结论是相对弥合约一半差距，并未说明已完全无损。

Abstract: Dynamic vocabularies built on-the-fly via Lempel-Ziv-Welch (LZW) compression let a language model represent the same text in fewer tokens, but prior work shows that this efficiency comes at a cost to reasoning and perplexity. We show that this quality gap is not a fundamental price of compression but can be substantially closed through targeted improvements to the compression layer and LZW codec, without modifying the underlying pretrained language model. Our analysis reveals four key issues: (1) LZW’s compression algorithm creates an implicit conflict between the input embedding and the output embedding, a tension that a single shared embedding cannot resolve; (2) naively applying RoPE to LZW-compressed sequences ignores that merged tokens can span multiple tokens; (3) merging certain tokens (notably digits) disproportionately hurts downstream accuracy; and (4) dynamic vocabularies introduce an inherent segmentation ambiguity that confounds standard evaluation. We address these through decoupled input/output dynamic embedding layer, size-aware RoPE positioning, and a modified LZW codec supporting selective merges and multi-view segmentations. Across four models, our recipe improves perplexity on every corpus we evaluated as well as mathematical reasoning accuracy, closing roughly half the quality gap between zip2zip and the uncompressed baseline at a similar compression ratio on general text. This represents a significant step toward lossless adaptive-vocabulary compression for language models.

Abstract: User simulators are gaining attention as environments to train LLM assistants that can better cope with the nuances of multi-turn conversations with real users. Prior work on user simulation has emphasized generating realistic user utterances, leaving unexplored their potential as a source of direct feedback on the assistant’s responses. Such evaluation signals may not be explicitly communicated in language, but can instead reside in the latent reasoning of users. In this work, we introduce user simulators with intrinsic feedback, namely, models equipped with latent evaluation states: internal thoughts, reactions, and scalar ratings that evaluate the assistant from a first-person user perspective at each conversation turn. [115] Inject a Turn, Not a Token: Learning Where to Redirect LLM Reasoning for Self-Correction cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jiwon Jeon, Myungsik Cho, Jeonghye Kim, Youngchul Sung

TL;DR: UPTURN 学习推理失败轨迹中的最佳干预位置，并将纠错指令作为新用户轮次注入，而非插入生成文本；四种推理模型、六个数学基准上均超过 32 次采样基线，且无需微调 LLM。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型难以稳定发现并纠正自身推理错误。以往训练式方法通常微调模型生成修订或反馈；本文转而学习失败轨迹中何时干预以及如何改变后续推理方向。

Result: 作者在冻结编码器上训练一个 130K 参数 MLP 头，只用最终答案是否正确来预测干预位置。对固定生成预算的实验发现，将纠错指令内联插入文本不能可靠转向推理；把同一指令放进新的用户轮次则能改变模型路线。由此提出 UPTURN（User-Prompt TURN injection at a learned position）：保留所选前缀，在新用户轮次插入指令，再重新生成后缀，无需微调 LLM。在四种推理模型、六个数学基准的所有设置中，平均准确率均高于 32-rollout 基线；在匹配纠错预算下超过自我纠错和反思 token 注入方法，并可迁移到不同推理模型和 GPQA-Diamond，无需重新训练，还可与 GRPO 及自我纠错互补。

Insight: 干预的消息结构可能决定纠错指令是否被模型当作新的控制信号；新用户轮次比在同一生成流中插入文字更有效。论文强调固定预算比较和零微调，但摘要未给各基准绝对准确率。

Abstract: Large language models struggle to reliably identify and correct errors in their own reasoning. Unlike prior training-based approaches that fine-tune an LLM to generate revisions or feedback, we learn where to intervene in failed rollouts and determine how to redirect them. To choose where to intervene, we train a 130K-parameter MLP head on a frozen encoder using only final-answer correctness. For redirection, we find that under a fixed generation budget, the tested inline injections fail to reliably redirect reasoning, whereas presenting the same instruction in a new user turn changes the model’s course. The resulting correction framework, UPTURN (User-Prompt TURN injection at a learned position), requires no LLM fine-tuning: it retains a selected prefix, injects an instruction as a new user turn, and regenerates the suffix. Across four reasoners and six math benchmarks, UPTURN improves average accuracy in every setting over a 32-rollout baseline. It outperforms self-correction and reflection-token injection baselines under matched correction budgets, transfers across reasoners and to GPQA-Diamond without retraining, and complements GRPO and self-correction methods.

Motivation: 法律文本理解需要推理半结构化法规，但仅使用掩码语言模型的法律模型会把法规当作扁平 token 序列，忽视条款层级及约束关系。作者希望将结构知识编码到模型参数中，避免推理时必须遍历离散知识图。

Insight: 预训练阶段利用法规层级和图关系，可使结构知识进入编码器参数，从而无需推理时执行显式图遍历。结果包括同域合成问答、LexGLUE 对比和奥地利法规跨域迁移，三类比较口径不同；摘要未说明各增幅是相对值还是百分点。

Abstract: Legal language understanding requires reasoning over semi-structured regulatory documents, yet standard pre-training objectives do not explicitly target this structural capability. Because current legal language models rely solely on masked language modeling, they treat statutory text as flat token sequences and ignore the underlying hierarchical constraints. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-task pre-training approach for German traffic law (StVO). Our approach combines three complementary objectives: Next Sentence Prediction (NSP), Knowledge Graph (KG) Guided Clustering (CLU), and Knowledge Graph (KG) Aware Paragraph Classification (CLS). This approach explicitly encodes structured legal knowledge directly into the model weights. This encoding enables zero-shot legal reasoning at inference time and removes the need for discrete graph traversal. We evaluate our approach across nine Transformer-based encoders and 324 experimental runs. On synthetic StVO question answering, our models demonstrate consistent relative improvements between 10.3% and 155.0% over baselines. Furthermore, relative to the established LexGLUE baselines (Chalkidis et al., 2022), our models improve over prior results on four of seven legal benchmarks. It also achieves consistent zero-shot transfer gains between 3.4% and 15.8% on Austrian driving regulations. These experimental results indicate that our training approach generalizes successfully beyond the initial training legal domain.

Abstract: Any automation within reliability-critical contexts such as medicine or law has to be transparent and verifiable. Details

Motivation: 可靠的语言模型应能判断何时作答、何时说“不知道”。研究比较直接训练模型与训练独立答案核验/选择组件两种路径，并关注正确回答、答错惩罚和允许弃答共同构成的效用。

Result: 研究以 Qwen3-14B 的事实问答为对象，在三个训练随机种子和两个留出置信阈值下比较答案选择器与原模型校准置信度过滤器。学习型选择器在阈值平均效用上更高。TriviaQA 严格匹配评分下，覆盖率从 20.2% 提高至 30.9%，被评分答案中的汇总错误率从 23.0% 降至 14.5%；部分人工复核审计仍支持这一结果。测试的强化学习改动没有显示稳定增益。在 NQ-Open 上，选择器虽然优于过滤器，但仍逊于始终弃答。

Abstract: A useful language model should know when to answer and when to say “I don’t know.” Can we improve that decision by training the model itself or by training a separate component to check its answers? We study both approaches on Qwen3-14B for factual questions, using a score that rewards correct answers, penalizes mistakes, and allows abstention. Across three training seeds and two held-out confidence thresholds, a learned answer selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the original model’s calibrated confidence filter. On TriviaQA, mean coverage increases from 20.2% to 30.9%, while the pooled error rate among scored answers decreases from 23.0% to 14.5% under exact-match grading. The tested reinforcement-learning changes do not establish consistent gains. On NQ-Open, the selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the filter but still performs worse than always abstaining. A partial human-reviewed audit preserves the TriviaQA finding. Our results support learning to select answers in this setting while showing that reliable abstention across datasets remains unresolved.

[119] When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors cs.CLPDF

Shubh Garg, Chen Shani

TL;DR: EICS 与 CASP-VL 用受控空间扰动评估 VLM 表征稳定性；研究发现空间关系表征弱于词汇信息，且谓词 token 中可识别的关系在汇总为 EOS 或均值池化全局表示时会丢失。

Details

Motivation: VLM 即使在组合基准上明显高于随机水平，其内部表示是否忠实保留空间关系仍不清楚。仅凭最终答案评测可能掩盖关系信息在中间表征中丢失的问题。

Result: 作者提出 EICS（Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability），在控制单概念干预下检验表征是否稳定编码概念；并构造基于 Visual Genome 场景图的因果受控空间扰动基准 CASP-VL。在多个 VLM 架构上，空间关系比词汇信息表征得更不忠实，即便模型在组合基准上的得分高于随机水平。进一步分析显示，关系仍能在谓词自身 token 的表征中识别，但将句子压缩为 EOS token 或均值池化全局表示时便丢失。由此揭示一种输出评测难以发现的失败：输入文本中存在的关系信息未保留在后续使用的全局表示里。

Insight: 模型可以生成正确答案或在基准上高于随机，却仍可能在下游依赖的句级表示中丢失空间关系。结论涉及谓词 token 与两种句级汇总方式；摘要未给具体模型分数或表示稳定性数值。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate’s own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.

[120] SpireBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Strategic Play of Agentic Models with Slay the Spire cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Scott C. Lowe, Sander W. Keemink

TL;DR: SpireBench 用完整《杀戮尖塔》高难度通关任务评测 20 个 LLM 的长程策略；前沿模型可匹敌普通玩家但远逊专家，且随战役延长逐渐落后，风险判断偏保守。

Details

Motivation: 许多智能体基准短小、一次性，难以衡量长时程策略；单看最终成绩也不易区分知识、推理和运气的作用。作者选择早期决策会影响后续战局的完整商业卡牌游戏作为长程评测环境。

Result: SpireBench 让模型在最高难度下完整游玩《Slay the Spire》，并通过对游戏术语进行一致、全面的重命名，检验模型是否依赖既有游戏知识。20 个 LLM 中，前沿模型超过启发式基线、达到平均人类玩家水平，但远低于专家；模型首幕胜率可与专家相当，随着运行时间延长差距扩大。强弱模型的主要区别在单场战斗策略；所有模型都会误判整局风险，倾向保留生命而非冒险变强。模型跨局写入的记忆常错误且过度保守，未能带来改进。作者称其 harness 首次提供完整原版游戏，并与既有 harness 比较。

Abstract: Agentic large language models now tackle complex tasks, yet strategic reasoning over long horizons is under-studied: most agentic benchmarks are short and episodic, and performance on long tasks is hard to dissect into stored knowledge, reasoning and luck. We introduce SpireBench, in which models play the complete commercial deck-builder Slay the Spire (StS), where early decisions decide long-term success. Across 20 large language models (LLMs) at the game’s highest difficulty, frontier models beat heuristic baselines and match the average human player but remain far behind experts. This gap widens as the run lengthens: the strongest models win the first act as often as experts, then fall behind. What separates strong models from weak is how well they play each fight; all of them misjudge risk over a run, preserving health rather than spending it to grow stronger. A comprehensive, self-consistent renaming of every game term shows that part of their performance rests on prior knowledge of the game, and models do not improve from the memories they write across runs, which are often wrong and overly cautious. Our harness is the first to give a model the complete original game, and we compare it with existing harnesses. Overall, SpireBench offers an unsaturated long-horizon benchmark with tools to locate where and why agents fail.

Wenlin Lin, Fanzhe Wei

Motivation: 大规模异构文档问答既要找到正确片段，也要读取文本、图片与表格。常见 RAG 智能体让同一模型同时用各种工具并自行安排步骤，可能过度搜索而不深入阅读，难以稳定完成多跳证据整合。

Result: KnowForge 采用分工流水线：混合检索知识点索引；只能搜索的 Locator；由代码调度、强制第二跳的桥接判定器；合并所有搜索排名且有类型和容量限制的证据池；只能读取文本、图像和表格的 Deep Reader；以及带格式检查器的答案合成器。自托管 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（3B 激活参数）在完整 M3DocVQA（2,441 题、3,368 篇 PDF）上 F1 为 47.5，较已发表 M3DocRAG 高 11.0、较同索引同模型的一次检索回答高 7.1；DeepSeek-V4-Flash 得 53.5。MultiHop-RAG 准确率 73.5，高于最强发表 RAG/GraphRAG 的 71.2；MMLongBench-Doc 上同一模型由 34.8 升至 47.0，接近 GPT-4o 读取页面图像的 46.3。消融显示 Deep Reader 在三个语料上分别贡献 3.8、9.8、0 个点；把所有工具合并给一个智能体反而低 1.9 F1，因其倾向继续搜索而非阅读。

Insight: 任务分工和显式搜索—阅读切换能缓解单智能体工具使用中的过度搜索，且阅读器收益随语料视觉性变化。指标覆盖三个不同基准，F1 与准确率不可混为一谈；延迟随模型调用次数而非语料规模增长的结论来自其索引设置。

Abstract: Question answering over large heterogeneous document collections requires first finding the right passages and then reading them, yet current retrieval-augmented agents give one model both kinds of tools and let it schedule itself. We present KnowForge, a pipeline separated by job: a hybrid-retrieval knowledge-point index; a Locator agent that can only search; a code-scheduled bridge judge that forces the missing second hop; a typed, size-bounded evidence pool that merges every search’s ranking; a Deep Reader agent that can only read text, images and tables; and a synthesizer with a format checker. With a self-hosted Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (3B active), KnowForge reaches F1 47.5 on the full open-domain M3DocVQA (2,441 questions, 3,368 PDFs), +11.0 over the published M3DocRAG system and +7.1 over one-shot retrieve-and-answer on the same index and model, and 53.5 with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Per-job ablations on the full benchmarks attribute the gain: the Deep Reader is worth 3.8, 9.8 and 0 points on the three corpora, tracking how visual each is; merging both agents into one with all tools and twice the rounds loses 1.9 F1 because it keeps searching instead of reading; a round-budget sweep shows a step at four Locator rounds and a plateau beyond; query latency scales with model calls rather than corpus size. We serve the three indexed benchmarks as read-only knowledge bases (free registration) and release all prompts and the runner code: https://github.com/metask-ai/knowforge-bench

[122] BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Jiaming Su, Suorong Yang, Xudong Li, Tengchao Yang, Peng Ye

TL;DR: BoundaryEvo 先用少量教师长推理轨迹建立学生初始能力，再持续选择学生尚未掌握、专家可解的问题；仅用 1,000 样本就在 11 个推理基准上优于静态/动态选样基线。

Details

Motivation: 蒸馏长推理轨迹能提升小模型推理，但固定训练集忽视例题价值会随学生能力变化；已掌握样本监督不足，远超能力边界的样本又难学。样本预算很小时，能力变化与数据选择错配尤其昂贵，初始弱模型的可靠选样也困难。

Result: BoundaryEvo 分两阶段进行数据—模型共进化。Capability Priming 阶段直接蒸馏少量教师生成的长推理轨迹，不另训模型评判过滤，以先建立基础推理能力；Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution 阶段反复挑选当前学生尚未掌握、专家却能可靠求解的问题，并随学生进步更新训练集。仅使用 1,000 个训练样本，在 11 个推理基准上持续超过静态和动态选样基线；Qwen3.5-4B 在 IMO-AnswerBench 从 62.9 提高到 75.3，在 AMO-Bench 从 26.8 提高到 46.0。

Abstract: Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data–model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.

Details

Motivation: 临床预后需要联合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史和群体信息。简单文本序列化或单一检索方式可能丢失数值/时间结构；同时，性能比较必须考虑不同方法可用的输入时间窗是否一致。

Result: PRISM 用逐特征数值 token 化、对纵向 ECG 作近期加权汇总，并维护双模态群体记忆库。检索在表示空间进行，模型接收相似训练队列就诊的均值池化嵌入，不将任何单个检索病历放入提示词。MDS-ED 的 1,443 项任务上，若使用到院前 96 小时数据，诊断、恶化、ICU 入院和死亡 AUROC 分别为 86.2%、93.1%、91.8%、92.8%；该窗口不可用于论文报告的基线。仅用基准时间窗输入时 PRISM AUROC 为 0.8895，UniPACT 为 0.8937，MDS-ED 为 0.8889。相同五种证据流和冻结编码器下，紧凑融合模型为 0.7652，PRISM 为 0.9092。将数值 EHR 改成文本序列化使 AUROC 降 3.66 点，移除到院前病史降 1.97，清零检索降 0.99。真实检索较随机邻居宏平均高 0.97 点，但配对任务级符号检验未证明显著普遍优势（p=0.067）。外部 MC-MED 上，监督微调 AUROC 0.8611，高于本地训练梯度提升模型 0.8227 和仅源数据 PRISM 0.7841。

Abstract: Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.

[124] A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Mayug Maniparambil, Ayush Rai, Ekansh Verma, Noel E. O’Connor, Fergal Reid

TL;DR: 研究发现仅靠特权答案条件下的打分教师做 on-policy 自蒸馏，既不能定位错误也不保证纠错；先生成 critique 再条件化打分可形成纠错信用，并用于推理重启和 cold-start GRPO。

Details

Motivation: OPD 试图用参考答案条件教师对学生 rollout 打分，替代稀疏 RL 结果奖励并提供 token 级信用。但关键在于这类分布差异信号是否真的指出推理错误并降低错误 token 概率。

Result: 作者用学生轨迹的金标准错误跨度测试自蒸馏目标中的全分布 KL 和 token 对数比，发现它们既不定位错误、也不将概率从错误 token 移开，并且不能可靠区分同一问题的正确/错误 rollout。作者认为这属于结构性问题：定位错误本身需要搜索，而打分教师并未被要求搜索。相同模型和特权信息若可生成 critique，则错误定位显著更好。据此提出 critique-conditioned self-distillation：先批评当前轨迹，再让打分教师以 critique 为条件。新信号能定位错误、在错误跨度内纠正，并提高触发回溯的 “Wait”“But”“Actually” 等 token 概率。Critique 还可在测试时用于从错误轨迹续跑，解决采样本身无法解决的数学题；也可用于 cold-start GRPO，在 verifiable reward 没有信号的提示上提供优势信号。

Insight: 教师获得正确答案并不自动意味着其分布分数包含可执行的定位/纠错信息；显式 critique 提供了缺失的搜索步骤。摘要提出的能力包括错误定位、回溯 token 信号和两个应用场景，但未给基准量化增益。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student’s own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as Wait'', But’’, and ``Actually’’. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

[125] UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Nazar Andrushko, Vera Schmitt, Veronika Solopova

TL;DR: UkrMeme 发布 200 张乌克兰战时自然 meme 的说服技巧标注数据集，并翻译部分 Propaganda 950 为 PTM-UA；以图像、文字和多模态零样本 VLM 及分层 F1 评估，比较 LRP 与模型 token 归因解释。

Details

Motivation: meme 会结合图像和文字传达说服性信息，在政治冲突语境中尤其重要。单独分析文本或视觉线索可能无法识别其说服技巧，且解释方法需要说明模型依据哪些语义证据作出判断。

Result: 作者构建 UkrMeme，包含 200 张自然产生的乌克兰战时 meme，并标注说服技巧；另发布 PTM-UA，即保留原图像内容、翻译 meme 文字的 Propaganda 950 子集。研究以零样本提示评估 VLM 的图像输入、文本输入和多模态输入，采用分层 F1 指标。还比较 Layer-wise Relevance Propagation 与模型生成的 token 归因，并分析两类解释方法强调的语义证据类别。摘要未提供具体模型排名或 F1 分数。

Insight: 保持原图并翻译文字的配对数据有助于比较视觉、语言和多模态输入对说服检测的影响；解释比较则关注显著证据类型。数据集仅含 200 张乌克兰战时 meme，结果不能直接代表更广泛语言、文化或政治内容。

Abstract: Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.

[126] Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CYPDF

Emilio Ferrara

TL;DR: PAGE 框架将 AI 用于大规模漫画研究：逐页解析分格/文字/书目信息、按人类 codebook 标注并用人工样本校准；在 3,929 本美国漫画和 87 卷日本漫画上开展三项研究。

Details

Motivation: 漫画是重要的视觉文化档案，但学术研究通常逐本进行，数十万页内容难以整体分析。作者主张生成式 AI 等方法可支持规模化研究、档案检索、无障碍访问和艺术家署名。

Result: PAGE 流程解析每张扫描页中的分格、文字和书目信息；用遵循书面编码手册的 VLM 对页面进行类似人工编码的注释；再以人工标注样本校准，并在控制出版社和类型后估计档案变化。框架应用于 1938–1956 年的 3,929 本美国漫画和 87 卷日本漫画，完成三项研究：结合文本搜索和视觉阅读分析大众媒介如何呈现政治权力；跨行业测量页面视觉形式并识别艺术家风格；在人工标注核验下追踪两种漫画传统中的说话者。作者还讨论将工具用于按图像内容检索、帮助视障读者和保护脆弱档案。

Insight: 该工作把页面解析、codebook 驱动的视觉标注、人工 grounding 与受控档案分析连成研究流程，强调 AI 作为漫画研究工具而非取代领域解释。摘要未给模型误差、人工标注规模或三项研究的量化结论。

Abstract: Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938–1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page’s visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

[127] Cancerous Backdoor Attack: Gradual Activation via Invisible Tokens and A Stage Controller cs.CR | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Tahmid Imtiaz Imu, Emama Nahid, Prodipta Sen Amartya, Ahsanul Jobayer, Yutong Song

TL;DR: 本文研究用不可见 token 和阶段控制器逐步激活大语言模型后门，使推理损害在多轮交互中累积而非一次触发；摘要报告跨模型和推理数据集均能产生渐进破坏，但未提供量化结果。

Details

Motivation: 典型 LLM 后门满足触发条件后执行单一终端行为，未覆盖恶意行为在到达最终触发阶段前逐步改变推理过程的情形。渐进污染可能在最终答案明显异常之前就影响中间推理。

Result: Cancerous Backdoor Attack 通过不可见 token 给模型提供用户可见请求中没有的隐藏阶段，并由阶段控制器在推理过程中跟踪攻击进度，实现跨多轮交互的渐进激活。作者在多个推理数据集上评估，报告该攻击可在不同模型和任务上持续造成渐进式推理破坏，并与既有后门攻击基线比较取得更好结果。摘要没有说明具体模型、数据集、成功率或防御效果。

Insight: 只检查最终答案准确率可能无法发现尚未完全显现的推理污染，因此过程级监测也有必要。本文摘要仅支持攻击机制与定性实验结论，未提供足够细节量化风险或说明防御方式。

Abstract: Backdoor Attacks on Large Language Models (LLMs) typically follow a trigger-response pattern, where satisfying a trigger condition activates a single terminal behavior. However, this single-point backdoor activation does not explore attacks in which malicious behavior develops gradually through multiple intermediate stages before reaching the terminal activation stage. We present Cancerous Backdoor Attack, that gradually corrupts an LLM’s reasoning over a sequence of interactions by leveraging invisible tokens and a stage controller. Specifically, invisible tokens provide hidden stages to the model without appearing in the user’s visible request and the stage controller tracks the attack’s progression during inference to realize gradual activation. We evaluate Cancerous Backdoor Attack across several reasoning datasets. The attack consistently produces gradual reasoning corruption across models and tasks. We compare Cancerous Backdoor Attack with prior backdoor attack baselines to demonstrate our proposed attack outperforms the baselines. Our results indicate that reasoning corruption can develop gradually before it becomes visible, making answer accuracy alone insufficient for detecting backdoor attacks.

[128] Beyond the Final Embedding: Retrieving Memories from Token-Level Evidence cs.IR | cs.CLPDF

Han Luo, Zhan Zhuang, Xu He, Jujia Zhao, Yu Li

TL;DR: 该检索方法结合 query token 对记忆 token 的稀疏匹配与整段向量相似度，无需训练即可改善八个基准的检索；在 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 上增加查询适配后，Recall@10 和 NDCG@10 平均分别多 13.3/14.3 个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 记忆增强助手通常用单一向量比较查询与会话/文档，但相关信息可能只在记忆的一小段内容中。嵌入模型已计算出的上下文 token 表征可用于更局部的证据匹配，无需重新训练编码器。

Result: 方法让每个查询 token 从候选记忆中检索少量匹配 token，再按记忆聚合局部匹配分数，并与整段记忆相似度结合排序。可选的低秩 query adapter 可用监督信号训练，同时固定编码器及已存记忆表征。无额外训练的 token 匹配方法在八个基准和多个模型家族上提高检索表现；在 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 上，查询适配进一步使平均 Recall@10 和 NDCG@10 分别增加 13.3 和 14.3 个百分点。检索改善也转化为长期记忆问答准确率提升。

Insight: 稀疏局部证据匹配补足了单向量压缩导致的细节丢失，而整段相似度仍保留全局语境；查询适配不需重新编码存储记忆。摘要没有列各基准分数或 QA 提升值，13.3/14.3 是指定模型上的平均检索指标增幅。

Abstract: Memory-augmented assistants retrieve evidence from stored conversations and documents to answer queries. Most embedding-based retrievers compare single-vector representations of queries and memories, even when relevance depends on a small part of the content. Such local evidence can be matched to queries using the contextual token representations already computed by the same embedding models. Our method uses sparse token matching to improve retrieval without additional training. Each query token retrieves a small set of matching tokens across candidate memories. The matching scores are aggregated for each memory and combined with whole-memory similarity to rank memories. An optional low-rank query adapter can be trained with supervision while keeping the encoder and stored memory representations fixed. Our method improves retrieval across eight benchmarks and multiple model families without additional training. Query adaptation further increases the gains to 13.3 and 14.3 percentage points in average Recall@10 and NDCG@10 on Qwen3-Embedding-4B. These retrieval gains translate into higher question-answering accuracy on long-term memory benchmarks.

[129] Kairos: Toward a Byte-Level Foundation Model for Every Modality cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Fabien Furfaro

TL;DR: Kairos 提出共享字节空间的多模态基础模型架构，但本文只做了小规模收敛健全性检查：证明组件能优化，不证明泛化、多模态能力或与现有模型相比更好。

Details

Motivation: 在单台本地机器训练小型语言模型时，架构试错预算有限。Kairos 将统一字节级表征与多种注意力、残差、MoE 和多模态 codec 组件结合；本文首先要回答的是实现是否能训练收敛，而非功能效果是否成立。

Result: 架构包含共享投影上的双向 gated-DeltaNet 线性注意力与滑窗注意力（LiZAttention2）、分块 Attention-Residual 聚合、稀疏 MoE 前馈层，以及面向文本、图像、音频、视频、激光雷达和控制流的多尺度字节 codec，并用共享字节空间上的均匀噪声去噪目标训练。作者逐组件给出算法，并进行收敛检查：以 1–15M 参数模型拟合一条 440 字节句子；codec 和注意力选项各用三个随机种子，另外七项因素为单次扫描。两种 3,000 步注意力设置的最低 loss 约为 10⁻³；600 步后不同 codec 的平均最低 loss 从 patch codec 的 0.018 到深度卷积 codec 的 0.354，三种子排序一致。共享注意力投影的早期优势随着训练步数或数据多样性增加而消失，冻结投影后甚至反转，说明是收敛速度差异而非容量优势。

Insight: 这些实验只证明当前实现能优化小样本拟合任务；作者明确指出它们不能说明泛化、多模态训练效果或相对已有模型的性能。200M 总参数/25M 激活参数目标及吞吐数字是分析性规划，非实测结果。

Abstract: Small language models trained on a single local machine leave little room for architectural trial and error. Kairos combines bidirectional gated-DeltaNet linear attention and sliding-window attention over shared projections (LiZAttention2), block-wise Attention-Residual aggregation, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward layer and a multi-scale byte-level codec, trained with a uniform-noise denoising objective over one byte space shared by text, image, audio, video, lidar and control streams; this report asks whether the combination optimizes at all before asking whether it is useful. We give code-derived algorithms for each component, including a cross-session recurrent-state memory gate, then run convergence sanity checks: overfittinga single 440-byte sentence at 1–15M parameters, text only, with three seeds for codec and attention choices and single-run sweeps for seven further factors. The two attention variants tested at 3,000 steps (LiZAttention2 and window attention alone) reach a minimum loss near $10^{-3}$. After 600 steps, the mean minimum loss ranges from 0.018 (patch codec) to 0.354 (depthwise convolutional codec) with an identical ranking on three seeds. An apparent advantage of shared over separate attention projections closes with more steps or more diverse data and reverses when the projections are frozen: it is a convergence-speed effect, not a capacity effect. These checks suggest that the implementation optimizes; they say nothing about generalization, multimodal training or comparison with existing models, which a planned pretraining run is meant to address. The Discussion sizes a 200M-total/25M-active target analytically; none of its throughput figures is measured. Code is available at https://github.com/fabienfrfr/Kairos . Python package at https://pypi.org/project/kairos-fm/ .

[130] REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Joshua Maccoby, Wendy Hui Wang

TL;DR: REDaCT 在推理时检测思维链中的遗忘集泄漏，并注入离线学习的纠正嵌入继续生成；作者报告避免推理崩溃，同时完全忘记答案、近乎完全忘记思维链内容。

Details

Motivation: 大推理模型的遗忘不仅需删除最终答案中的遗忘集信息，也要清除推理过程中的泄漏。现有方法可能导致推理崩溃：模型无法生成连贯思维链或不能正常结束推理。

Result: REDaCT（REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning）包含二元泄漏检测器，用于定位推理轨迹中泄露遗忘集信息的句子；另有离线学习的纠正嵌入，在推理时首次发现泄漏处注入，以引导后续轨迹避开相关内容。作者在多个数据集上评估，报告相较先进遗忘方法显著更好地保留推理连贯性、完全避免推理崩溃，同时保持答案遗忘率为完全遗忘、思维链遗忘率接近完全；摘要未提供具体数据集或分数。

Insight: 在首次泄漏处介入并重生成后缀，试图同时控制中间推理和最终答案，而不对整个推理模型做常规微调。遗忘效果和推理保留结论为摘要报告，具体度量及跨场景稳健性需看正文。

Abstract: Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: https://github.com/jmaccoby/redact.

[131] Recurrent Harness for Agentic Continual Learning cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yuxin Xiao, Zhiyun Lu, Jianyu Wang, Yangtian Zhang, Yang Song

TL;DR: ReHarness 在严格在线流式持续学习中加入对 harness 文本状态的反向修订，用每个实例的执行轨迹更新此前检索的反思；三项基准、三种主干上优于多类基线。

Details

Motivation: 持续运行的语言模型智能体需要适应分布变化，但现有 harness 常只沿前向信息流读写状态，或依赖额外回放/验证数据优化 harness，限制了纯在线适应。

Result: ReHarness 在前向读取和写入之外加入反向修订：每个实例完成后，用该实例的轨迹作为反馈，修改当时检索到的反思文本，追溯性地更新 harness 状态，以回答哪些经验本可被记录得更适合当前实例。三个持续学习基准、三种语言模型主干上，方法持续优于原始上下文、记忆/反思方法和提示优化基线。进一步分析显示，修订有来源依据且有意义，能纠正分布变化下过度概括或过期经验，并同时改善持续学习的稳定性与可塑性。

Insight: 文本记忆不是只能追加；根据后续实例反馈回改先前反思，可纠正旧经验并支持在线适应，而不需要额外回放或验证集。摘要未列基准名称和数值，结论限于三项持续学习基准及三种主干。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly operate over long interaction streams, requiring online adaptation to distribution shifts through continual learning. However, existing agent harnesses typically either evolve their states only through forward information flow or rely on additional replay or validation data for harness optimization. To address this, we propose ReHarness, a recurrent harness framework for agentic continual learning that complements forward read and write with backward revision under a strictly online streaming protocol. We ask what information could have been captured differently in the harness state to better inform the current instance in the stream. Specifically, after each instance, we leverage the resulting trajectory as feedback to revise the reflections retrieved for that instance, thereby propagating feedback backward through the textual harness state. Across three continual learning benchmarks and three language model backbones, ReHarness consistently outperforms strong raw-context baselines, memory- and reflection-based methods, and prompt optimization approaches. Further analysis shows that ReHarness produces source-grounded and meaningful revisions, corrects overgeneralized or outdated experience under distribution shifts, and improves both stability and plasticity during continual learning.

[132] S$^3$R: Fast and Faithful RL Rollouts via Self-Sparse Speculative Decoding cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Yuhang He, Hongyu Ge, Zihan Lin, Siyi Liu, Yongqi Zhang

TL;DR: S³R 用同一策略的稀疏注意力提出 token，再通过全注意力精确 speculative 验证，使 RLVR rollout 组仍服从稠密策略分布，并用 KV 感知调度降低缓存反复换入换出。

Details

Motivation: 长上下文 RLVR rollout 的解码常受 KV 缓存流量限制，稀疏注意力能减少读取，但直接从稀疏策略采样会改变用于组相对优化的响应组分布。即使逐响应精确重加权，因每个响应的优势依赖同组其他回答奖励，通常也无法恢复稠密策略的期望更新。

Result: Self-Sparse Speculative Rollouts（S³R）由稀疏注意力提出 token，再用全注意力验证和精确 speculative sampling 生成响应；草拟和验证共享同一策略参数，不需训练独立草拟器，最终响应组服从稠密策略分布。作者分析了其加速条件以及草拟、验证成本，并指出稠密验证仍需完整历史 KV。KV 感知调度器在保持 rollout 并行度的同时减少 KV 缓存重复卸载/恢复。激进稀疏草拟消融中，S³R 训练保持稳定，而直接稀疏采样加训练侧修正不稳定。长上下文数学推理中，摘要报告相对 Qwen3 自回归 rollout 的端到端训练吞吐为“1.39×~1.35×”，该写法顺序和对应设置不明；任务表现相当。

Insight: 该方法通过全注意力精确验证保留稠密策略的采样分布，同时用稀疏草拟减少平均解码成本；因此没有直接将优化策略换成稀疏分布。吞吐倍率在摘要中存在格式/设置歧义，未自行归一为明确区间。

Abstract: Fast rollout generation for long-context reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) requires reducing KV-cache traffic without changing the response groups used for policy optimization. In our profiled RLVR workloads, attention accounts for 72–76% of autoregressive decoding kernel time. Sparse attention reduces these reads, but sampling directly from a sparse policy changes the response groups used for learning. For group-relative optimization, we show that even exact response-wise reweighting does not generally recover the expected dense-policy update because the advantage of each response depends on the rewards of other group members. We propose Self-Sparse Speculative Rollouts (S$^3$R). It uses sparse attention to propose tokens, then generates responses through full-attention verification and exact speculative sampling. Drafting and verification share the same policy parameter, eliminating the need to train or update a separate drafter. The resulting response groups follow the dense-policy distribution. Under S$^3$R, we characterize speedup conditions and the costs of drafting and verification. We found that dense verification still requires the complete historical KV cache. A KV-aware scheduler therefore limits KV-cache repeated offloading and restoration while maintaining rollout parallelism. % OR thrashing In a matched ablation with an aggressive sparse drafting budget, S$^3$R maintains stable training while direct sparse sampling with training-side correction becomes unstable. On long-context mathematical reasoning, S$^3$R achieves $1.39\times~1.35\times$ the end-to-end training throughput of autoregressive rollouts on Qwen3 model, with comparable task performance.

[133] CAST: Credit Alignment for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Jingru Yang, Jinhang Wu, Minjing Shi, Shengfeng He, Xuyan Li

TL;DR: CAST 为跨 tokenizer on-policy 蒸馏增加 chunk 级信用分配和无对齐位置的选择性锚定；六项数学、科学与代码基准上，对 Qwen3→Mistral/Llama 转移平均提升 2.45/1.32 分。

Details

Motivation: 跨 tokenizer OPD 先需把教师与学生序列对齐。解码文本对齐可给出 chunk 对应关系，却无法确定 chunk 优势应如何分配到各学生 token，且没有教师对应的 token 不受约束。

Result: CAST 将 chunk 构造与信用分配分开。对已对齐 chunk，依据预测不确定性和局部教师信号平滑性，把 chunk 优势重新分配给学生 token，强调局部一致教师反馈支持的学习机会，同时保持 chunk 总优势不变；其权重精确求解一个 KL 正则信用分配问题。对未对齐位置，使用 KL 惩罚选择性锚定学生初始策略，限制缺乏教师监督处的漂移。由于两个环节解耦，同 tokenizer 时也可用于标点划分或固定长度 chunk。在六个数学、科学和代码基准上，跨 tokenizer 从 Qwen3-8B 转到 Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-8B 时，相对最强评测基线平均提升 2.45、1.32 分；同 tokenizer 转到 Qwen3-1.7B 时提升 2.10 分。

Insight: chunk 级信号需同时解决内部 token 信用分配与无对应位置的策略漂移。CAST 分别用不确定性加平滑性分摊优势、用 KL 锚定未对齐 token；结果数字是跨六项基准的平均分提升。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer on-policy distillation (OPD) requires aligning teacher and student sequences before their scores can be compared. Decoded-text alignment provides such correspondence at the chunk level, but leaves a distinct problem unresolved: credit alignment. A chunk-level signal specifies how much a span should change, but not how that signal should be distributed across student tokens, while tokens without teacher correspondence remain unconstrained. We propose CAST (Chunk-aware credit Assignment with Selective Token Anchoring), a shift-or-stay framework that addresses both issues. For aligned chunks, CAST redistributes each chunk’s advantage across student tokens using predictive uncertainty and local teacher-signal smoothness, emphasizing learning opportunities supported by locally consistent teacher feedback while preserving the chunk’s total advantage. The resulting weights exactly solve a KL-regularized credit-allocation problem. For unaligned positions, CAST selectively anchors the student to its initial policy through a KL penalty, limiting drift where teacher supervision is unavailable. Because credit assignment is separated from chunk construction, CAST also applies to punctuation-defined or fixed-length chunks under shared tokenization. Across six mathematics, science, and code benchmarks, CAST improves the average over the strongest evaluated baseline by $2.45$ and $1.32$ points for cross-tokenizer transfer from Qwen3-8B to Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively, and by $2.10$ points for shared-tokenizer transfer to Qwen3-1.7B.

[134] Kernelized Parameter Sensitivity Atlases for Amortized Conditional Interpretability cs.LG | cs.CV | stat.MLPDF

Davide Wiest, Ahanaf Hasan Ariq, Abdul Monaf Chowdhury

TL;DR: KPSA 将经验神经切线核分块分解为参数敏感性图谱，用轻量核在新输入上估计输入条件化敏感度；相较反向传播 oracle 大幅降低计算与显存。

Details

Motivation: 梯度归因可揭示特定输入依赖哪些模型参数，但对大量样本逐一反向传播成本高；静态重要性分数更快，却丢失输入依赖性。

Result: Kernelized Parameter Sensitivity Atlas（KPSA）从经验神经切线核的块分解得到归一化参数组敏感度，将来源嵌入显式查询空间，并用轻量核估计未见输入的敏感度。理论部分给出区分有限来源误差与不可约表示误差的界，以及 margin 条件下 top-k 恢复保证。参数预算为 0.02%–0.1% 时，在视觉、预测和蛋白质建模设置中，选中参数组达到一阶反向传播 oracle 扰动敏感度的 39%–85%；视觉和预测中的稀疏敏感性回路跨层延伸并随输入改变。在 Transformer MLP 特征激活归因中，视觉任务保留 oracle 与恒定敏感度基线差距的 81%，批量查询吞吐由 551 提至 2,041 张/秒，峰值显存由 4.55 降至 0.44 GB；蛋白质任务保留该差距的 68%。

Insight: 核图谱可将输入依赖的参数敏感度以较低成本摊销到新样本上；消融还显示，在 KPSA 空间做近邻检索仍有竞争力。不同应用报告口径包括扰动敏感性占比和 oracle—基线差距保留率，应与吞吐/显存数据区分。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution provides input-specific information about model parameters, but computing it via backpropagation for many samples is expensive, while static importance scores discard input dependence. We derive KPSA (Kernelized Parameter Sensitivity Atlas) from a block decomposition of the empirical neural tangent kernel. It measures normalized parameter-group sensitivity, embeds those sources in an explicit query space, and uses a lightweight kernel to estimate sensitivities for unseen inputs. We derive bounds that separate finite-source from irreducible representation error and give margin-based top-$k$ recovery. This paper studies two interpretability applications. Firstly, KPSA identifies parameter groups with high susceptibility to perturbations across vision, forecasting, and protein-modeling setups. Across a fivefold range of parameter budgets ($0.02$-$0.1%$), its selected groups attain $39$-$85%$ of the perturbation susceptibility of a first-order backpropagation oracle. In vision and forecasting, these parameter groups form sparse sensitivity circuits that extend across depth and vary systematically with the input. Secondly, we use KPSA on an activation-attribution problem for MLP features in transformers. It retains $81%$ of the gap between a backpropagation oracle and a constant-sensitivity baseline on vision while increasing batched query-time throughput from 551 to 2,041 images/s and reducing peak GPU memory from 4.55 to 0.44GB. On the protein-modeling task, it recovers $68%$ of this gap. An ablation of KPSA’s kernel aggregation shows that nearest-neighbor retrieval in KPSA space can remain competitive. The empirics show that compact kernel atlases can store useful input-dependent parameter sensitivity while amortizing repeated gradient attribution, a step toward scalable conditional interpretability. Code can be found at https://github.com/Axym-Labs/kpsa .

Yinlong Dai, Homanga Bharadhwaj, Dylan P. Losey

Abstract: Prior research on learning from humans often requires real human demonstrations. But advances in video generation models offer a new paradigm: we can now prompt these generators to make synthetic videos of the task. On the plus side, these synthetic videos take place in the robot’s current environment, and do not require actual human teaching. However, while the generated videos appear to show plausible motions, they lack both physical consistency and robot grounding, and thus the robot cannot directly roll out the trajectory shown by the video. In this paper we seek to formalize the process of going from synthetic video generation to real-world robot trajectories for zero-shot manipulation tasks. We recognize that this problem is an instance of constrained optimization. The object trajectories extracted from the synthetic video provide an initial guess at the correct motion, and the robot must refine these trajectories (for instance, within a digital twin) while enforcing physical feasibility and semantic consistency. Our optimization-based formalism provides insights into the video generation properties necessary for success. Specifically, we show that the video generation must probabilistically seed the search within a semantic basin of attraction where the trajectory can be locally modified without losing its task alignment. Our analysis further reveals that state-of-the-art approaches can be viewed as instances of our unified formalism with key simplifications. In our experiments we remove these simplifications to more closely match the underlying optimization, leading to more robust zero-shot trajectories from synthetic videos and improving average task success by 34.1% compared to the best-performing baseline. Abstract: Embodied coding agents construct robot policies from natural-language instructions by generating executable programs and refining them from execution feedback. This refinement assumes that execution feedback reveals whether the task succeeded and, after a failure, provides sufficient evidence to guide program revision. Aerial manipulation violates this assumption in two ways. Payload constraints restrict onboard camera coverage and leave task-critical outcomes outside the robot’s view (partial observability), while strong dynamic coupling and limited control margins make physical interactions fragile (limited interaction robustness) and leave failure causes unresolved. Re-examining the same feedback cannot recover observations that were never collected or separate failure causes that the available evidence does not distinguish. To acquire this missing evidence, we introduce Active Feedback (AF), an active “View and Probe” framework. When execution feedback leaves unresolved questions, the agent synthesizes a separate diagnostic program that runs in simulation to inspect unseen outcomes from informative viewpoints (View) or test hypotheses about failure causes through targeted physical interactions (Probe). Across six simulated aerial manipulation tasks with 60 trials per condition, AF reduces false completion declarations from 25 to 5 and raises final task success from 31.7% to 75% compared with terminal feedback under the same coding-agent configuration and task-execution limit. Using shared robot APIs, task programs refined with AF in simulation run on a physical aerial manipulator without task-program source-code modification and complete multi-stage manipulation tasks in the real world. These results show that executable diagnosis fills gaps in execution feedback for embodied coding agents, improving both completion assessment and program refinement in aerial manipulation.

[139] RARA: Robust Policy Fine-Tuning through Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment for Robotic Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Xuhui Kang, Sung-Wook Lee, Yan Zhuang, Tianle Zhong, Yen-Ling Kuo

TL;DR: RARA 用预训练演示检索锚定表示，再在微调时约束检索样本表征漂移；Diffusion Policy 与 GR00T 在 13 项任务上的平均成功率提升 9.5 个百分点（域内）和 13.6 个百分点（分布外）。

Details

Motivation: 预训练 VLA 策略可作为通用操作基础，但少量演示下适配新任务困难。直接微调虽能提升域内性能，却可能扭曲预训练表示并损害分布外泛化。

Result: RARA 分两阶段适配：首先从预训练数据中检索任务意图和动作相似的演示，将目标演示的策略表示与检索演示的预训练表示对齐；然后用目标演示微调策略，同时限制检索演示表示在微调中的变化。这样先降低表示错配，再在动作学习阶段保留预训练知识。在 RoboTwin、RoboCasa 的 13 项任务及真实机器人实验中，Diffusion Policy 和 GR00T 的微调平均成功率相较对照域内提升 9.5 个百分点、分布外提升 13.6 个百分点，并超过所比较基线。

Insight: 检索到的预训练相似演示既用于初始化对齐，也作为微调期间的稳定锚点，从而缓解少样本适配造成的表示漂移。报告提升按域内/分布外汇总，摘要未给各策略、任务或真实机器人单独数值。

Abstract: Pre-trained robot policies, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, provide a strong foundation for generalist robotic manipulation, but adapting them to new tasks with only a few demonstrations remains challenging. Directly fine-tuning a pre-trained policy often achieves strong in-distribution performance, yet it can distort the representations learned during pre-training and weaken the model’s generalization ability under out-of-distribution shifts. We propose Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment (RARA), a two-stage adaptation method, to address this issue. First, RARA retrieves representation features from pretraining with similar task intent and motion, and aligns the policy’s representations of the target demonstrations with the pre-trained representations of the retrieved demonstrations. Second, RARA fine-tunes the policy on the target demonstrations while limiting how much its representations of the retrieved demonstrations change during fine-tuning. By reducing the representation mismatch before action learning and preserving pre-trained knowledge during adaptation, RARA limits unnecessary representation drift during fine-tuning. Experiments with Diffusion Policy and GR00T on 13 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboCasa, and on a real robot show that RARA improves the average success of fine-tuning by 9.5 points in-distribution and 13.6 points out-of-distribution, and outperforms all compared baselines. Project page: https://shua-kang.github.io/RARA_robot/

[140] Beyond Successful Robot Demonstrations: Learning from Human Interactions and Modeling Failure Dynamics cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Peiyan Tu, Cong Wang, Hanxin Zhu, Xin Jin, Zhibo Chen

TL;DR: HF-WM 用人类第一人称视频学习动作条件世界模型，并用受扰成功动作做 failure-aware GRPO 后训练，以捕捉失败动力学；作者报告视频生成达到 SOTA 且有助下游策略评估。

Details

Motivation: 机器人世界模型需要覆盖多样交互动力学，但机器人数据规模有限。人类第一人称视频补充丰富交互经验，却因手部与机械臂形态差距难直接迁移；成功演示占主导也使失败动力学代表不足。

Result: HF-WM 结合人类交互学习与失败感知后训练。为缩小 embodiment gap，双分支架构联合视频生成与辅助 pointflow 预测，以稳定点流作为人类和机器人交互的共同运动表示，同时抑制第一人称相机运动。为补足失败建模，作者对成功演示派生的受扰动作进行 GRPO 后训练，并用动作对齐和物理合理性奖励引导模型，无需配对失败真值视频即可学习可信失败动力学。实验报告 HF-WM 在动作条件视频生成上达到 SOTA，并在下游机器人策略评估中有较强效用；摘要未提供具体数据集或指标。

Insight: pointflow 表示将视觉运动与具身动作对齐，成功动作扰动则为无失败标签的数据补充失败轨迹信号。结果包含生成质量和策略评估两个方面，但摘要没有量化指标，不能判断各模块单独收益。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models offer a promising foundation for generalist robotic intelligence by predicting the consequences of actions. However, existing robotic datasets provide limited coverage of the diverse interaction dynamics required for generalizable world modeling. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer complementary interaction experience, yet the embodiment gap between human hands and robotic manipulators hinders effective cross-embodiment learning. Meanwhile, the predominance of successful demonstrations leaves failure dynamics substantially underrepresented, limiting the ability of world models to anticipate unsuccessful executions. To address these limitations, we introduce HF-WM, an action-conditioned world model that combines Human interaction learning with Failure-aware post-training. To bridge the embodiment gap, we develop a dual-branch framework that couples video generation with auxiliary pointflow prediction, using stabilized pointflow as a unified motion representation across human and robot interactions while suppressing egocentric camera motion. To improve failure modeling, we further perform Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training on perturbed actions derived from successful demonstrations, guided by action alignment and physical plausibility rewards, enabling the model to learn plausible failure dynamics without paired ground-truth videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HF-WM achieves state-of-the-art performance in action-conditioned video generation and exhibits strong utility for policy evaluation in downstream robotic tasks.

[141] Dream to Remember: Continual Steering of Generalist Robot Policies with World Models cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Bahey Tharwat, Iman Nematollahi, Marvin Alles, Xingyuan Zhang, Akshay L Chandra

TL;DR: D2R 用冻结世界模型想象回放旧任务，以持续训练一个小型 steering 模块而不改通用机器人策略；CALVIN 连续八任务平均成功率从 63.7% 升至 74.8%，LoRA 微调则降至 11.7%。

Details

Motivation: 通用机器人策略通过模仿学习获得，受示范质量和覆盖限制。强化学习可通过微调权重或训练小模块引导冻结生成策略拓展技能，但任务顺序到来时两者都可能灾难性遗忘：微调会损伤旧任务，只训练共享 steering 模块也会转移遗忘。

Result: Dream to Remember（D2R）持续训练 steering 模块，让冻结通用策略学习新任务，同时保留旧技能。世界模型冻结并对先前任务进行想象 rollout，作为无 replay buffer、无新数据的旧行为锚点。若世界模型足够准确，新任务可完全在想象中学习；若不够准确，则在真实环境学习新任务，只用世界模型复习旧任务。CALVIN 上连续学习八项任务全程在想象中完成，平均成功率由 63.7% 升至 74.8%，同序列 LoRA 微调降至 11.7%。用冻结现成 π₀ 和 A2World 学习未见 LIBERO-90 任务时，即便世界模型不足以用于策略优化，D2R 仍保留 π₀ 在原先微调 LIBERO 套件上的表现。真实 Franka 机器人上也完全通过想象连续改善三项任务并保留早先提升。

Insight: 世界模型可用于旧任务 rehearsal，而不必承担新任务优化；当其预测不够准确时仍可将其局限于旧技能保持。结果覆盖 CALVIN、LIBERO-90 与真实 Franka，模型保真度决定新任务能否完全在想象中训练。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained with imitation learning and are bounded by the quality and coverage of their demonstrations. Reinforcement learning can improve and extend them, either by fine-tuning the policy weights or by training a small module to steer the input noise of a frozen generative policy. When tasks arrive sequentially, both approaches suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Fine-tuning on a task degrades prior tasks, and steering only shifts forgetting to the shared steering module. We propose Dream to Remember (D2R), which continually steers a frozen generalist policy toward new tasks, while retaining its earlier skills. As the steering module learns a new task, a frozen pretrained world model rehearses prior tasks through imagined rollouts, anchoring the module to its earlier behavior without a replay buffer or new data. When the world model is faithful enough, D2R learns the new task entirely in imagination. Otherwise, it learns the task in the environment while using the world model only to rehearse prior tasks. On CALVIN, D2R improves eight tasks in sequence entirely in imagination, raising average success from 63.7% to 74.8%, while LoRA fine-tuning on the same sequence drops it to 11.7%. With a frozen off-the-shelf generalist policy and world model, $π_0$ and A2World, D2R learns unseen LIBERO-90 tasks in the environment while keeping $π_0$’s performance on the LIBERO suites it was fine-tuned on, even though the world model is not accurate enough for policy optimization. Finally, on a real Franka robot, D2R improves three tasks in sequence entirely in imagination while retaining the earlier improvements.

[142] Splat2Act: From Captured Scenes to Personalized VLA Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Xiaomeng Ye, Krishna Jaganathan, Gyubeum Lim, Gunjan Chhablani, Muhammad Zubair Irshad

TL;DR: Splat2Act 用手机场景扫描和物体照片自动构建建筑级 3DGS 仿真环境，生成仿真 oracle 演示并两阶段个性化 π0.5；目标场景成功率 62.5%，Stretch 3 实机子集成功率 63.9%。

Details

Motivation: 用户希望通用操作机器人能处理自己家中或办公室的物品，但即使强通用策略 π0.5 在作者采集的部署场景中未适配时拿取成功率为 0%。为每个环境收集真实演示成本高，现有 real-to-sim 方法也多聚焦单张桌面而非整栋室内环境。

Result: Splat2Act 构建自动化 real-to-sim-to-real 流程，仅需人类用手机扫描场景并拍摄物品。系统用 3D Gaussian Splatting 和碰撞几何构建逼真数字孪生，自动发现桌面及非桌面置物点，构造可行拿取任务，并由特权仿真 oracle 生成成功演示。对 101 个扫描校园环境（可导航面积中位数 104.87 m²、每个 7 个置物点），生成 852 个置物点和超过 68,000 条演示。π0.5 两阶段微调：先适配公共物品资产，再适配用户自有物体和目标场景置物点。最佳个性化策略在目标场景聚合成功率 62.5%；部署到 Stretch 3 后，12 项任务子集实机成功率 63.9%，接近同任务仿真的 66.7%。

Insight: 建筑级自动建模和特权仿真演示让个性化从少量用户采集转为大规模模拟数据训练。实机结果来自 12 项任务子集，场景成功率与仿真 66.7% 的匹配只针对这些任务；未适配策略的 0% 是作者采集部署场景结果。

Abstract: A user who brings a generalist manipulation policy into their home or office wants it to pick up their objects from their shelves, counters, and tables. Yet even a strong generalist like $π_{0.5}$ achieves 0% pickup success in our captured deployment scenes without adaptation, and collecting real demonstrations in every user’s environment is prohibitively expensive. We present Splat2Act, an automated real-to-sim-to-real pipeline that personalizes a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a specific scene and object set using only a handheld phone scan and images of the user’s objects. Unlike prior real-to-sim work that reconstructs individual tabletops, Splat2Act operates on building-scale indoor environments: it builds a photorealistic digital twin with 3D Gaussian Splatting and collision geometry, automatically discovers tabletop and non-tabletop receptacles, constructs feasible pickup tasks, and generates successful demonstrations with a privileged simulation oracle, with only scene and object capture required from a human. Applied to 101 scanned campus environments with a median navigable area of 104.87 m$^2$ and 7 receptacles, the pipeline yields 852 receptacles and over 68,000 pickup demonstrations. We fine-tune $π_{0.5}$ in two stages: Stage 1 adapts to hundreds of public object assets, drawn either from the deployment scene alone or from all 101 scenes; Stage 2 personalizes to the user’s own objects on the deployment scene’s receptacles. Our strongest personalized policy reaches 62.5% aggregate pickup success in the target scene. Deployed on a real Stretch 3, the same policy achieves 63.9% success on a 12-task subset, closely tracking its 66.7% simulated success on those tasks. These results suggest that automated real-to-sim-to-real training can personalize VLA manipulation policies using only simulation-oracle data.

[143] AV-FlowEdit: Training-Free Prompt-Driven Audio-Visual Editing with Event-Aware Spectral Trajectory Preservation cs.SD | cs.CV | eess.ASPDF

Shaohua Pan, Xiao Liu

TL;DR: AV-FlowEdit 利用源/目标流差异同时驱动音视频编辑方向与保留线索，并在采样时保护不支持修改的区域；免训练、免反演地支持视频单独、音频单独和联合编辑。

Details

Motivation: 联合音视频扩散模型能生成同步内容，但编辑已有片段时既要实现目标修改，又要保持非目标结构及跨模态对应。采样过程中的漂移可能破坏原有事件和音视频一致性。

Result: AV-FlowEdit 是无需训练、无需优化式反演的提示驱动音视频编辑框架。核心观察是源与目标的流差异既提供跨模态编辑方向，也提供内容保留线索；在共享扰动下同时更新视频和音频分支，使方法支持仅视频、仅音频和联合编辑，并保留预训练模型的跨模态交互。Cross-Modal Event-Aware Spectral Trajectory Preservation 结合流显著性、对齐音频活动、多尺度低频位移和扩散阶段调度，抑制采样累积漂移：保留缺少编辑支持的区域，同时允许预期音画事件变化。实验报告提示对齐和时间一致性较强，音频质量及跨模态连贯性有竞争力；摘要未列出量化分数。

Insight: 音频和视频共同提供编辑目标与保留约束，轨迹保留模块再按事件相关性区分应改动和应冻结内容。方法不需要额外训练、外部掩码或反演，但摘要未给具体主干、数据集或编辑成功率。

Abstract: Joint audio-video diffusion models generate synchronized content, but editing existing clips requires target changes without disrupting non-target structure or cross-modal correspondence. We present AV-FlowEdit, a training-free and inversion-free framework for prompt-driven audio-video editing. Our key insight is that source–target flow differences provide both cross-modal editing directions and preservation cues. Under shared perturbations, they jointly update the video and audio branches, supporting visual-only, audio-only, and joint edits while retaining pretrained cross-modal interactions. To suppress drift accumulated during sampling, Cross-Modal Event-Aware Spectral Trajectory Preservation combines flow saliency, aligned audio activity, multiscale low-frequency displacement, and diffusion-stage scheduling. This adaptively preserves unsupported regions while allowing intended audio-visual events. AV-FlowEdit requires no model training, external masks, or optimization-based inversion. Experiments show strong prompt alignment and temporal consistency, together with competitive audio quality and cross-modal coherence.

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to build programs from scratch as software engineers do rather than to resolve issues within a repository or to generate a repository from a given requirements document. However, scalable verifiable training environments that force agents to discover what is required and to decide how the system should be designed remain scarce. Moreover, existing agent workflows also cannot sustain long-horizon development in which the agent explores the reference program before implementation. To address these challenges, we introduce ProgramGym, a framework consisting of Program-Env and Program-Agent for scalable training environments of long-horizon program reproduction agents. Specifically, Program-Env constructs verifiable training environments from repositories through a feedback-driven loop that repairs runtime sandbox builds and the quality of black-box tests. Using this novel pipeline, we obtain 1,648 training environments spanning C, C++, Go, and Rust. Program-Agent combines a role-isolated workflow of analysis, implementation, and review with revision management for compilation checks and failure recovery. Experiments on ProgramBench show that both Program-Env and Program-Agent improve performance on program reproduction. Fine-tuning on trajectories with these constructed environments raises the performance of Qwen3.8-27B from 34.87% to 42.84%. Program-Agent further raises the base and SFT models to 55.71% and 56.25%, both exceeding some flagship models. [145] Can Multimodal Decision Models Classify Breast Ultrasound Images? Benchmarking CLEF and CLEF-Flash Across Prompts and Datasets eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Tran Cao Minh, Hai Tran, Ha Minh Tan

TL;DR: 对 2,818 张乳腺超声图的基准显示 CLEF/CLEF-Flash 表现依数据集和提示而变；临床提示下 CLEF-Flash 三个数据集准确率最高，但 BUS-BRA 所有配置都低于多数类基线。

Details

Motivation: 多模态决策模型可直接输出预定义选项概率，为图像分类提供结构化接口。该研究检验 CLEF 与 CLEF-Flash 在乳腺超声数据集上、不改变参数和不做用户预处理时，能否可靠完成良恶性等分类。

Result: 基准覆盖 Dataset B、BUSI、BUS-BRA 共 2,818 张图；前两类数据集做良性/恶性二分类，BUSI 另含正常类。测试三种提示：最简标签、明确分类任务、超声临床标准，报告准确率、macro-F1、平衡准确率、macro precision、吞吐和延迟。临床提示下 CLEF-Flash 在三个数据集准确率最高：Dataset B 74.85%、BUSI 66.28%、BUS-BRA 51.63%；但 BUSI 上 CLEF 的任务提示取得最高 macro-F1 64.51% 和平衡准确率 68.72%。所有配对比较中 CLEF-Flash 延迟更低。临床指导提高 CLEF-Flash 相对简短提示的准确率，却降低 CLEF 在所有数据集上的准确率。BUS-BRA 中良性误判为恶性的情况频繁，所有配置准确率都低于 67.63% 多数类预测基线。

Insight: 表现高度依赖数据集、提示和指标，单报准确率可能掩盖类别失衡；BUS-BRA 结果尤其说明模型配置甚至不如多数类策略。研究是不改参数的基准评估，不应据此推断临床诊断安全性或实际医疗效用。

Abstract: Multimodal decision models return probabilities over predefined alternatives, offering a structured interface for image classification. This study benchmarks inference with CLEF and CLEF-Flash on 2818 breast ultrasound images from Dataset B, BUSI, and BUS-BRA. Dataset B and BUS-BRA use binary benign-malignant classification, whereas BUSI includes normal, benign, and malignant categories. Model parameters remain unchanged, and original images are submitted without user-defined preprocessing. Three prompts provide minimal labels, an explicit classification task, or sonographic criteria. We report accuracy, macro F1, balanced accuracy, macro precision, throughput, and latency. CLEF-Flash with the clinical prompt achieves the highest accuracy on all three datasets: 74.85% on Dataset B, 66.28% on BUSI, and 51.63% on BUS-BRA. On BUSI, however, CLEF with the task-guided prompt achieves the highest macro F1 (64.51%) and balanced accuracy (68.72%). CLEF-Flash has lower latency in every matched comparison. Clinical instructions improve CLEF-Flash accuracy relative to the minimal prompt but reduce CLEF accuracy on all datasets. Confusion matrices reveal frequent benign-to-malignant errors, particularly on BUS-BRA, where all configurations fall below the 67.63% accuracy of a majority-class predictor. These findings demonstrate dataset-and prompt-dependent performance and motivate class-specific evaluation before applying multimodal decision models to breast ultrasound analysis.

[146] FlyHack: Stress-Testing Mechanistic Claims in a Connectome-Constrained Visual Model q-bio.NC | cs.CVPDF

Aashish Dhawan

TL;DR: 对 flyvis 果蝇运动视觉模型的压力测试表明，机制结论会依赖模型、干预、回路表示和训练解；连通组约束模型适合提出可检验假设，但不应将其机制结论泛化到真实果蝇。

Details

Motivation: 连通组约束网络允许直接干预生物结构，但据此得出的机制结论仍可能依赖比较模型、干预方法、回路表示及训练解。本文系统检验这些选择对果蝇运动视觉模型机制主张的影响。

Result: 研究在含 50 个模型的 flyvis 集合上比较任务匹配因果 CNN 控制，并重复干预。行为层面，三层 reverse-phi 反转强依赖周期和速度，任务匹配 CNN 也出现该现象；两类模型均未复现果蝇静止锯齿响应。对抗方向反转所需扰动在 flyvis 为刺激对比度的 0.21–0.27 倍、CNN 为 0.13–0.25 倍，但扩大 CNN 后与参数匹配控制的差距大幅缩小。机制归因需匹配干预：对 held-out mean 替换，一级边归因 patching 的 pooled AUROC/Spearman 为 0.970/0.872；若与同一真值比较零对齐分数则为 0.933/0.126。贪心细胞类型集仅在 50 个模型中的 25 个满足模型内 T4/T5 调谐标准；在 40 个模型中，T4/T5 输出静默使 8 个 optic-flow 片段中最多仅 2 个保留行为。进一步响应幅度检查后，六个首批成功模型中仅两个细胞类型集仍过阈值。

Insight: 模型可直接检验回路机制假设，但结论必须限定模型族、干预、表示和训练解；同一行为或机制指标可随这些因素变化。文章的重点是界定机制归因可靠范围，而不是证明所有模型现象都对应生物回路机制。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained neural networks make biological components directly accessible to intervention, but their mechanistic conclusions can still depend on the comparison model, intervention, circuit representation and trained solution. We stress-test these dependencies in flyvis, a \emph{Drosophila} motion-vision model, using its 50-model ensemble, task-matched causal CNN controls and replicated interventions. At the level of model behaviour, three-level reverse-phi reversal in flyvis is strongly period- and velocity-dependent and also appears in task-matched CNNs; neither family reproduces the fly’s stationary-sawtooth response. Adversarial direction reversal requires a perturbation bound of 0.21–0.27 times stimulus contrast in flyvis versus 0.13–0.25 in CNNs, but the gap over a parameter-matched CNN largely closes for larger CNNs. At the level of mechanism, matching attribution to the intervention matters: for held-out-mean replacement, first-order edge attribution patching reaches pooled AUROC 0.970 and Spearman 0.872, versus 0.933 and 0.126 when its zero-aligned score is evaluated against the same ground truth. Circuit conclusions also vary with representation and ensemble member. Greedy cell-type sets meet a model-relative T4/T5 tuning criterion in 25 of 50 models, while T4/T5 output silencing reduces the tested optic-flow behaviour to at most two of eight clips in 40. In six models where the cell-type search first succeeds, the corresponding connection sets can be pruned to 13–31 added classes; under an additional two-sided response-amplitude check, only two of those six first-success cell-type sets still meet the tuning threshold. These results support a narrower use of connectome-constrained models: they make mechanistic hypotheses directly testable, but the resulting claims must be scoped to the model family, intervention, representation and trained solution.
